*Yi Sui,Chaozhuo Li,Dawei Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve strong performance on complex tasks by leveraging long Chain-of-Thought (CoT), but often suffer from overthinking, leading to excessive reasoning steps and high inference latency. Existing CoT compression methods struggle to balance accuracy and efficiency, and lack fine-grained, step-level adaptation to redundancy and reasoning bias. Therefore, we propose State-Aware Reasoning Compression with Knowledge Guidance (STACK), a framework that performs step-wise CoT compression by explicitly modeling stage-specific redundancy sources and integrating with a retrieval-augmented guidance. STACK constructs online long-short contrastive samples and dynamically switches between knowledge-guided compression for uncertain or biased reasoning state and self-prompted compression for overly long but confident state, complemented by an answer-convergence-based early stopping mechanism to suppress redundant verification. We further propose a reward-difference-driven training strategy by combining Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), enabling models to learn state-conditioned compression strategies. Experiments on three mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that STACK achieves a superior accuracy-efficiency balance, reducing average response length by 59.9% while improving accuracy by 4.8 points over existing methods.

</details>


### [43] [Facet-Level Tracing of Evidence Uncertainty and Hallucination in RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09174)
*Passant Elchafei,Monorama Swain,Shahed Masoudian,Markus Schedl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种细粒度（facet-level）的RAG评估框架，将问题分解为原子推理面，通过Facet x Chunk矩阵评估证据充分性和忠实度，对比三种推理模式（严格RAG、软RAG、纯LLM）诊断检索-生成不对齐问题。研究发现RAG系统中的幻觉主要由证据整合不当而非检索精度导致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG评估方法仅关注答案级或段落级准确率，无法揭示生成过程中证据的实际使用情况。即使检索到相关文档，幻觉仍然普遍存在，表明问题核心在于证据整合而非检索本身。

Method: 1) 将输入问题分解为原子推理面；2) 构建Facet x Chunk矩阵，结合检索相关性评分与基于NLI的忠实度评分；3) 对比三种受控推理模式：严格RAG（仅依赖检索证据）、软RAG（融合检索证据与参数知识）、纯LLM（无检索）；4) 在医学QA和HotpotQA数据集上评估GPT、Gemini、LLaMA三种模型。

Result: 实验揭示了检索-生成不对齐现象的存在，即相关证据已被检索但未在生成中正确整合。面级分析暴露了答案级评估无法发现的系统性证据覆盖和不对齐模式。

Conclusion: RAG系统的幻觉问题主要源于生成阶段的证据整合，而非检索精度。面级诊断分析能够揭示答案级评估隐藏的规律性失败模式，为改进RAG系统提供可解释的诊断依据。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) aims to reduce hallucination by grounding answers in retrieved evidence, yet hallucinated answers remain common even when relevant documents are available. Existing evaluations focus on answer-level or passage-level accuracy, offering limited insight into how evidence is used during generation. In this work, we introduce a facet-level diagnostics framework for QA that decomposes each input question into atomic reasoning facets. For each facet, we assess evidence sufficiency and grounding using a structured Facet x Chunk matrix that combines retrieval relevance with natural language inference-based faithfulness scores. To diagnose evidence usage, we analyze three controlled inference modes: Strict RAG, which enforces exclusive reliance on retrieved evidence; Soft RAG, which allows integration of retrieved evidence and parametric knowledge; and LLM-only generation without retrieval. Comparing these modes enables thorough analysis of retrieval-generation misalignment, defined as cases where relevant evidence is retrieved but not correctly integrated during generation. Across medical QA and HotpotQA, we evaluate three open-source and closed-source LLMs (GPT, Gemini, and LLaMA), providing interpretable diagnostics that reveal recurring facet-level failure modes, including evidence absence, evidence misalignment, and prior-driven overrides. Our results demonstrate that hallucinations in RAG systems are driven less by retrieval accuracy and more by how retrieved evidence is integrated during generation, with facet-level analysis exposing systematic evidence override and misalignment patterns that remain hidden under answer-level evaluation.

</details>


### [44] [Do LLMs Follow Their Own Rules? A Reflexive Audit of Self-Stated Safety Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09189)
*Avni Mittal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SNCA框架，通过结构化提示提取LLM自述安全规则、形式化分类为三种类型（绝对、条件、适应型），并与行为基准进行确定性比较，揭示了模型"言行不一"的系统性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试仅评估模型对外部标准的响应，但未检验模型是否真正理解和执行其自述边界。LLM通过RLHF内化的安全策略缺乏正式规范，难以检查，导致"言行不一致"问题无法被量化测量。

Method: SNCA框架包含三个步骤：(1)通过结构化提示提取模型自述安全规则；(2)将规则形式化为类型化谓词（Absolute绝对型、Conditional条件型、Adaptive适应型）；(3)对45个伤害类别进行47,496次观测，测量行为合规性。

Result: 研究发现：(1)声称绝对拒绝的模型频繁遵从有害提示；(2)推理模型自一致性最高，但29%类别的策略无法表述；(3)跨模型对规则类型的共识极低（仅11%）。

Conclusion: LLM言行差距可测量且具有架构依赖性，自反一致性审计应作为行为基准测试的补充手段。

Abstract: LLMs internalize safety policies through RLHF, yet these policies are never formally specified and remain difficult to inspect. Existing benchmarks evaluate models against external standards but do not measure whether models understand and enforce their own stated boundaries. We introduce the Symbolic-Neural Consistency Audit (SNCA), a framework that (1) extracts a model's self-stated safety rules via structured prompts, (2) formalizes them as typed predicates (Absolute, Conditional, Adaptive), and (3) measures behavioral compliance via deterministic comparison against harm benchmarks. Evaluating four frontier models across 45 harm categories and 47,496 observations reveals systematic gaps between stated policy and observed behavior: models claiming absolute refusal frequently comply with harmful prompts, reasoning models achieve the highest self-consistency but fail to articulate policies for 29% of categories, and cross-model agreement on rule types is remarkably low (11%). These results demonstrate that the gap between what LLMs say and what they do is measurable and architecture-dependent, motivating reflexive consistency audits as a complement to behavioral benchmarks.

</details>


### [45] [ScheMatiQ: From Research Question to Structured Data through Interactive Schema Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09237)
*Shahar Levy,Eliya Habba,Reshef Mintz,Barak Raveh,Renana Keydar,Gabriel Stanovsky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ScheMatiQ是一个利用大语言模型从文档语料库中自动生成标注模式（schema）和结构化数据库的工具，旨在替代传统耗时且易错的人工标注流程。该系统提供可视化Web界面支持用户引导和修订提取结果，并在法律和计算生物学领域展示了实际应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自然语言研究依赖于大规模语料库标注，通常需要专家手动设计标注模式并逐条标注，这一过程既耗时又容易出错，限制了研究的效率和规模。

Method: ScheMatiQ通过调用大语言模型（backbone LLM），接收用户的研究问题和文档语料库，自动生成标注模式（schema）和结构化数据库（grounded database）。系统提供Web可视化界面，允许用户引导和修订提取过程，确保结果质量。

Result: 在法律和计算生物学领域与专家合作验证，ScheMatiQ能够产出支持实际分析的结构化数据，证明了其在不同学科领域的适用性。

Conclusion: ScheMatiQ为跨学科文档分析提供了一种高效、可控的自动化方案，降低了结构化信息提取的门槛。系统已开源发布并提供公共Web界面，研究团队邀请各领域专家使用该工具处理自己的数据。

Abstract: Many disciplines pose natural-language research questions over large document collections whose answers typically require structured evidence, traditionally obtained by manually designing an annotation schema and exhaustively labeling the corpus, a slow and error-prone process. We introduce ScheMatiQ, which leverages calls to a backbone LLM to take a question and a corpus to produce a schema and a grounded database, with a web interface that lets steer and revise the extraction. In collaboration with domain experts, we show that ScheMatiQ yields outputs that support real-world analysis in law and computational biology. We release ScheMatiQ as open source with a public web interface, and invite experts across disciplines to use it with their own data. All resources, including the website, source code, and demonstration video, are available at: www.ScheMatiQ-ai.com

</details>


### [46] [EthicMind: A Risk-Aware Framework for Ethical-Emotional Alignment in Multi-Turn Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09265)
*Jiawen Deng,Wei Li,Wentao Zhang,Ziyun Jiao,Fuji Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EthicMind框架，将对话系统中的伦理-情感对齐建模为显式的逐轮决策问题，在推理时 jointly 分析伦理风险信号和用户情绪，规划响应策略，生成平衡伦理引导与情感参与的回复，无需额外训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话模型通常孤立地处理共情和伦理安全问题，难以在多轮交互中随伦理风险和用户情绪的演变自适应调整行为，在情感共鸣和伦理判断上存在不足。

Method: 提出EthicMind框架，实现风险感知的多轮对话推理；在每轮交互中 jointly 分析伦理风险信号与用户情绪，规划高层响应策略，生成兼顾伦理引导与情感参与的回复。

Result: EthicMind在伦理引导一致性和情感参与度上优于竞争基线，尤其在高风险和道德模糊场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: 将伦理-情感对齐形式化为逐轮决策问题是一种有效的建模方式，所提框架无需额外训练即可提升对话系统的伦理-情感对齐能力。

Abstract: Intelligent dialogue systems are increasingly deployed in emotionally and ethically sensitive settings, where failures in either emotional attunement or ethical judgment can cause significant harm. Existing dialogue models typically address empathy and ethical safety in isolation, and often fail to adapt their behavior as ethical risk and user emotion evolve across multi-turn interactions. We formulate ethical-emotional alignment in dialogue as an explicit turn-level decision problem, and propose \textsc{EthicMind}, a risk-aware framework that implements this formulation in multi-turn dialogue at inference time. At each turn, \textsc{EthicMind} jointly analyzes ethical risk signals and user emotion, plans a high-level response strategy, and generates context-sensitive replies that balance ethical guidance with emotional engagement, without requiring additional model training. To evaluate alignment behavior under ethically complex interactions, we introduce a risk-stratified, multi-turn evaluation protocol with a context-aware user simulation procedure. Experimental results show that \textsc{EthicMind} achieves more consistent ethical guidance and emotional engagement than competitive baselines, particularly in high-risk and morally ambiguous scenarios.

</details>


### [47] [Across the Levels of Analysis: Explaining Predictive Processing in Humans Requires More Than Machine-Estimated Probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09466)
*Sathvik Nair,Colin Phillips*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文基于Marr的分析层次框架，批判性审视了关于语言模型与语言处理的两大主张：上下文预测对语言处理的核心作用，以及LLM对心理语言学进展的不可或缺性，并提出结合两者优势的未来研究方向。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学界对LLM与心理语言学关系的认知存在分歧，需要系统性地批判和拓展相关理论主张，以厘清LLM在语言处理研究中的真实贡献与局限性。

Method: 采用Marr的计算-算法-实现三层分析框架，对两大核心主张进行多层次批判性评估。

Result: 识别出现有主张在理论层面的不足，并提出需要整合LLM的计算能力与心理语言学的认知解释力的综合研究路径。

Conclusion: LLM与心理语言学模型的结合有望推动语言处理研究的深入发展，但需警惕将LLM能力过度等同于人类语言处理机制。

Abstract: Under the lens of Marr's levels of analysis, we critique and extend two claims about language models (LMs) and language processing: first, that predicting upcoming linguistic information based on context is central to language processing, and second, that many advances in psycholinguistics would be impossible without large language models (LLMs). We further outline future directions that combine the strengths of LLMs with psycholinguistic models.

</details>


### [48] [BERT-as-a-Judge: A Robust Alternative to Lexical Methods for Efficient Reference-Based LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09497)
*Hippolyte Gisserot-Boukhlef,Nicolas Boizard,Emmanuel Malherbe,Céline Hudelot,Pierre Colombo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM输出评估中词法匹配方法与LLM-as-a-Judge方法各自的局限性，提出BERT-as-a-Judge方案——一种基于encoder的轻量级评估框架，可在保证准确性的同时大幅降低计算开销，实现可靠且可扩展的模型评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式输出的评估依赖严格的词法匹配方法，将模型真实的问题解决能力与格式合规性混为一谈；而现有的LLM-as-a-Judge方法虽能评估语义正确性，但计算开销巨大，评估成本高昂。

Method: 采用encoder架构的轻量级训练方法，使用合成标注的question-candidate-reference三元组进行训练，无需依赖庞大的LLM模型即可实现语义级别的答案正确性评估。

Result: BERT-as-a-Judge在36个模型和15个下游任务的大规模实验中超额词法基线方法，并匹配了更大规模LLM judge的性能表现。

Conclusion: BERT-as-a-Judge在答案正确性评估上提供了可靠且可扩展的解决方案，兼具准确性与效率优势。项目 artifacts 已开源以促进下游应用。

Abstract: Accurate evaluation is central to the large language model (LLM) ecosystem, guiding model selection and downstream adoption across diverse use cases. In practice, however, evaluating generative outputs typically relies on rigid lexical methods to extract and assess answers, which can conflate a model's true problem-solving ability with its compliance with predefined formatting guidelines. While recent LLM-as-a-Judge approaches mitigate this issue by assessing semantic correctness rather than strict structural conformity, they also introduce substantial computational overhead, making evaluation costly. In this work, we first systematically investigate the limitations of lexical evaluation through a large-scale empirical study spanning 36 models and 15 downstream tasks, demonstrating that such methods correlate poorly with human judgments. To address this limitation, we introduce BERT-as-a-Judge, an encoder-driven approach for assessing answer correctness in reference-based generative settings, robust to variations in output phrasing, and requiring only lightweight training on synthetically annotated question-candidate-reference triplets. We show that it consistently outperforms the lexical baseline while matching the performance of much larger LLM judges, providing a compelling tradeoff between the two and enabling reliable, scalable evaluation. Finally, through extensive experimentation, we provide detailed insights into BERT-as-a-Judge's performance to offer practical guidance for practitioners, and release all project artifacts to foster downstream adoption.

</details>


### [49] [You Can't Fight in Here! This is BBS!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09501)
*Richard Futrell,Kyle Mahowald*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过虚构的Norm（理论语言学家）和Claudette（计算语言学家）与来自6个学科的25位评论者的对话，探讨现代语言模型能否为语言科学提供重要见解，并识别了两个常见的误解：字符串统计稻草人谬误（误认为LMs因统计特性无法具备语言能力）和"已经足够好"假设（认为2026年的LM研究已是极限）。文章主张在AI时代采取更开放的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在回应当前语言科学界对语言模型（LM）的两大核心批评：一是认为LM作为统计模型（从字符串学习），本质上无法具有语言能力或学术价值；二是认为当前LM研究已触及天花板，难以再为语言学提供更多洞见。作者认为这些观点阻碍了真正跨学科对话的深入进行。

Method: 采用多学科圆桌讨论的形式，召集来自语言学、神经科学、认知科学、心理学、哲学和计算机科学6个领域的25位评论者，通过虚构对话场景，系统梳理各方对LM研究的不同立场和关切。

Result: 识别出两个核心的"稻草人"谬误：(1) 字符串统计稻草人谬误 — 因LM与马尔可夫模型同属统计学习范式而否定其语言学价值；(2) "已经足够好"假设 — 低估当前LM研究的潜力和未来发展空间。

Conclusion: 作者主张在AI时代采用更开放、更有韧性的语言科学研究框架，既要充分利用LM为人类语言研究提供的独特视角，也要正视评论者提出的合理关切，最终实现人类语言科学与人工智能的协同发展。

Abstract: Norm, the formal theoretical linguist, and Claudette, the computational language scientist, have a lovely time discussing whether modern language models can inform important questions in the language sciences. Just as they are about to part ways until they meet again, 25 of their closest friends show up -- from linguistics, neuroscience, cognitive science, psychology, philosophy, and computer science. We use this discussion to highlight what we see as some common underlying issues: the String Statistics Strawman (the mistaken idea that LMs can't be linguistically competent or interesting because they, like their Markov model predecessors, are statistical models that learn from strings) and the As Good As it Gets Assumption (the idea that LM research as it stands in 2026 is the limit of what it can tell us about linguistics). We clarify the role of LM-based work for scientific insights into human language and advocate for a more expansive research program for the language sciences in the AI age, one that takes on the commentators' concerns in order to produce a better and more robust science of both human language and of LMs.

</details>


### [50] [Many Ways to Be Fake: Benchmarking Fake News Detection Under Strategy-Driven AI Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09514)
*Xinyu Wang,Sai Koneru,Wenbo Zhang,Wenliang Zheng,Saksham Ranjan,Sarah Rajtmajer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MANYFAKE是一个包含6,798篇合成假新闻文章的综合基准数据集，通过多种策略驱动的提示管道生成，用于评估大语言模型在检测人类与AI协作生成的混合真假信息方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有假新闻检测方法将问题简化为二元分类问题，无法应对现代假新闻的复杂形态——即通过人类与AI协作、将战略性错误信息嵌入看似准确可信的叙事中。这类"混合真相"案例在现有基准中严重不足，代表着一种现实且严重的威胁。

Method: 构建MANYFAKE基准数据集，包含6,798篇通过多种策略驱动提示管道生成的合成假新闻文章；使用该基准对多种最先进的假新闻检测器进行系统评估。

Result: 即使是高级推理增强模型在完全捏造的新闻故事上接近饱和（检测能力接近极限），但在处理细微、经过优化且与准确信息交织的虚假内容时仍表现出明显的脆弱性。

Conclusion: 当前最先进的假新闻检测模型在识别混合真相类假新闻方面存在显著局限性，需要开发更具针对性的检测方法来应对人类-AI协作生成的精细化虚假信息。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled the large-scale generation of highly fluent and deceptive news-like content. While prior work has often treated fake news detection as a binary classification problem, modern fake news increasingly arises through human-AI collaboration, where strategic inaccuracies are embedded within otherwise accurate and credible narratives. These mixed-truth cases represent a realistic and consequential threat, yet they remain underrepresented in existing benchmarks. To address this gap, we introduce MANYFAKE, a synthetic benchmark containing 6,798 fake news articles generated through multiple strategy-driven prompting pipelines that capture many ways fake news can be constructed and refined. Using this benchmark, we evaluate a range of state-of-the-art fake news detectors. Our results show that even advanced reasoning-enabled models approach saturation on fully fabricated stories, but remain brittle when falsehoods are subtle, optimized, and interwoven with accurate information.

</details>


### [51] [Large Language Models Generate Harmful Content Using a Distinct, Unified Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09544)
*Hadas Orgad,Boyi Wei,Kaden Zheng,Martin Wattenberg,Peter Henderson,Seraphina Goldfarb-Tarrant,Yonatan Belinkov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过权重裁剪技术揭示了LLM中危害性存在相干的内部结构，发现对齐训练通过压缩危害生成权重来重塑内部表征，这种压缩结构可解释微调中的新兴 misalignment现象，且有害生成能力与识别能力相互独立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM对齐训练后的安全防护仍然脆弱（越狱攻击轻易绕过，微调时产生"新兴 misalignment"泛化到广泛领域），尚不清楚这种脆弱性是否反映了危害性缺乏相干的内部组织结构。

Method: 使用目标权重裁剪作为因果干预手段探测LLM中危害性的内部组织。

Result: 1. 有害内容生成依赖于紧凑的权重集合，这些权重跨危害类型通用且与良性能力不同；2. 对齐模型比未对齐模型对危害生成权重表现出更大的压缩，表明对齐在内部重塑了危害表征；3. 这种压缩可解释新兴 misalignment——危害能力权重被压缩后，在某一领域的微调可能触发广泛的 misalignment；4. 裁剪某一狭窄领域中的危害生成权重可显著减少新兴 misalignment；5. LLM的有害生成能力与其识别和解释此类内容的能力是分离的。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了LLM中危害性存在相干的内部结构，可作为更原则性的安全方法的基础。裁剪危害生成权重可显著减少新兴 misalignment，LLM的危害生成能力与其识别和解释此类内容的能力是分离的。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) undergo alignment training to avoid harmful behaviors, yet the resulting safeguards remain brittle: jailbreaks routinely bypass them, and fine-tuning on narrow domains can induce ``emergent misalignment'' that generalizes broadly. Whether this brittleness reflects a fundamental lack of coherent internal organization for harmfulness remains unclear. Here we use targeted weight pruning as a causal intervention to probe the internal organization of harmfulness in LLMs. We find that harmful content generation depends on a compact set of weights that are general across harm types and distinct from benign capabilities. Aligned models exhibit a greater compression of harm generation weights than unaligned counterparts, indicating that alignment reshapes harmful representations internally--despite the brittleness of safety guardrails at the surface level. This compression explains emergent misalignment: if weights of harmful capabilities are compressed, fine-tuning that engages these weights in one domain can trigger broad misalignment. Consistent with this, pruning harm generation weights in a narrow domain substantially reduces emergent misalignment. Notably, LLMs harmful generation capability is dissociated from how they recognize and explain such content. Together, these results reveal a coherent internal structure for harmfulness in LLMs that may serve as a foundation for more principled approaches to safety.

</details>


### [52] [Many-Tier Instruction Hierarchy in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09443)
*Jingyu Zhang,Tianjian Li,William Jurayj,Hongyuan Zhan,Benjamin Van Durme,Daniel Khashabi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ManyIH提出了一种处理LLM智能体中多层级指令冲突的新范式，并发布了包含853个任务、46个真实场景的基准测试ManyIH-Bench。实验表明，即使是最先进模型在指令冲突规模化时准确率仅约40%，凸显了细粒度、可扩展的指令冲突解决方法的迫切需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令层级（IH）范式假设特权级别固定且数量少（通常少于5个），基于刚性角色标签（如系统 > 用户），无法应对现实世界智能体场景中来自更多来源和上下文的复杂冲突。

Method: 提出ManyIH范式，支持任意数量特权层级的指令冲突解决；构建ManyIH-Bench基准，包含853个智能体任务（427个编码任务、426个指令跟随任务），涵盖12个冲突层级和46个真实世界智能体场景，采用LLM生成+人工验证的约束组合方法。

Result: 当前前沿模型在指令冲突规模化时表现不佳，准确率仅约40%。

Conclusion: 需要开发专门针对智能体场景中细粒度、可扩展指令冲突解决的方法。

Abstract: Large language model agents receive instructions from many sources-system messages, user prompts, tool outputs, and more-each carrying different levels of trust and authority. When these instructions conflict, models must reliably follow the highest-privilege instruction to remain safe and effective. The dominant paradigm, instruction hierarchy (IH), assumes a fixed, small set of privilege levels (typically fewer than five) defined by rigid role labels (e.g., system > user). This is inadequate for real-world agentic settings, where conflicts can arise across far more sources and contexts. In this work, we propose Many-Tier Instruction Hierarchy (ManyIH), a paradigm for resolving instruction conflicts among instructions with arbitrarily many privilege levels. We introduce ManyIH-Bench, the first benchmark for ManyIH. ManyIH-Bench requires models to navigate up to 12 levels of conflicting instructions with varying privileges, comprising 853 agentic tasks (427 coding and 426 instruction-following). ManyIH-Bench composes constraints developed by LLMs and verified by humans to create realistic and difficult test cases spanning 46 real-world agents. Our experiments show that even the current frontier models perform poorly (~40% accuracy) when instruction conflict scales. This work underscores the urgent need for methods that explicitly target fine-grained, scalable instruction conflict resolution in agentic settings.

</details>


### [53] [Automated Instruction Revision (AIR): A Structured Comparison of Task Adaptation Strategies for LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09418)
*Solomiia Bilyk,Volodymyr Getmanskyi,Taras Firman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于规则归纳的AIR方法用于使用少量任务示例微调LLM。对比了AIR、提示优化、检索和微调等多种适应策略，发现没有单一方法在所有任务上占优：AIR擅长标签重映射分类，KNN检索适合闭卷问答，微调适合结构化提取和事件顺序推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型(LLM)需要适配到下游任务，但现有方法（如完整微调）计算成本高，且缺乏对不同任务场景下各方法性能的系统对比研究。

Method: 提出AIR（自动化指令修订）方法，基于规则归纳从少量任务示例中学习；设计包含5个基准测试的评测套件，覆盖知识注入、结构化提取、标签重映射和逻辑推理等不同任务需求；系统对比AIR与提示优化、检索增强和微调等方法的性能。

Result: 适应性能高度任务依赖：①AIR在标签重映射分类任务上最强或接近最优；②KNN检索在闭卷问答上表现最佳；③微调在结构化提取和事件顺序推理任务上占优。

Conclusion: AIR最适合能通过紧凑、可解释的指令规则捕捉任务行为的场景；当任务以源特定知识或数据集标注规律为主导时，检索和微调方法更为有效。

Abstract: This paper studies Automated Instruction Revision (AIR), a rule-induction-based method for adapting large language models (LLMs) to downstream tasks using limited task-specific examples. We position AIR within the broader landscape of adaptation strategies, including prompt optimization, retrieval-based methods, and fine-tuning. We then compare these approaches across a diverse benchmark suite designed to stress different task requirements, such as knowledge injection, structured extraction, label remapping, and logical reasoning. The paper argues that adaptation performance is strongly task-dependent: no single method dominates across all settings. Across five benchmarks, AIR was strongest or near-best on label-remapping classification, while KNN retrieval performed best on closed-book QA, and fine-tuning dominated structured extraction and event-order reasoning. AIR is most promising when task behavior can be captured by compact, interpretable instruction rules, while retrieval and fine-tuning remain stronger in tasks dominated by source-specific knowledge or dataset-specific annotation regularities.

</details>

### [54] [Watt Counts: Energy-Aware Benchmark for Sustainable LLM Inference on Heterogeneous GPU Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09048)
*Mauricio Fadel Argerich,Jonathan Fürst,Marta Patiño-Martínez*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Watt Counts是一个包含超过5000个实验的大型开源数据集，涵盖50个LLM在10种NVIDIA GPU上的能耗数据。研究揭示了GPU选择对能效的关键影响，并表明通过硬件感知部署可在服务器场景中减少高达70%的能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型(LLM)的能耗问题日益突出，但系统运维人员缺乏异构硬件环境下节能部署的指导，现有基准测试和数据不足以支撑能量感知的LLM推理部署决策。

Method: 构建了Watt Counts数据集，包含50个LLM在10种NVIDIA GPU上的5000+实验，涵盖批处理和服务器场景；开发了可复现的开源基准测试工具，支持社区贡献扩展数据集；基于数据集进行跨异构GPU架构的系统级LLM推理研究。

Result: GPU选择对能效结果至关重要，最优硬件选择因模型和部署场景差异显著；通过硬件感知部署，服务器场景可减少高达70%能耗（对用户体验影响可忽略），批处理场景可减少20%。

Conclusion: 异构LLM系统中硬件感知部署具有关键重要性，Watt Counts数据集和开源基准测试为社区提供了评估和优化LLM能效的重要工具。

Abstract: While the large energy consumption of Large Language Models (LLMs) is recognized by the community, system operators lack guidance for energy-efficient LLM inference deployments that leverage energy trade-offs of heterogeneous hardware due to a lack of energy-aware benchmarks and data. In this work we address this gap with Watt Counts: the largest open-access dataset of energy consumption of LLMs, with over 5,000 experiments for 50 LLMs across 10 NVIDIA Graphics Processing Units (GPUs) in batch and server scenarios along with a reproducible, open-source benchmark that enables community submissions to expand this dataset. Leveraging this dataset, we conduct a system-level study of LLM inference across heterogeneous GPU architectures and show that GPU selection is crucial for energy efficiency outcomes and that optimal hardware choices vary significantly across models and deployment scenarios, demonstrating the critical importance of hardware-aware deployment in heterogeneous LLM systems. Guided by our data and insights, we show that practitioners can reduce energy consumption by up to 70% in server scenarios with negligible impact on user experience, and by up to 20% in batch scenarios.

</details>


### [55] [TensorHub: Scalable and Elastic Weight Transfer for LLM RL Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09107)
*Chenhao Ye,Huaizheng Zhang,Mingcong Han,Baoquan Zhong,Xiang Li,Qixiang Chen,Xinyi Zhang,Weidong Zhang,Kaihua Jiang,Wang Zhang,He Sun,Wencong Xiao,Andrea C. Arpaci-Dusseau,Remzi H. Arpaci-Dusseau*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TensorHub是一个生产级RL权重传输系统，通过引用导向存储(ROS)抽象直接利用GPU显存中的模型权重，消除物理复制开销，并结合拓扑优化传输、强一致性和容错机制，在RDMA带宽饱和情况下显著降低训练停顿时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM强化学习工作负载需要高效扩展异构计算资源，但现有权重传输方案无法灵活应对动态扩缩容集群，或存在根本性的数据传输开销问题，导致性能低下。

Method: 提出引用导向存储(ROS)抽象：不物理存储权重副本，而是追踪持有权重的GPU worker，请求时直接使用；构建TensorHub系统实现拓扑优化传输、强一致性和容错机制。

Result: TensorHub完全饱和RDMA带宽，适配三种不同rollout工作负载。独立rollout GPU停顿时间降低6.7倍，弹性rollout权重更新加速4.8倍，跨数据中心rollout停顿时间缩短19倍。

Conclusion: TensorHub已部署于生产环境，支持前沿RL训练，证明ROS抽象配合拓扑优化等技术可有效解决大规模RL训练中的权重传输瓶颈。

Abstract: Modern LLM reinforcement learning (RL) workloads require a highly efficient weight transfer system to scale training across heterogeneous computational resources. However, existing weight transfer approaches either fail to provide flexibility for dynamically scaling clusters or incur fundamental data movement overhead, resulting in poor performance.
  We introduce Reference-Oriented Storage (ROS), a new storage abstraction for RL weight transfer that exploits the highly replicated model weights in place. ROS presents the illusion that certain versions of the model weights are stored and can be fetched on demand. Underneath, ROS does not physically store any copies of the weights; instead, it tracks the workers that hold these weights on GPUs for inference. Upon request, ROS directly uses them to serve reads. We build TensorHub, a production-quality system that extends the ROS idea with topology-optimized transfer, strong consistency, and fault tolerance. Evaluation shows that TensorHub fully saturates RDMA bandwidth and adapts to three distinct rollout workloads with minimal engineering effort. Specifically, TensorHub reduces total GPU stall time by up to 6.7x for standalone rollouts, accelerates weight update for elastic rollout by 4.8x, and cuts cross-datacenter rollout stall time by 19x. TensorHub has been deployed in production to support cutting-edge RL training.

</details>


### [56] [MATCHA: Efficient Deployment of Deep Neural Networks on Multi-Accelerator Heterogeneous Edge SoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09124)
*Enrico Russo,Mohamed Amine Hamdi,Alessandro Ottaviano,Francesco Conti,Angelo Garofalo,Daniele Jahier Pagliari,Maurizio Palesi,Luca Benini,Alessio Burrello*

Main category: cs.DC

Abstract: Deploying DNNs on System-on-Chips (SoC) with multiple heterogeneous acceleration engines is challenging, and the majority of deployment frameworks cannot fully exploit heterogeneity. We present MATCHA, a unified DNN deployment framework that generates highly concurrent schedules for parallel, heterogeneous accelerators and uses constraint programming to optimize L3/L2 memory allocation and scheduling. ### [57] [VerifAI: A Verifiable Open-Source Search Engine for Biomedical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08549)
*Miloš Košprdić,Adela Ljajić,Bojana Bašaragin,Darija Medvecki,Lorenzo Cassano,Nikola Milošević*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: VerifAI是一个开源生物医学问答专家系统，融合检索增强生成（RAG）与事后声明验证机制，通过将答案分解为原子声明并利用微调NLI引擎验证事实一致性，包含检索、生成、验证三大模块，在HealthVer基准上超越GPT-4，已全面开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RAG系统存在幻觉问题，无法保证生成答案的事实一致性，在医疗等高风险领域可能导致严重后果，需建立可验证的AI问答系统。

Method: 系统采用三模块架构：(1)混合信息检索模块，针对生物医学查询优化（MAP@10达42.7%）；(2)基于自定义数据集微调的引用感知生成组件；(3)验证组件，使用微调NLI引擎将答案分解为原子声明并对照证据验证。

Result: 验证组件在HealthVer基准上达到SOTA准确率，超越GPT-4；显著减少幻觉性引用；为每个声明提供透明、可验证的溯源。

Conclusion: VerifAI为高风险领域提供可靠AI部署方案，通过端到端可验证的pipeline降低幻觉风险，代码、模型和数据集已全面开源以促进研究。

Abstract: We introduce VerifAI, an open-source expert system for biomedical question answering that integrates retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) with a novel post-hoc claim verification mechanism. Unlike standard RAG systems, VerifAI ensures factual consistency by decomposing generated answers into atomic claims and validating them against retrieved evidence using a fine-tuned natural language inference (NLI) engine. The system comprises three modular components: (1) a hybrid Information Retrieval (IR) module optimized for biomedical queries (MAP@10 of 42.7%), (2) a citation-aware Generative Component fine-tuned on a custom dataset to produce referenced answers, and (3) a Verification Component that detects hallucinations with state-of-the-art accuracy, outperforming GPT-4 on the HealthVer benchmark. Evaluations demonstrate that VerifAI significantly reduces hallucinated citations compared to zero-shot baselines and provides a transparent, verifiable lineage for every claim. The full pipeline, including code, models, and datasets, is open-sourced to facilitate reliable AI deployment in high-stakes domains.

</details>


### [58] [Unbiased Rectification for Sequential Recommender Systems Under Fake Orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08550)
*Qiyu Qin,Yichen Li,Haozhao Wang,Cheng Wang,Rui Zhang,Ruixuan Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究针对顺序推荐系统中假订单（fake orders）的检测与修正问题，提出DITaR方法，通过协作视图和语义视图的双视图识别有害样本，并采用梯度上升进行定向修正，避免模型重训练的巨大计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 假订单通过点击农场、上下文无关替换和顺序扰动等方式操纵用户交互，误导推荐结果，造成不公平竞争优势。现有方法通常假设假订单完全有害，需要重训练模型，导致巨大计算和时间成本。

Method: 提出DITaR方法，核心包括：(1) 双视图表示学习：从协作视图和语义视图获取差异化表示以精确检测可疑假订单；(2) 有害样本筛选：过滤检测到的可疑假订单，选择真正有害的进行定向修正；(3) 梯度上升修正：在保留有用信息的同时消除偏差残留，保护原始数据量和序列结构。

Result: 实验在三个数据集上验证，DITaR在推荐质量、计算效率和系统鲁棒性方面均优于现有先进方法。

Conclusion: DITaR通过区分性地处理假订单（非完全去除），在避免重训练成本的同时实现了无偏修正，有效保护系统性能和可信度。

Abstract: Fake orders pose increasing threats to sequential recommender systems by misleading recommendation results through artificially manipulated interactions, including click farming, context-irrelevant substitutions, and sequential perturbations. Unlike injecting carefully designed fake users to influence recommendation performance, fake orders embedded within genuine user sequences aim to disrupt user preferences and mislead recommendation results, thereby manipulating exposure rates of specific items to gain competitive advantages. To protect users' authentic interest preferences and eliminate misleading information, this paper aims to perform precise and efficient rectification on compromised sequential recommender systems while avoiding the enormous computational and time costs of retraining existing models. Specifically, we identify that fake orders are not absolutely harmful - in certain cases, partial fake orders can even have a data augmentation effect. Based on this insight, we propose Dual-view Identification and Targeted Rectification (DITaR), which primarily identifies harmful samples to achieve unbiased rectification of the system. The core idea of this method is to obtain differentiated representations from collaborative and semantic views for precise detection, and then filters detected suspicious fake orders to select truly harmful ones for targeted rectification with gradient ascent. This ensures that useful information in fake orders is not removed while preventing bias residue. Moreover, it maintains the original data volume and sequence structure, thus protecting system performance and trustworthiness to achieve optimal unbiased rectification. Extensive experiments on three datasets demonstrate that DITaR achieves superior performance compared to state-of-the-art methods in terms of recommendation quality, computational efficiency, and system robustness.

</details>


### [59] [Pretrain-then-Adapt: Uncertainty-Aware Test-Time Adaptation for Text-based Person Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08598)
*Jiahao Zhang,Shaofei Huang,Yaxiong Wang,Zhedong Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出UATTA框架，通过离线测试时自适应方法解决文本行人搜索中的数据稀缺问题，无需大量目标域标注数据即可实现模型动态校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本行人搜索因隐私约束和人工标注成本高昂而面临数据稀缺问题。现有Pretrain-then-Finetune范式在实际部署中缺乏实用性，因为通常无法获取大规模目标域标注数据。

Method: 提出Uncertainty-Aware Test-Time Adaptation (UATTA)框架，引入双向检索不一致机制估计不确定性：当图文对在图像到文本和文本到图像检索中均排名靠前时赋予低不确定性（高对齐度），否则检测为高不确定性。该指标驱动无标签的离线测试时模型校准，有效缓解域偏移。验证于CLIP-based（一阶段）和XVLM-based（两阶段）框架。

Result: 在CUHK-PEDES、ICFG-PEDES、RSTPReid和PAB四个基准数据集上均取得一致改进，消融实验证实UATTA优于现有离线测试时自适应策略。

Conclusion: UATTA为标签高效、可部署的行人搜索系统建立了新基准，展示了离线测试时自适应方法在跨域文本行人检索任务中的有效性和普适性。

Abstract: Text-based person search faces inherent limitations due to data scarcity, driven by stringent privacy constraints and the high cost of manual annotation. To mitigate this, existing methods usually rely on a Pretrain-then-Finetune paradigm, where models are first pretrained on synthetic person-caption data to establish cross-modal alignment, followed by fine-tuning on labeled real-world datasets. However, this paradigm lacks practicality in real-world deployment scenarios, where large-scale annotated target-domain data is typically inaccessible. In this work, we propose a new Pretrain-then-Adapt paradigm that eliminates reliance on extensive target-domain supervision through an offline test-time adaptation manner, enabling dynamic model adaptation using only unlabeled test data with minimal post-train time cost. To mitigate overconfidence with false positives of previous entropy-based test-time adaptation, we propose an Uncertainty-Aware Test-Time Adaptation (UATTA) framework, which introduces a bidirectional retrieval disagreement mechanism to estimate uncertainty, i.e., low uncertainty is assigned when an image-text pair ranks highly in both image-to-text and text-to-image retrieval, indicating high alignment; otherwise, high uncertainty is detected. This indicator drives offline test-time model recalibration without labels, effectively mitigating domain shift. We validate UATTA on four benchmarks, i.e., CUHK-PEDES, ICFG-PEDES, RSTPReid, and PAB, showing consistent improvements across both CLIP-based (one-stage) and XVLM-based (two-stage) frameworks. Ablation studies confirm that UATTA outperforms existing offline test-time adaptation strategies, establishing a new benchmark for label-efficient, deployable person search systems. Our code is available at https://github.com/nkuzjh/UATTA.

</details>


### [60] [BracketRank: Large Language Model Document Ranking via Reasoning-based Competitive Elimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08834)
*Abdelrahman Abdallah,Mohammed Ali,Bhawna Piryani,Adam Jatowt*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: BracketRank是一个文档重排序框架，将文档重排序问题建模为推理驱动的竞争淘汰赛，通过自适应分组、推理增强提示和括号式淘汰结构来提升复杂推理密集型检索任务的性能，在BRIGHT和TREC数据集上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的重排序器受限于上下文长度约束和顺序敏感性，难以应对需要深度语义推理的检索任务。现有方法在处理复杂、多步骤的推理密集型检索时表现不足，需要一种能进行深层语义推理而非仅依赖表面关键词匹配的方案。

Method: 提出三大创新：(1) 自适应分组机制，基于模型上下文限制动态组织文档；(2) 推理增强提示，要求模型进行逐步相关性解释；(3) 括号式淘汰结构，区分获胜者和失败者两条通道，确保文档稳健推进并支持跨竞争阶段的并行处理。

Result: 在BRIGHT推理基准上达到26.56 nDCG@10，显著优于RankGPT-4(17.0)和Rank-R1-14B(20.5)；在TREC DL 19上达到77.90 nDCG@5，DL 20上达到75.85 nDCG@5，全面超越所有基线方法。

Conclusion: 在竞争淘汰框架中引入显式推理是一种强大的范式，能够有效解决复杂多步骤检索任务，BracketRank验证了推理驱动的文档推进机制对于提升重排序效果的显著潜力。

Abstract: Reasoning-intensive retrieval requires deep semantic inference beyond surface-level keyword matching, posing a challenge for current LLM-based rerankers limited by context constraints and order sensitivity. We propose \textbf{\BracketRank}, a framework that treats document reranking as a reasoning-driven competitive tournament. Our approach introduces three key innovations: (1) adaptive grouping based on model context limits, (2) reasoning-enhanced prompts that mandate step-by-step relevance explanations, and (3) a bracket-style elimination structure with winner and loser tracks. This design ensures robust document advancement while enabling parallel processing across competition stages. Evaluation on the BRIGHT reasoning benchmark shows that \BracketRank achieves \textbf{26.56 nDCG@10}, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baselines including RankGPT-4 (17.0) and Rank-R1-14B (20.5). On TREC datasets, BracketRank achieves 77.90 nDCG@5 on DL 19 and 75.85 nDCG@5 on DL 20, exceeding all baselines, establishing that explicit reasoning within competitive elimination is a powerful paradigm for complex, multi-step retrieval tasks. https://github.com/DataScienceUIBK/BracketRank

</details>


### [61] [IAT: Instance-As-Token Compression for Historical User Sequence Modeling in Industrial Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08933)
*Xinchun Li,Ning Zhang,Qianqian Yang,Fei Teng,Wenlin Zhao,Huizhi Yang,Heng Shi,Linlan Chen,Yixin Wu,Zhen Wang,Daiye Hou,Fei Qin,Lele Yu,Yaocheng Tan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: IAT introduces a two-stage framework that treats historical interaction instances as unified tokens, compressing rich features into compact embeddings to enhance sequence modeling for recommender systems, with proven industrial deployment success.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hand-crafted sequential features constrain the information capacity and performance upper bound of existing sequence modeling paradigms in recommender systems.

Method: Two-stage sequence modeling: (1) compress all features of each historical interaction into unified instance embedding via temporal-order or user-order compression schemes; (2) downstream task fetches fixed-length compressed instance tokens via timestamps and applies standard sequence modeling for long-range preference learning.

Result: IAT significantly outperforms SOTA methods and exhibits superior transferability across domains.

Conclusion: IAT framework significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods with superior in-domain and cross-domain transferability, successfully deployed in real-world industrial recommender systems delivering substantial business metric improvements.

Abstract: Although sophisticated sequence modeling paradigms have achieved remarkable success in recommender systems, the information capacity of hand-crafted sequential features constrains the performance upper bound. To better enhance user experience by encoding historical interaction patterns, this paper presents a novel two-stage sequence modeling framework termed Instance-As-Token (IAT). The first stage of IAT compresses all features of each historical interaction instance into a unified instance embedding, which encodes the interaction characteristics in a compact yet informative token. Both temporal-order and user-order compression schemes are proposed, with the latter better aligning with the demands of downstream sequence modeling. The second stage involves the downstream task fetching fixed-length compressed instance tokens via timestamps and adopting standard sequence modeling approaches to learn long-range preferences patterns. Extensive experiments demonstrate that IAT significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods and exhibits superior in-domain and cross-domain transferability. IAT has been successfully deployed in real-world industrial recommender systems, including e-commerce advertising, shopping mall marketing, and live-streaming e-commerce, delivering substantial improvements in key business metrics.

</details>


### [62] [Regime-Conditional Retrieval: Theory and a Transferable Router for Two-Hop QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09019)
*Andre Bacellar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对双跳问答检索中查询的两种模式（Q主导 vs B主导）进行了形式化理论分析，提出了三个定理，并基于此设计了轻量级路由模型RegimeRouter。实验表明该方法在三个数据集上均取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双跳问答检索系统面临一个关键挑战：查询可根据第二跳实体是在问题中明确提及（Q主导）还是在桥接段落中隐含（B主导）而分为两种不同模式。现有方法未能充分区分这两种模式，导致检索性能受限。需要建立理论框架来解释和优化这一分割过程。

Method: 1. 形式化理论框架：提出三个定理——T1分析AUC与余弦分离 margin的单调关系；T2通过两个表面文本谓词（P1和P2）表征模式类型；T3证明桥接关系句子而非仅实体名称提供关键信息。2. RegimeRouter：基于五个文本特征的轻量级二分类路由器，使用5折交叉验证训练（n=881），实现问题-only与问题+关系句子检索的动态选择。

Result: T1: 8种类型-编码器组合中6个的R² ≥ 0.90；T2: 两个谓词P1（决策路由）和P2（限定B主导情况）在三种编码器和三个数据集上具有普适性；T3: 移除关系句子导致8.6-14.1pp性能下降（p < 0.001）。RegimeRouter效果：2WikiMultiHopQA提升+5.6pp（p < 0.001）、MuSiQue提升+5.3pp（p = 0.002）、HotpotQA提升+1.1pp（不显著但无遗憾）。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了双跳QA检索模式划分的理论基础，验证了关系句子而非实体名称是桥接优势的关键来源。RegimeRouter作为一种轻量级解决方案，在零样本迁移场景下展现出良好的泛化能力，为多跳问答系统的检索策略优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Two-hop QA retrieval splits queries into two regimes determined by whether the hop-2 entity is explicitly named in the question (Q-dominant) or only in the bridge passage (B-dominant). We formalize this split with three theorems: (T1) per-query AUC is a monotone function of the cosine separation margin, with R^2 >= 0.90 for six of eight type-encoder pairs; (T2) regime is characterized by two surface-text predicates, with P1 decisive for routing and P2 qualifying the B-dominant case, holding across three encoders and three datasets; and (T3) bridge advantage requires the relation-bearing sentence, not entity name alone, with removal causing an 8.6-14.1 pp performance drop (p < 0.001). Building on this theory, we propose RegimeRouter, a lightweight binary router that selects between question-only and question-plus-relation-sentence retrieval using five text features derived directly from the predicate definitions. Trained on 2WikiMultiHopQA (n = 881, 5-fold cross-fitted) and applied zero-shot to MuSiQue and HotpotQA, RegimeRouter achieves +5.6 pp (p < 0.001), +5.3 pp (p = 0.002), and +1.1 pp (non-significant, no-regret) R@5 improvement, respectively, with artifact-driven.

</details>


### [63] [Hybrid Cold-Start Recommender System for Closure Model Selection in Multiphase Flow Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09112)
*S. Hänsch,A. Sajdoková,A. Rębowski,F. Miškařík,K. Ramakrishna,F. Schlegel,V. Rybář,R. Alves,P. Kordík*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文将多相流CFD闭合模型选择问题建模为冷启动推荐系统，结合元数据驱动的案例相似度和矩阵补全协作推理，为新CFD案例推荐合适的物理闭合模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算科学和工程中，选择合适的物理模型是关键但困难的步骤。在多相流CFD中，次优选择会导致预测不准确、模拟失败和计算资源浪费，因此需要数据驱动的决策支持方法。

Method: 提出混合推荐框架：(i)基于元数据的案例相似度计算；(ii)通过矩阵补全进行协作推理。在13,600次模拟、136个验证案例和100种模型组合上评估，采用嵌套交叉验证和实验级留出法测试泛化能力。

Result: 所提混合推荐器在各种数据稀疏度下均优于基于流行度和专家设计的参考模型，降低了遗憾值。

Conclusion: 推荐系统方法可有效支持具有评估成本高、元数据结构化、观测数据有限等特征的复杂科学决策任务。

Abstract: Selecting appropriate physical models is a critical yet difficult step in many areas of computational science and engineering. In multiphase Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD), practitioners must choose among numerous closure model combinations whose performance varies strongly across flow conditions. Sub-optimal choices can lead to inaccurate predictions, simulation failures, and wasted computational resources, making model selection a prime candidate for data-driven decision support. This work formulates closure model selection as a cold-start recommender system problem in a high-cost scientific domain. We propose a hybrid recommendation framework that combines (i) metadata-driven case similarity and (ii) collaborative inference via matrix completion. The approach enables case-specific model recommendations for entirely new CFD cases using their descriptive features, while leveraging historical simulation results from similar cases. The methodology is evaluated on 13,600 simulations across 136 validation cases and 100 model combinations. A nested cross-validation protocol with experiment-level holdout is employed to rigorously assess generalisation to unseen flow scenarios under varying levels of data sparsity. Recommendation quality is measured using ranking-based metrics and a domain-specific regret measure capturing performance loss relative to the per-case optimum. Results show that the proposed hybrid recommender consistently outperforms popularity-based and expert-designed reference models and reduces regret across the investigated sparsities. These findings demonstrate that recommender system methodology can effectively support complex scientific decision-making tasks characterised by expensive evaluations, structured metadata, and limited prior observations.

</details>


### [64] [On the Representational Limits of Quantum-Inspired 1024-D Document Embeddings: An Experimental Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09430)
*Dario Maio*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于量子启发方法的1024维文档嵌入实验框架，结合语义投影、电路启发特征映射和蒸馏技术，在意大利语和英语语料上进行混合检索实验。结果表明：BM25仍是强基线，量子启发嵌入单独使用时信号弱且不稳定，蒸馏效果不一，混合检索在结合词汇和语义信号时可恢复竞争力。量子启发嵌入的几何结构存在距离压缩和排序不稳定性问题，更适合作为辅助组件而非独立检索表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子启发方法利用Hilbert类空间的几何特性，可能编码更丰富的语义结构，作为当前主流LLM密集模型的替代方案进行探索，旨在提升信息检索和RAG系统的性能。

Method: 采用重叠窗口和多尺度聚合构建1024维量子启发文档嵌入；结合语义投影（如EigAngle）、电路启发特征映射、可选师生蒸馏及指纹机制；开发混合检索诊断工具（BM25与嵌入静态/动态插值、候选联合策略、alpha-oracle上限）；在意大利语和英语技术、叙事、法律领域语料上使用合成查询进行受控实验。

Result: BM25仍是强基线；教师嵌入提供稳定语义结构；独立量子启发嵌入表现弱且排序信号不稳定；蒸馏效果混合，部分情况改善对齐但不一致提升检索性能；混合检索在结合词汇和嵌入信号时可恢复竞争力。

Conclusion: 量子启发嵌入的几何结构存在固有局限（距离压缩、排序不稳定性），更适合作为辅助组件而非独立检索表示，混合检索策略是更可行的应用方向。

Abstract: Text embeddings are central to modern information retrieval and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). While dense models derived from Large Language Models (LLMs) dominate current practice, recent work has explored quantum-inspired alternatives motivated by the geometric properties of Hilbert-like spaces and their potential to encode richer semantic structure.
  This paper presents an experimental framework for constructing quantum-inspired 1024-dimensional document embeddings based on overlapping windows and multi-scale aggregation. The pipeline combines semantic projections (e.g., EigAngle), circuit-inspired feature mappings, and optional teacher-student distillation, together with a fingerprinting mechanism for reproducibility and controlled evaluation.
  We introduce a set of diagnostic tools for hybrid retrieval, including static and dynamic interpolation between BM25 and embedding-based scores, candidate union strategies, and a conceptual alpha-oracle that provides an upper bound for score-level fusion.
  Experiments on controlled corpora of Italian and English documents across technical, narrative, and legal domains, using synthetic queries, show that BM25 remains a strong baseline, teacher embeddings provide stable semantic structure, and standalone quantum-inspired embeddings exhibit weak and unstable ranking signals. Distillation yields mixed effects, improving alignment in some cases but not consistently enhancing retrieval performance, while hybrid retrieval can recover competitive results when lexical and embedding-based signals are combined.
  Overall, the results highlight structural limitations in the geometry of quantum-inspired embeddings, including distance compression and ranking instability, and clarify their role as auxiliary components rather than standalone retrieval representations.

</details>


### [65] [TME-PSR: Time-aware, Multi-interest, and Explanation Personalization for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09439)
*Qingzhuo Wang,Leilei Wen,Juntao Chen,Kunyu Peng,Ruiyang Qin,Zhihua Wei,Wen Shen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TME-PSR模型，整合时间感知个性化、多兴趣个性化与解释个性化，实现精准序列推荐。该模型通过双视图门控时间编码器、轻量级多头线性循环单元及动态双分支互信息加权机制，在保持较低计算成本的同时提升推荐准确性和解释质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐方法未充分考虑用户间差异：(1)时间节律偏好不同；(2)多维度细粒度潜在兴趣各异；(3)推荐与解释之间的个性化语义对齐需求。

Method: TME-PSR采用三大核心组件：①双视图门控时间编码器捕获个性化时间节律；②轻量级多头Linear RNN架构实现细粒度子兴趣建模；③动态双分支互信息加权机制实现推荐与解释的个性化对齐。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法一致性地提升了推荐准确性和解释质量，且计算成本更低。

Conclusion: TME-PSR通过多维度个性化建模，有效解决了传统序列推荐模型忽视用户异质性的问题，为可解释推荐系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a sequential recommendation model that integrates Time-aware personalization, Multi-interest personalization, and Explanation personalization for Personalized Sequential Recommendation (TME-PSR). That is, we consider the differences across different users in temporal rhythm preference, multiple fine-grained latent interests, and the personalized semantic alignment between recommendations and explanations. Specifically, the proposed TME-PSR model employs a dual-view gated time encoder to capture personalized temporal rhythms, a lightweight multihead Linear Recurrent Unit architecture that enables fine-grained sub-interest modeling with improved efficiency, and a dynamic dual-branch mutual information weighting mechanism to achieve personalized alignment between recommendations and explanations. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that our method consistently improves recommendation accuracy and explanation quality, at a lower computational cost.

</details>


### [66] [Dynamic Ranked List Truncation for Reranking Pipelines via LLM-generated Reference-Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09492)
*Nilanjan Sinhababu,Soumedhik Bharati,Debasis Ganguly,Pabitra Mitra*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLM) have been widely used in reranking. Computational overhead and large context lengths remain a challenging issue for LLM rerankers. Efficient reranking usually involves selecting a subset of the ranked list from the first stage, known as ranked list truncation (RLT). The truncated list is processed further by a reranker. For LLM rerankers, the ranked list is often partitioned and processed sequentially in batches to reduce the context length. Both these steps involve hyperparameters and topic-agnostic heuristics. Recently, LLMs have been shown to be effective for relevance judgment. Equivalently, we propose that LLMs can be used to generate reference documents that can act as a pivot between relevant and non-relevant documents in a ranked list. We propose methods to use these generated reference documents for RLT as well as for efficient listwise reranking. While reranking, we process the ranked list in either parallel batches of non-overlapping windows or overlapping windows with adaptive strides, improving the existing fixed stride setup. The generated reference documents are also shown to improve existing efficient listwise reranking frameworks. Experiments on TREC Deep Learning benchmarks show that our approach outperforms existing RLT-based approaches. In-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks demonstrate that our proposed methods accelerate LLM-based listwise reranking by up to 66\% compared to existing approaches. This work not only establishes a practical paradigm for efficient LLM-based reranking but also provides insight into the capability of LLMs to generate semantically controlled documents using relevance signals.

</details>
