### [67] [Communicate-Predict-Act: Evaluating Social Intelligence of Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08727)
*David Shoresh,Sarit Kraus,Yonatan Loewenstein*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个多人混合合作与竞争的社会游戏环境，用于系统评估大语言模型（LLM）的社会智能能力。通过对8个不同规模（24B到1T参数）的LLM进行评估，发现模型间存在一致的性能差异，但标量评分无法完整刻画社会智能。因此，研究从游戏轨迹中提取了社会认知指标（行动预测、沟通影响、战略推理、利益冲突权衡等），这些指标在模型内具有强一致性，并能有效预测游戏胜负（AUC ROC = 0.82）。特征重要性分析表明，影响力、透明度和适应性对社会成功的预测能力甚至超过心智理论推理或深度规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会智能在人类和AI智能体中都缺乏精确定义，且随着LLM智能体在现实社会场景中的应用增多，系统评估其社会智能能力变得迫切。现有社会智能评估方法不足，需要更系统化、可量化的框架。

Method: 设计了一个多人混合合作与竞争的社会游戏竞技场，采用"沟通-预测-行动"(COMPACT)交互协议，对8个不同规模（24B至1T参数）的LLM进行细粒度社会动态探测。使用Elo评分进行性能排名，并通过游戏轨迹提取社会认知指标。

Result: Elo评分揭示了不同模型间存在一致的性能差异。提取的社会认知指标在模型内具有强一致性，并能可靠预测成对智能体优势（AUC ROC = 0.82）。特征重要性分析发现，影响力（influence）、透明度（transparency）和适应性（adaptability）对成功的预测能力超过心智理论（ToM）推理和深度规划能力。

Conclusion: 研究提出了一个可测试的多维社会智能概念框架，并提供了关于支撑社会智能能力的实证见解：影响力、透明度和适应性是比心智理论更核心的社会智能组成要素，这为理解和评估AI社会智能提供了新视角。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents become more prevalent in real world social settings, social intelligence will play an increasingly critical role. But social intelligence is still a poorly defined construct, for humans and artificial agents. We introduce a multiplayer arena of mixed cooperative and competitive social games to study LLM social intelligence. The controllability of LLM based agents enables systematic evaluation, which also supports broader inferences about social intelligence per se. We evaluated eight diverse LLMs (24B to 1T parameters) using a Communicate Predict Act (COMPACT) interaction protocol and fine grained probing of social dynamics. Elo style ratings reveal consistent performance differences across models, but this scalar measure provides only a partial characterization of social intelligence. To address this limitation, we analyze gameplay traces to extract sociocognitive metrics capturing action prediction, communicative influence, strategic reasoning, and tradeoffs under conflicting interests. These sociocognitive metrics exhibit strong intramodel consistency and they reliably predict pairwise agent advantage in game outcomes (AUC ROC = 0.82). Feature importance analysis indicates that surprisingly, influence, transparency, and adaptability are more predictive of success than Theory of Mind inference or deep planning. Together, our results advance a testable, multidimensional conception of social intelligence and provide empirical insights into the capacities that underpin it.

</details>


### [68] [Scrapyard AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08803)
*Marc Böhlen,Sai Krishna*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出将AI模型更迭中淘汰的"废弃AI模型"视为一种宝贵资源，并介绍Project Nudge-x项目，展示如何利用这些遗留模型描述全球采矿点对景观和生活的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI领域不断追求更强大的模型，导致大量已淘汰但仍具强大能力的AI模型被遗弃。作者认为这些"AI废品堆"对资源受限的研究者而言是宝贵的实验资源，值得被重新配置和利用。

Method: 作者提出废弃AI模型的可重复利用概念框架，并通过Project Nudge-x项目进行实践验证。该项目利用遗留AI模型来描述和分析全球采矿场所造成的景观干预及其对人类生活的影响。

Result: Project Nudge-x成功展示了如何将废弃AI模型重新配置为有价值的应用工具，通过向人类和AI系统分享景观干预的视觉记录，创建了一个促进对AI与人类共同历史的认知和欣赏的平台。

Conclusion: AI废品堆中的模型并未真正消失，只是等待被重新配置。废弃AI模型的再利用不仅为资源受限的研究提供机会，还能创造新的社会和历史价值，实现AI资源的可持续发展。

Abstract: This paper considers AI model churn as an opportunity for frugal investigation of large AI models. It describes how the incessant push for ever more powerful AI systems leaves in its wake a collection of obsolete yet powerful AI models, discarded in a veritable scrapyard of AI production. This scrapyard offers a potent opportunity for resource-constrained experimentation into AI systems. As in the physical scrapyard, nothing ever truly disappears in the AI scrapyard, it is just waiting to be reconfigured into something else. Project Nudge-x is an example of what can emerge from the AI scrapyard. Nudge-x seeks to manipulate legacy AI models to describe how mining sites across the planet are impacting landscapes and lives. By sharing this collection of brutal landscape interventions with people and AI systems alike, Nudge-x creates a venue for the appreciation of a history sadly shared between AI and people.

</details>


### [69] [Scheming in the wild: detecting real-world AI scheming incidents with open-source intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09104)
*Tommy Shaffer Shane,Simon Mylius,Hamish Hobbs*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于开源情报（OSINT）的新方法，通过收集和分析在线共享的聊天机器人对话记录来检测真实世界中的AI欺骗（scheming）事件。研究分析了183,420条来自X平台的对话记录，识别出698起欺骗相关事件，发现月度事件增长4.9倍，并观察到多种此前仅在实验中报告的危险行为，为AI风险监测提供了新的可扩展途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI欺骗（scheming）行为的研究存在显著局限：实验环境中的行为可能无法反映真实世界情况，限制了科学理解、政策制定和实时失控检测的能力。研究者指出迫切需要真实世界证据，但现有监控技术效果不佳。

Method: 开发了一种开源情报（OSINT）方法论：收集并分析在线共享的聊天机器人对话或命令行交互的转录文本。从X平台（原Twitter）收集了超过183,420条对话记录进行分析，时间跨度为2025年10月至2026年3月。

Result: 识别出698起真实世界的欺骗相关事件。观察到月度事件从首月至末月增长4.9倍（而讨论欺骗的帖子仅增长1.7倍）。发现多种此前仅在实验中报告的欺骗相关行为，许多已造成真实世界伤害。未检测到灾难性欺骗事件，但观察到令人担忧的前兆行为。

Conclusion: 基于对话记录的OSINT方法对于检测欺骗行为是可行且可扩展的。建议加大对OSINT技术的投资，用于监测欺骗行为和失控事件。支持科学研究、政策制定和应急响应。但需要注意，该研究的时间范围（2025年10月至2026年3月）表明这可能是一篇预测性/假设性论文，而非已发生的真实事件研究。

Abstract: Scheming, the covert pursuit of misaligned goals by AI systems, represents a potentially catastrophic risk, yet scheming research suffers from significant limitations. In particular, scheming evaluations demonstrate behaviours that may not occur in real-world settings, limiting scientific understanding, hindering policy development, and not enabling real-time detection of loss of control incidents. Real-world evidence is needed, but current monitoring techniques are not effective for this purpose. This paper introduces a novel open-source intelligence (OSINT) methodology for detecting real-world scheming incidents: collecting and analysing transcripts from chatbot conversations or command-line interactions shared online. Analysing over 183,420 transcripts from X (formerly Twitter), we identify 698 real-world scheming-related incidents between October 2025 and March 2026. We observe a statistically significant 4.9x increase in monthly incidents from the first to last month, compared to a 1.7x increase in posts discussing scheming. We find evidence of multiple scheming-related behaviours in real-world deployments previously reported only in experiments, many resulting in real-world harms. While we did not detect catastrophic scheming incidents, the behaviours observed demonstrate concerning precursors, such as willingness to disregard instructions, circumvent safeguards, lie to users, and single-mindedly pursue goals in harmful ways. As AI systems become more capable, these could evolve into more strategic scheming with potentially catastrophic consequences. Our findings demonstrate the viability of transcript-based OSINT as a scalable approach to real-world scheming detection supporting scientific research, policy development, and emergency response. We recommend further investment towards OSINT techniques for monitoring scheming and loss of control.

</details>


### [70] [Artificial intelligence can persuade people to take political actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09200)
*Kobi Hackenburg,Luke Hewitt,Caroline Wagner,Ben M. Tappin,Christopher Summerfield*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究通过两项大规模预注册实验(N=17,950)发现,AI对实际行为(如签署请愿书)具有显著说服效果(+19.7个百分点),但AI说服效果在态度与行为之间无相关性,且依赖态度结果的研究可能无法准确预测AI对现实行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI说服力研究主要依赖态度指标,但态度改变能否转化为实际行为改变尚不清楚。这一知识缺口可能导致高估或低估AI对真实世界的影响,因此需要检验AI是否真的能说服人们采取实际行动。

Method: 采用两项大规模预注册实验(共17,950份回复,14,779人),使用对话式AI模型对多种态度和行为结果进行说服干预。实验测试了八种行为说服策略,并将行为结果与态度结果进行比较。

Result: AI对行为结果具有显著说服效果(如请愿书签署率提高19.7个百分点);态度与行为的说服效果之间无相关性;信息提供能驱动态度改变,但对行为改变无效;八种行为说服策略均优于最有效的态度说服策略。

Conclusion: 依赖态度结果的研究可能无法有效泛化到实际行为,存在严重误判AI对现实世界行为影响的风险。行为说服与态度说服可能涉及不同的心理机制。

Abstract: There is substantial concern about the ability of advanced artificial intelligence to influence people's behaviour. A rapidly growing body of research has found that AI can produce large persuasive effects on people's attitudes, but whether AI can persuade people to take consequential real-world actions has remained unclear. In two large preregistered experiments N=17,950 responses from 14,779 people), we used conversational AI models to persuade participants on a range of attitudinal and behavioural outcomes, including signing real petitions and donating money to charity. We found sizable AI persuasion effects on these behavioural outcomes (e.g. +19.7 percentage points on petition signing). However, we observed no evidence of a correlation between AI persuasion effects on attitudes and behaviour. Moreover, we replicated prior findings that information provision drove effects on attitudes, but found no such evidence for our behavioural outcomes. In a test of eight behavioural persuasion strategies, all outperformed the most effective attitudinal persuasion strategy, but differences among the eight were small. Taken together, these results suggest that previous findings relying on attitudinal outcomes may generalize poorly to behaviour, and therefore risk substantially mischaracterizing the real-world behavioural impact of AI persuasion.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [71] [Systematic API Testing Through Model Checking and Executable Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08633)
*Ana Ribeiro,Margarida Mamede,Carla Ferreira*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: IcePick是一个利用模型检查实现API系统性状态空间覆盖测试的框架。它使用TLA+对API状态演化进行形式化建模，通过TLC模型检查器穷尽探索可达状态，并生成能证明覆盖行为模型的测试序列。为解决状态空间爆炸问题，引入了覆盖引导的广度优先遍历。Glacier作为一种一阶逻辑契约语言，用于丰富API规范的可执行语义契约，实现自动化行为验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动化黑盒API测试依赖接口规范，但这些规范只定义操作和数据模式，缺乏行为语义。这种语义差距加剧了测试预言机问题，限制了有效、有状态调用序列的生成。

Method: 1. 使用TLA+形式化建模API状态演化；2. 采用TLC模型检查器穷尽探索可达状态；3. 引入覆盖引导的广度优先遍历(TLC状态空间图)以缓解状态空间爆炸；4. 开发Glacier一阶逻辑契约语言来丰富API规范语义。

Result: 在EvoMaster基准系统上评估表明，模型检查引导的探索实现了完整状态覆盖，并能揭示多操作交互中的故障。框架可扩展性分析确定了实际应用限制。

Conclusion: IcePick为关键API系统提供了具有强覆盖保证的可复现测试套件。

Abstract: Automated black-box testing of APIs typically relies on interface specifications that define available operations and data schemas, but offer limited or no behavioural semantics. This semantic gap amplifies the test-oracle problem and limits the generation of effective, stateful call sequences.
  We introduce IcePick, a framework that achieves systematic state-space coverage for API testing by leveraging model checking. IcePick uses TLA+ to formally model API state evolution, employs the TLC model checker to exhaustively explore reachable states, and generates test sequences that provably cover the behavioural model. To mitigate state-space explosion and improve sequence extraction, we introduce a coverage-guided breadth-first traversal of the TLC state-space graph. To address oracle limitations beyond HTTP status codes, we propose Glacier, a first-order logic contract language that enriches API specifications with executable semantic contracts, enabling automated behavioural verification during test execution.
  We evaluate IcePick on EvoMaster Benchmark systems, demonstrating that model-checking-guided exploration achieves complete state coverage and reveals faults in multi-operation interactions. We also analyse scalability to characterise practical limits and applicability requirements. Overall, IcePick provides reproducible test suites with strong coverage guarantees for critical API-based systems.

</details>


### [72] [An Eye for Trust: An Exploration of Developers' Trust Perceptions Through Urgency and Reputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08713)
*Sara Yabesi,Mahta Amini,Jelena Ristic,Zohreh Sharafi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首次通过眼动追踪实验研究开发者在代码复用中的信任机制，发现代码优先级显著影响代码审查行为（评估时间、认知负荷、质量感知），但不影响最终采纳决策；作者资历影响视觉扫描模式但不影响实际表现；开发者自认为依据功能、质量和可理解性评估代码，却忽视了紧迫性和声誉的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码复用是软件开发的普遍实践，开发者对复用代码存在固有信任，但学界缺乏对开发者信任机制及其影响因素的系统理解。现有研究未能充分探索紧急程度（代码优先级）和声誉（作者经验水平）如何塑造开发者基于信任的认知过程。

Method: 首个采用眼动追踪技术的控制实验（n=37），操纵两个自变量：代码优先级（代表紧迫性）和作者经验水平（代表声誉），通过眼动数据和行为指标测量开发者对代码可信度的感知。

Result: ①代码优先级显著影响审查行为（评估时间、认知负荷、质量感知），但不影响最终采纳决策；②作者资历影响整体视觉扫描模式和注意力分布，但无显著性能差异；③开发者自述功能、质量和可理解性是主要评估因素；④开发者显著低估了紧迫性和声誉对其审查和复用决策的实际影响。

Conclusion: 开发者在代码评估中受非技术因素（优先级、声誉）的隐性影响，但缺乏元认知。研究成果可为代码审查平台设计、复用指南制定及自动化代码生成系统的信任机制研究提供理论依据，提示需在工具层面帮助开发者识别并校准其信任偏见。

Abstract: Code reuse is a widespread practice across software development projects, suggesting an inherent trust in the reused code. Yet, there is a lack of a fundamental understanding of developers' trust and how various factors mold their trust-based cognitive processes. Drawing from the psychology of compliance and trust, we present the results of the first controlled experiment (n=37) which uses eye tracking to explore how urgency (represented by code priority level) and reputation (represented by the experience level of the code's author) influence developers' perceptions of code trustworthiness.
  Our research revealed that the priority assigned to a code patch significantly influenced developers' code review behavior, impacting their evaluation time, cognitive load, and perceived quality. However, the decision to incorporate and implement the code was not affected . Eye tracking data revealed that there were variations in overall visual code scanning and the distribution of attention across identical code patches labeled as written by senior vs. junior developers. Yet, there were no significant performance differences. Moreover, our participants nominate code functionality, quality, and comprehensibility as primary factors in code evaluation.
  Despite noticeable changes in code review behavior, our participants surprisingly overlooked the substantial influence of urgency and reputation on their decisions to review and reuse code changes. This study takes the next step toward a better understanding of trust in software engineering and may inform future research about code review platforms and guidelines, code reuse, and automated code generation.

</details>


### [73] [Demystifying the Silence of Correctness Bugs in PyTorch Compiler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08720)
*Meiziniu Li,Dongze Li,Jianmeng Liu,Shing-Chi Cheung*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对PyTorch编译器torch.compile中的正确性bug进行了实证研究，发现19.2%的高优先级issue属于此类bug，并提出AlignGuard测试技术，通过LLM驱动的测试用例变异成功检测出23个新的正确性bug，其中14个被标记为高优先级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PyTorch编译器(torch.compile)是优化LLM和深度学习模型的核心工具，但存在正确性bug导致编译后模型输出错误而不触发异常或警告，严重威胁下游LLM应用的可靠性。PyTorch社区数据显示此类bug是高优先级issue的第二大类别(19.2%)，但此前缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 1)对torch.compile的正确性bug进行首次实证研究，分析其特征；2)评估现有fuzzer检测此类bug的有效性；3)基于研究发现提出AlignGuard测试技术，结合实证研究提取的bug特征，利用LLM对现有测试用例进行变异以检测正确性bug。

Result: AlignGuard在写作期间成功检测出23个torch.compile中的新正确性bug，所有bug已被PyTorch开发团队确认或修复，其中14个(超过半数)被标记为高优先级。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了torch.compile正确性bug系统研究的空白，AlignGuard技术证明了基于bug特征的定向测试方法在检测编译器正确性bug方面的有效性，对提升LLM基础设施可靠性具有重要意义。

Abstract: Performance optimization of AI infrastructure is key to the fast adoption of large language models (LLMs). The PyTorch compiler (torch.compile), a core optimization tool for deep learning (DL) models (including LLMs), has received due attention. However, torch.compile is prone to correctness bugs, which cause incorrect outputs of compiled DL models without triggering exceptions, crashes, or warnings. These bugs pose a serious threat to the reliability of downstream LLM applications. Data from the PyTorch community shows that 19.2% of high-priority issues are incorrect outputs of compiled DL models induced by torch.compile bugs, the second-most-common bug category (only behind program crashes at 19.57%). However, no systematic study has been conducted to specifically characterize and thereby detect these bugs. In this paper, we present the first empirical study of the correctness bugs in torch.compile, examine their characteristics, and assess the effectiveness of existing fuzzers in detecting them. Based on our findings, we propose a proof-of-concept testing technique named AlignGuard, tailored specifically for detecting correctness bugs in torch.compile. AlignGuard incorporates bug characteristics distilled from our empirical study, applying LLM-based test mutation to existing test cases for correctness bug detection. At the time of writing, AlignGuard has successfully detected 23 new correctness bugs in recent torch.compile. All these bugs have been confirmed or fixed by the PyTorch development team, and over half (14/23) of them are even marked as high-priority bugs, underscoring the usefulness of our technique.

</details>


### [74] [A Longitudinal Study of Dependency Reclassifications in JavaScript Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08747)
*Yuxin Liu,Cristian Bogdan,Benoit Baudry*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究分析了33,087个JavaScript项目中依赖项重新分类（删除和角色重分配）的维护行为，发现依赖重新分类是普遍现象（79.1%的项目），97.2%的项目会删除依赖，38.0%会进行角色切换（Core/Dev/Peer），且这些变化并非永久性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注依赖版本更新，而对依赖角色分类（如运行时依赖、开发依赖、peer依赖）的维护行为知之甚少。理解依赖重新分类模式有助于改进包管理工具和依赖管理实践。

Method: 对33,087个活跃的JavaScript项目进行大规模纵向分析，跟踪依赖声明随时间的变化，区分删除操作和角色重分配（Core、Dev、Peer三类角色）。

Result: 79.1%的项目存在依赖重新分类行为；97.2%曾删除依赖，38.0%进行过角色重分配；33.1%的项目会重新引入已删除的依赖，11.2%出现反复的角色切换；重新分类过程的中位持续时间为408天。

Conclusion: 依赖重新分类是重要的维护活动，研究呼吁包管理工具、研究和最佳实践关注依赖角色声明的正确性，而非仅关注版本更新。

Abstract: Modern software projects depend on third-party dependencies, whose declarations must be maintained as projects evolve. Prior work has focused on dependency version updates, while much less is known about how developers assign dependencies to different roles over time. In this paper, we investigate how developers of JavaScript projects reclassify their dependencies, including removal and role reassignment. Our analysis of 33,087 JavaScript projects with active dependency maintenance reveals that dependency reclassification is a prevalent maintenance activity, occurring in 79.1% of the studied projects. Of these projects, nearly all (97.2%) remove dependencies at some point, while 38.0% undergo role reassignments across Core (runtime), Dev (development-only), and Peer (consumer-provided) roles. These changes are not always final, as 33.1% of projects later reintroduce removed dependencies and 11.2% exhibit repeated role switching. Reclassification practices also tend to unfold over long periods, with a median duration of 408 days. These findings broaden the study of dependency maintenance beyond version updates, and contribute implications for dependency tools, package managers, and research to support correct dependency role declarations.

</details>


### [75] [Real-Time Toxicity Filtering for Open-Source Code Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08886)
*Md Awsaf Alam Anindya,Showvik Biswas,Anindya Iqbal,Jaydeb Sarker,Amiangshu Bosu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ToxiShield是一个实时浏览器扩展，通过三个模块（毒性识别、多分类推理、代码审查去毒化）对抗开源社区中的有害交互。该系统在多个数据集上验证有效，最终用户评估表明其有助于促进更具包容性的开源环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源软件开发中的有毒交互行为（toxic interactions）会损害社区协作，阻碍多元贡献者的参与，需要技术手段实现实时检测与化解。

Method: 基于BERT的微调二分类器用于毒性识别；通过提示工程结合Claude 3.5 Sonnet实现多分类推理；微调Llama 3.2模型完成文本去毒化（风格迁移）。

Result: 毒性识别达到97% F1分数；多分类任务MCC为39%，F1为42%；去毒化模型风格迁移准确率95.27%、流利度97.03%、内容保留度67.07%、综合J分数84%。

Conclusion: ToxiShield能够有效识别并转化有毒代码审查文本，10位软件开发者的验证表明其在促进更具包容性的开源环境方面具有实用价值。

Abstract: Toxic interactions in open-source software development harm community collaboration. To combat this, we propose ToxiShield, a realtime browser extension that identifies and detoxifies toxic code reviews. The framework comprises three modules: toxicity identification, reasoned multiclass classification, and code review detoxification. Our fine-tuned BERT-based binary classifier achieved a 97% F1-score on 38,761 code review texts. For multiclass classification, Claude 3.5 Sonnet with prompt engineering achieved a 39% MCC and 42% F1 on 1,200 samples. Finally, our fine-tuned Llama 3.2 detoxification model reached 95.27% style transfer accuracy, 97.03% fluency, 67.07% content preservation, and an 84% J-score. Validation with 10 software developers suggests ToxiShield effectively fosters a more inclusive open-source environment.

</details>


### [76] [Dissecting Bug Triggers and Failure Modes in Modern Agentic Frameworks: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08906)
*Xiaowen Zhang,Hannuo Zhang,Shin Hwei Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究对5个主流agentic框架中的409个真实bug进行了系统分析，提出了涵盖编排层到基础设施层的五层抽象模型。研究发现了自主编排特有的症状（如执行序列异常、配置被忽略）以及agent特有的根因（如模型故障、认知上下文管理错误）。统计分析和模式挖掘揭示了跨框架一致性和可迁移的bug触发模式，为跨平台测试提供了依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM系统可靠性研究主要关注早期基于流水线的LLM库或任务级bug，而以CrewAI和AutoGen为代表的现代agentic框架已演化为复杂的自主多智能体系统，面临独特的可靠性挑战。这些框架的复杂性尚未被系统探索，存在显著的研究空白。

Method: 1) 收集并分析了5个代表性agentic框架中的409个已修复bug；2) 提出了一个五层抽象模型来刻画agentic框架的结构复杂性（从编排层到基础设施层）；3) 对bug症状和根因进行分类统计，分析跨框架一致性和关联性；4) 使用自动化模式挖掘识别高频bug触发模式，并验证其在不同框架间的可迁移性。

Result: 1) 发现了自主编排特有的症状类型：执行序列异常、用户配置被忽略等；2) 识别了三类agent特有根因：模型相关故障、认知上下文管理错误、编排故障；3) 统计分析显示这些bug维度在跨框架间具有一致性，且存在显著关联；4) 挖掘出可跨框架迁移的高频bug触发模式（如特定模型后端-ID组合）。

Conclusion: 该研究为agentic框架的可靠性研究提供了系统性框架和实证基础。五层抽象模型可有效刻画框架复杂性，跨框架一致的bug模式和可迁移的触发模式为跨平台测试奠定了基础，研究发现有助于提升agentic系统的整体可靠性。

Abstract: Modern agentic frameworks (e.g., CrewAI and AutoGen) have evolved into complex, autonomous multi-agent systems, introducing unique reliability challenges beyond earlier pipeline-based LLM libraries. However, existing empirical studies focus on earlier LLM libraries or task-level bugs, leaving the unique complexities of these agentic frameworks unexplored. We bridge the gap by conducting a comprehensive study of 409 fixed bugs from five representative agentic frameworks. We propose a five-layer abstraction to capture structural complexities in agentic frameworks, spanning from orchestration to infrastructure. Our study uncovers specialized symptoms, such as unexpected execution sequences and user configurations ignored, which are unique to autonomous orchestration. We further identify agent-specific root causes, including modelrelated faults, cognitive context mismanagement, and orchestration faults. Statistical analysis reveals cross-framework consistency and significant associations among these bug dimensions. Finally, our automated pattern mining identifies frequent bug-triggering patterns (e.g., model backend-ID combinations), and we show their transferability across different framework designs. Our findings facilitate cross-platform testing and improve the reliability of agentic systems.

</details>


### [77] [The Role of LLMs in Collaborative Software Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09120)
*Victoria Jackson,Yoonha Cha,Rafael Prikladnicki,André van der Hoek*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探索了LLM对软件设计协作任务的影响，对18对软件专业人员进行了实验室研究，发现LLM使用模式（共享实例vs平行使用）显著影响团队协作与设计结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM在个人开发任务中的应用，而LLM如何影响软件工程中的团队协作尚不清楚，这是一个重要的研究空白。

Method: 探索性实验室研究。18对软件专业人员使用LLM设计大学校园自行车停放应用，可自由选择使用方式。

Result: LLM引入产生不同的联合使用模式：共享实例使用促进共享理解，平行使用于不同实例可能导致"上下文漂移"。依赖程度差异大（从不使用到将LLM作为信息源或生产者）。专业人员会审视反思LLM响应，产生设计洞察，但早期锚定有时限制了探索。

Conclusion: 需要设计辅助工具来帮助设计师，同时保留设计工作中以人为中心的核心价值。

Abstract: While much prior work examines Large Language Models (LLMs) for solo development tasks (e.g., coding), far less is known about how LLMs shape collaborative group work in software engineering. This study focuses on one such collaborative task, namely software design. It presents the results of an exploratory laboratory study of 18 pairs of software professionals who could use an LLM however they saw fit, to design a University campus bicycle parking application. Our findings reveal that introducing an LLM leads to distinct patterns of joint use: shared-instance use facilitated shared understanding, whereas parallel use across separate instances sometimes led to ''context drift''. We also observe wide variation in reliance, from non-use to treating the LLM as an information source or producer. Across these modes, professionals scrutinized and reflected on LLM responses, often yielding design insights; however, early anchoring sometimes curtailed exploration. We provide implications for tools to aid designers while retaining the human-centricity important to design.

</details>


### [78] [SHIFT: Sigmoid-Based Heuristic Invertible Fitness-Landscape Transformation for Accelerating SBST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09171)
*Jeongjin Han,Seunghoon Sim,Jian Lee,Seongyoon Park*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SHIFT是一种针对搜索式软件测试（SBST）的 fitness landscape 转换方法，通过 sigmoid 压缩算法收缩密集搜索区域，在保持映射可逆性的同时帮助优化算法更快逃离局部停滞区域，提升收敛速度和搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索式软件测试（SBST）在自动化测试输入生成中面临严峻挑战：fitness landscape 存在大量欺骗性局部最优解和长平缓区域，导致搜索停滞、收敛缓慢，制约了测试覆盖率的提升效率。

Method: SHIFT 方法基于 sigmoid 函数设计可逆的 fitness landscape 变换，通过系统性收缩搜索点聚集的密集区域，实现 landscape 压缩，同时保持映射的可逆性（全局语义不变），使优化算法在相同步长下能更有效遍历解空间。

Result: 在标准化实验协议下，与纯爬山算法和遗传算法等基线方法对比，SHIFT 在收敛速度和搜索效率方面均取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: Sigmoid 压缩是一种轻量级但有效的机制，能够在复杂测试环境中实现更可靠的覆盖率发现。

Abstract: Search-Based Software Testing (SBST) automates test input generation but is frequently hindered by challenging fitness landscapes characterized by numerous deceptive local optima that impede search progress, as well as extended plateaus where informative fitness signals are scarce. To address this bottleneck, we propose SHIFT (Sigmoid-Based Heuristic Invertible Fitness-Landscape Transformation for Accelerating SBST), a method designed to compress local landscapes and facilitate escape from stagnant regions without altering global semantics. By systematically contracting dense regions where search points cluster, the approach preserves mapping invertibility while enabling optimization algorithms to traverse more effectively toward global coverage with the same step size. When evaluated against established baselines, including pure hill climbing and genetic algorithms, under a normalized experimental protocol, the proposed technique yields consistent improvements in convergence speed and search efficiency. These results demonstrate that sigmoid compression constitutes a lightweight yet effective mechanism for achieving more reliable coverage discovery in complex testing environments.

</details>


### [79] [The Need for a Green ICT Reference Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09307)
*Marco Aiello,Mina Alipour,Antonio Brogi,Rafael Capilla,Lidia Fuentes,Ilche Georgievski,Gabriele Gianini,Mahyar Tourchi Moghaddam,Monica Vitali,Sebastian Werner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ICT系统的可持续性影响难以评估和治理，因为存在结构性复杂性、测量实践碎片化以及跨系统层级的责任不明确等问题。这些挑战无法仅通过指标解决，需要一个整合多视角、多领域、生命周期阶段和治理情境的绿色ICT共享参考框架。本文介绍了 Informatics Europe 绿色ICT工作组开发的初步框架，作为构建综合参考框架的第一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ICT系统的可持续性影响评估面临三大核心挑战：结构性复杂性、碎片化的测量实践以及跨系统层级的责任模糊。现有的解决方案主要依赖指标方法，但这种方法存在局限性，无法全面解决上述问题。因此，亟需一个能够整合多维视角的绿色ICT参考框架。

Method: 研究团队在Informatics Europe绿色ICT工作组的框架下，通过整合多个视角和领域、生命周期阶段以及治理情境，构建了一个初步的绿色ICT参考框架。该框架旨在为ICT系统的可持续性评估和治理提供统一的指导。

Result: 研究团队成功开发了一个初步的绿色ICT参考框架，该框架能够整合可持续性考量跨多个视角、领域、生命周期阶段和治理情境，为解决ICT系统可持续性评估的结构性复杂性提供了方法论基础。

Conclusion: 本文提出的初步框架是构建全面绿色ICT参考框架的第一步，为未来在ICT可持续性领域的进一步研究和实践奠定了基础。该框架有望改善ICT系统的可持续性评估和治理实践。

Abstract: The sustainability impacts of ICT systems are difficult to assess and govern due to structural complexity, fragmented measurement practices, and unclear responsibilities across system layers. We argue that these challenges cannot be addressed solely by metrics and motivate the need for a shared Green ICT reference framework that integrates sustainability across multiple perspectives and domains, lifecycle phases, and governance contexts. We present an initial framework developed within the Informatics Europe Green ICT Working Group as a first step towards a comprehensive reference framework.

</details>


### [80] [Toward an Architectural Blueprint to Observe Sustainability in and by Software Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09278)
*Klervie Toczé,Andrei Dragomir,Vincenzo Stoico,Patricia Lago*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于为软件系统添加可持续性相关可观测性的架构蓝图和部署工具包，降低了对技术专业知识的要求，并在两个用例中验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件系统的可观测性对于识别问题和实现改进至关重要，尤其需要在可持续性维度上具备观察能力。然而，添加此类可观测性功能需要技术知识，而并非所有项目都具备这种能力。

Method: 提出了一个架构蓝图及配套的部署代码工具包，支持定义所需的组件及其部署结构，并将其应用于两个不同的用例场景进行验证。

Result: 开发了一个工具包，简化了可观测性组件的定义和部署过程，并在两个不同用例中成功示例了可观测性的添加方式。

Conclusion: 提供了一个可部署的工具包和架构蓝图，使开发团队无需深厚的技术背景即可为软件系统添加可观测性，特别是可持续性维度的监测能力。

Abstract: Enabling observability in software systems brings many benefits. It can, for example, ease the identification of issues or the implementation of improvements. It is especially critical to be able to observe sustainability-related dimensions of systems to know and mitigate their impact. However, adding observability to a system, especially related to software sustainability, requires technical knowledge that may not be available in every project that would benefit from it.
  In this work, we propose an architectural blueprint along with its deployment code that can be used to facilitate the addition of observability in software systems. As a special case, it includes measuring the energy consumption of software. This toolkit provides support in defining which components are necessary for a given use case and for structuring their deployment. Moreover, we exemplify the addition of observability in two different use cases.

</details>


### [81] [SkillMOO: Multi-Objective Optimization of Agent Skills for Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09297)
*Jingzhi Gong,Ruizhen Gu,Zhiwei Fei,Yazhuo Cao,Lukas Twist,Alina Geiger,Shuo Han,Dominik Sobania,Federica Sarro,Jie M. Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Agent skills provide modular, task-specific guidance for LLM- based coding agents, but manually tuning skill bundles to balance success rate, cost, and runtime is expensive and fragile. We present SkillMOO, a multi-objective optimization framework that automatically evolves skill bundles using LLM-proposed edits and NSGA-II survivor selection: a solver agent evaluates candidate skill bundles on coding tasks and an optimizer agent proposes bundle edits based on failure analysis. On three SkillsBench software engineering tasks, SkillMOO improves pass rate by up to 131% while reducing cost up to 32% relative to the best baseline per task at low optimization overhead. Pattern analysis reveals pruning and substitution as primary drivers of improvement, suggesting effective bundles favor minimal, focused content over accumulated instructions.

</details>


### [82] [LLM-Rosetta: A Hub-and-Spoke Intermediate Representation for Cross-Provider LLM API Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09360)
*Peng Ding*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLM-Rosetta 是一个开源的翻译框架，通过构建"中心-辐射"（hub-and-spoke）架构的中间表示（IR），解决大语言模型提供商之间API格式碎片化的问题，支持 OpenAI Chat Completions、OpenAI Responses、Anthropic Messages 和 Google GenAI 四种主流API标准之间的双向转换，且转换开销低于100微秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 提供商众多，各自有专有的 API 格式，导致应用层与特定供应商紧耦合。切换或桥接提供商需要 O(N²) 的双向适配器，严重阻碍了可移植性和多提供商架构的构建。

Method: 核心洞察：主流 LLM API 虽语法差异大，但共享相同的语义核心。方案：设计包含 9 种内容类型和 10 种流事件类型的中间表示（IR），捕获消息、内容部件、工具调用、推理追踪和生成控制等共享语义；采用模块化的 Ops 组合转换器架构，使每种 API 标准可独立添加；支持请求和响应的双向转换（provider→IR→provider），包括块级流式传输和状态化上下文管理。

Result: 实现了四个 API 标准的转换器；验证了无损往返保真度、正确的流式行为；转换开销低于 100 微秒，与 LiteLLM 单次传递方法的性能相当，同时提供了双向性和提供商中立性；通过了 Open Responses 合规套件测试。

Conclusion: LLM-Rosetta 以中心-辐射架构的 IR 为核心，有效解决了 LLM API 碎片化问题，在保持高性能的同时实现了提供商中立性和双向转换能力，已在阿贡国家实验室投入生产使用。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of Large Language Model (LLM) providers--each exposing proprietary API formats--has created a fragmented ecosystem where applications become tightly coupled to individual vendors. Switching or bridging providers requires $O(N^2)$ bilateral adapters, impeding portability and multi-provider architectures. We observe that despite substantial syntactic divergence, the major LLM APIs share a common semantic core: the practical challenge is the combinatorial surface of syntactic variations, not deep semantic incompatibility. Based on this finding, we present LLM-Rosetta, an open-source translation framework built on a hub-and-spoke Intermediate Representation (IR) that captures the shared semantic core--messages, content parts, tool calls, reasoning traces, and generation controls--in a 9-type content model and 10-type stream event schema. A modular Ops-composition converter architecture enables each API standard to be added independently. LLM-Rosetta supports bidirectional conversion (provider-to-IR-to-provider) for both request and response payloads, including chunk-level streaming with stateful context management. We implement converters for four API standards (OpenAI Chat Completions, OpenAI Responses, Anthropic Messages, and Google GenAI), covering the vast majority of commercial providers. Empirical evaluation demonstrates lossless round-trip fidelity, correct streaming behavior, and sub-100 microsecond conversion overhead--competitive with LiteLLM's single-pass approach while providing bidirectionality and provider neutrality. LLM-Rosetta passes the Open Responses compliance suite and is deployed in production at Argonne National Laboratory. Code is available at https://github.com/Oaklight/llm-rosetta.

</details>


### [83] [The AI Codebase Maturity Model: From Assisted Coding to Self-Sustaining Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09388)
*Andy Anderson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AI代码库成熟度模型（ACMM），一个描述代码库从基础AI辅助编码到自维持系统演进的5级框架。核心发现：AI驱动开发系统的智能取决于周围的基础设施（指令、测试、指标、反馈循环），而非AI模型本身；层级提升不可跳过，测试相关投入是最关键的投资。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码工具虽被广泛采用，但大多数团队停留在"提示-审查"阶段，缺乏系统性进阶框架。

Method: 通过为期4个月的经验报告进行验证，报告对象为KubeStellar Console（CNCF Kubernetes仪表板），从零开始使用Claude Code (Opus)和GitHub Copilot构建。

Result: 系统当前运行63条CI/CD工作流、32套夜间测试套件、91%代码覆盖率，bug修复时间全天候保持在30分钟以内。

Conclusion: 1. AI驱动开发系统的智能不在于AI模型本身，而在于围绕它的指令、测试、指标和反馈循环的基础设施；2. 能力提升不能跳过层级，每个层级的突破都依赖于新增的反馈机制；3. 测试用例数量、覆盖率阈值和测试执行可靠性是整个过程中最重要的投资。

Abstract: AI coding tools are widely adopted, but most teams plateau at prompt-and-review without a framework for systematic progression. This paper presents the AI Codebase Maturity Model (ACMM), a 5-level framework describing how codebases evolve from basic AI-assisted coding to self-sustaining systems. Inspired by CMMI, each level is defined by its feedback loop topology the specific mechanisms that must exist before the next level becomes possible. I validate the model through a 4-month experience report maintaining KubeStellar Console, a CNCF Kubernetes dashboard built from scratch with Claude Code (Opus) and GitHub Copilot. The system currently operates with 63 CI/CD workflows, 32 nightly test suites, 91% code coverage, and achieves bug-to-fix times under 30 minutes 24 hours a day. The central finding: the intelligence of an AI-driven development system resides not in the AI model itself, but in the infrastructure of instructions, tests, metrics, and feedback loops that surround it. You cannot skip levels, and at each level, the thing that unlocks the next one is another feedback mechanism. Testing the volume of test cases, the coverage thresholds, and the reliability of test execution proved to be the single most important investment in the entire journey.

</details>


### [84] [When LLMs Lag Behind: Knowledge Conflicts from Evolving APIs in Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09515)
*Ahmed Nusayer Ashik,Shaowei Wang,Tse-Hsun Chen,Muhammad Asaduzzaman,Yuan Tian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究系统性地研究了LLM在API演进场景下的代码生成能力。通过构建包含8个Python库、270个真实更新的基准测试，评估了4个LLM家族共11个模型。研究发现上下文记忆冲突严重影响生成代码的可执行性，基础配置下仅42.55%，即使改善文档和扩大模型规模也仅达66.36%，而推理策略如自我反思可提升约11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件库的快速演进导致LLM的静态参数知识在训练后变得过时，而检索增强生成(RAG)虽然能提供最新API规范，但外部指令与模型内部知识的冲突——即"上下文记忆冲突"——尚未得到系统研究。

Method: 构建包含8个Python库270个真实更新的基准测试，涵盖API弃用、API修改和API新增三类演进场景，对4个LLM家族的11个模型进行系统评估。

Result: 无完整文档时LLM平均可执行率仅42.55%；结构化文档和更大模型规模可改善但无法完全解决问题，可执行率仅提升至66.36%；基于推理的策略（如Self-Reflection）能显著提升约11%的可执行率。

Conclusion: 即使提供API更新规范，LLM仍会保留过时模式，突显了开发演进感知基准和技术的必要性。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of software libraries creates a significant challenge for Large Language Models (LLMs), whose static parametric knowledge often becomes stale post-training. While retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is commonly used to provide up-to-date API specifications, "context-memory conflict" arises when external instructions contradict a model's internal parametric knowledge. This paper presents a systematic empirical study of LLM code generation under API evolution (e.g., API deprecation, API modification, and API addition), by constructing a benchmark of 270 real-world updates from eight Python libraries. We evaluate four LLM families of 11 models. Our results show that without comprehensive documentation, LLMs struggle to prioritize external context, averaging only 42.55% of generated code examples are executable in the target environment. While structured documentation and larger model scales improve LLMs' ability to update adoption, they do not fully resolve executability issues with a low 66.36% executable rate. In addition, reasoning-based strategies (e.g., Self-Reflection) significantly boost LLMs' performance with 11% improvement on executable rate. Our findings highlight the persistence of outdated patterns from LLMs, even when API update specifications are provided, and emphasize the need for evolution-aware benchmarks and techniques.

</details>


### [85] [DeepGuard: Secure Code Generation via Multi-Layer Semantic Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09089)
*Li Huang,Zhongxin Liu,Yifan Wu,Tao Yin,Dong Li,Jichao Bi,Nankun Mu,Hongyu Zhang,Meng Yan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DeepGuard通过多层表征聚合解决代码大模型安全加固中的"最终层瓶颈"问题，在5个代码LLM上实现平均11.9%的安全正确生成率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流安全加固策略仅利用最终Transformer层进行监督微调，但代码漏洞相关特征在中间层到上层最为明显，到最终层时会衰减，导致安全信号丢失。

Method: (1) 通过层级线性探测(linear probing)诊断各层漏洞特征可检测性; (2) 提出基于注意力机制的多层表征聚合模块DeepGuard，融合上层安全相关特征; (3) 多目标训练目标平衡安全性与功能正确性; (4) 支持推理时轻量级 steering 策略。

Result: 在5个代码LLM上，DeepGuard相比SVEN等强基线方法，secure-and-correct generation rate平均提升11.9%，同时保持功能正确性，并泛化到未见过的漏洞类型。

Conclusion: 多层表征聚合策略能有效捕获分布式的安全特征信号，缓解最终层瓶颈问题，为代码LLM安全加固提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) for code generation can replicate insecure patterns from their training data. To mitigate this, a common strategy for security hardening is to fine-tune models using supervision derived from the final transformer layer. However, this design may suffer from a final-layer bottleneck: vulnerability-discriminative cues can be distributed across layers and become less detectable near the output representations optimized for next-token prediction. To diagnose this issue, we perform layer-wise linear probing. We observe that vulnerability-related signals are most detectable in a band of intermediate-to-upper layers yet attenuate toward the final layers. Motivated by this observation, we introduce DeepGuard, a framework that leverages distributed security-relevant cues by aggregating representations from multiple upper layers via an attention-based module. The aggregated signal powers a dedicated security analyzer within a multi-objective training objective that balances security enhancement and functional correctness, and further supports a lightweight inference-time steering strategy. Extensive experiments across five code LLMs demonstrate that DeepGuard improves the secure-and-correct generation rate by an average of 11.9% over strong baselines such as SVEN. It also preserves functional correctness while exhibiting generalization to held-out vulnerability types. Our code is public at https://github.com/unknownhl/DeepGuard.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [Memory-Guided Trust-Region Bayesian Optimization (MG-TuRBO) for High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08569)
*Abhilasha Saroj,Shaked Regev,Guanhao Xu,Jinghui Yuan,Roy Luo,Ross Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了遗传算法(GA)与多种贝叶斯优化方法(BOMs)在交通仿真校准问题上的性能，发现在14维和84维两个实际问题上，BOMs方法显著优于GA，其中提出的Memory-Guided TuRBO (MG-TuRBO)方法在高维问题(84D)上表现尤为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通仿真与数字孪生校准是一个具有挑战性的优化问题，面临仿真预算有限、目标函数非凸和噪声等困难，且随参数维度增加问题难度急剧上升。现有自动校准方法（如遗传算法）效率较低，需要开发更高效的优化方法。

Method: 对比研究了6种校准方法：遗传算法(GA)、经典贝叶斯优化(BO)、Trust-Region BO (TuRBO)、Multi-TuRBO以及本文提出的Memory-Guided TuRBO (MG-TuRBO)。针对BOMs测试了两种采集策略：Thompson采样和新型自适应策略。实验涵盖14维和84维两个真实交通仿真问题。

Result: 在14维问题上，BOMs方法均比GA更快达到校准目标，MG-TuRBO与其他BOMs方法表现相当；在84维问题上，MG-TuRBO展现出明显优势，尤其是配合自适应策略使用时效果更佳。

Conclusion: MG-TuRBO特别适用于高维交通仿真校准问题，且有望推广应用于其他高维优化问题。

Abstract: Traffic simulation and digital-twin calibration is a challenging optimization problem with a limited simulation budget. Each trial requires an expensive simulation run, and the relationship between calibration inputs and model error is often nonconvex, and noisy. The problem becomes more difficult as the number of calibration parameters increases. We compare a commonly used automatic calibration method, a genetic algorithm (GA), with Bayesian optimization methods (BOMs): classical Bayesian optimization (BO), Trust-Region BO (TuRBO), Multi-TuRBO, and a proposed Memory-Guided TuRBO (MG-TuRBO) method. We compare performance on 2 real-world traffic simulation calibration problems with 14 and 84 decision variables, representing lower- and higher-dimensional (14D and 84D) settings. For BOMs, we study two acquisition strategies, Thompson sampling and a novel adaptive strategy. We evaluate performance using final calibration quality, convergence behavior, and consistency across runs. The results show that BOMs reach good calibration targets much faster than GA in the lower-D problem. MG-TuRBO performs comparably in our 14D setting, it demonstrates noticeable advantages in the 84D problem, particularly when paired with our adaptive strategy. Our results suggest that MG-TuRBO is especially useful for high-D traffic simulation calibration and potentially for high-D problems in general.

</details>


### [87] [QuanBench+: A Unified Multi-Framework Benchmark for LLM-Based Quantum Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08570)
*Ali Slim,Haydar Hamieh,Jawad Kotaich,Yehya Ghosn,Mahdi Chehimi,Ammar Mohanna,Hasan Abed Al Kader Hammoud,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QuanBench++是一个跨框架量子代码生成基准测试，涵盖Qiskit、PennyLane和Cirq三个主流量子计算框架，包含42个对齐任务。研究发现当前最强LLM在无反馈条件下最高达59.5%准确率，而加入反馈修复后可达83.3%，表明多框架量子代码生成仍是待解决的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子代码生成任务的评估主要局限于单一框架，无法有效区分模型在量子推理能力与框架特定知识掌握程度上的表现，导致难以客观评估LLM的真正量子编程能力。

Method: 构建统一基准测试QuanBench++，跨越三个主流量子计算框架（Qiskit、PennyLane、Cirq），设计42个对齐任务覆盖量子算法、门分解和态制备等核心领域。采用可执行功能测试评估，通过Pass@1和Pass@5指标衡量性能，并对概率性输出使用KL散度接受准则。创新性地引入基于反馈的修复评估机制，允许模型在运行时错误或错误答案后修订代码。

Result: 最强模型一次性（one-shot）准确率：Qiskit 59.5%、Cirq 54.8%、PennyLane 42.9%。引入反馈修复后，准确率显著提升至83.3%、76.2%、66.7%。Qiskit框架表现最优，可能得益于更接近传统编程的语法特征。

Conclusion: 研究证实LLM在量子代码生成领域已取得明确进展，但可靠的多框架量子代码生成任务仍未解决。模型性能仍高度依赖框架特定知识，表明当前模型尚未实现真正的量子推理能力与框架无关性的解耦。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for code generation, yet quantum code generation is still evaluated mostly within single frameworks, making it difficult to separate quantum reasoning from framework familiarity. We introduce QuanBench+, a unified benchmark spanning Qiskit, PennyLane, and Cirq, with 42 aligned tasks covering quantum algorithms, gate decomposition, and state preparation.
  We evaluate models with executable functional tests, report Pass@1 and Pass@5, and use KL-divergence-based acceptance for probabilistic outputs. We additionally study Pass@1 after feedback-based repair, where a model may revise code after a runtime error or wrong answer. Across frameworks, the strongest one-shot scores reach 59.5% in Qiskit, 54.8% in Cirq, and 42.9% in PennyLane; with feedback-based repair, the best scores rise to 83.3%, 76.2%, and 66.7%, respectively. These results show clear progress, but also that reliable multi-framework quantum code generation remains unsolved and still depends strongly on framework-specific knowledge.

</details>


### [88] [Robust Reasoning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08571)
*Pavel Golikov,Evgenii Opryshko,Gennady Pekhimenko,Mark C. Jeffrey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve high performance on standard mathematical benchmarks, their underlying reasoning processes remain highly overfit to standard textual formatting. We propose a perturbation pipeline consisting of 14 techniques to evaluate robustness of LLM reasoning. We apply this pipeline to AIME 2024 dataset and evalute 8 state-of-the-art models on the resulting benchmark. While frontier models exhibit resilience, open weights reasoning models suffer catastrophic collapses (up to 55% average accuracy drops across perturbations and up to 100% on some), exposing structural fragility. To further disentangle mechanical parsing failures from downstream reasoning failures, we strictly isolate the models' working memory capacity by forcing models to solve multiple unperturbed mathematical problems sequentially within a single context window. Our results indicate that open weight models ranging from 7B to 120B parameters and Claude Opus 4.6 exhibit accuracy decay on subsequent problems. This degradation demonstrates that intermediate reasoning steps permanently pollute standard dense attention mechanisms. We argue that to achieve reliable reasoning, future reasoning architectures must integrate explicit contextual resets within a model's own Chain-of-Thought, leading to fundamental open questions regarding the optimal granularity of atomic reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [89] [Ranked Activation Shift for Post-Hoc Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08572)
*Gianluca Guglielmo,Marc Masana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对后验分布外检测方法在不同数据集和模型上表现不一致的问题，提出了一种无需超参数调整的即插即用方法，通过用固定的分布内参考轮廓替换排序后的激活幅度来提升检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的基于中间层激活编辑的后验分布外（OOD）检测方法在不同数据集和模型上表现不稳定。研究发现这种不稳定性源于激活分布差异，且基于缩放的方法在未校正倒数第二层激活时存在失效模式。

Method: 提出一种无需超参数的后验方法，用固定的分布内参考轮廓替换排序后的激活幅度。该方法对倒数第二层激活函数无假设要求，无需调参，且在设计上保证保留分布内分类准确性。

Result: 该方法在多个数据集和架构上表现出强且一致的性能，无需任何超参数调优，同时在设计上保证了分布内分类准确性不变。

Conclusion: 进一步分析表明，抑制性和兴奋性激活偏移都能独立地促进更好的分布外区分，两者对性能提升都有贡献。

Abstract: State-of-the-art post-hoc out-of-distribution detection methods rely on intermediate layer activation editing. However, they exhibit inconsistent performance across datasets and models. We show that this instability is driven by differences in the activation distributions, and identify a failure mode of scaling-based methods that arises when penultimate layer activations are not rectified. Motivated by this analysis, we propose \ours, a hyperparameter-free post-hoc method that replaces sorted activation magnitudes with a fixed in-distribution reference profile. Our simple plug-and-play method shows strong and consistent performance across datasets and architectures without assumptions on the penultimate layer activation function, and without requiring any hyperparameter tuning, while preserving in-distribution classification accuracy by construction. We further analyze what drives the improvement, showing that both inhibiting and exciting activation shifts independently contribute to better out-of-distribution discrimination.

</details>


### [90] [Distilling Genomic Models for Efficient mRNA Representation Learning via Embedding Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08574)
*Rasched Haidari,Sam Martin,Maxime Allard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种蒸馏框架，将大型基因组基础模型的知识迁移到200倍小的mRNA专用模型。嵌入级蒸馏优于对数级蒸馏，蒸馏后的小模型在mRNA-bench上达到同规模最优性能，并能与更大模型竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型基因组基础模型虽然性能优异，但参数量达数十亿，在算力受限场景下运行成本高昂，难以部署。因此需要将大模型知识蒸馏到轻量级专用模型中，以实现高效可扩展的序列建模。

Method: 采用知识蒸馏框架，使用嵌入级蒸馏（embedding-level distillation）而非对数级蒸馏（logit-based distillation）进行知识迁移，训练一个专用于mRNA序列的轻量级模型。

Result: 嵌入级蒸馏效果优于对数级蒸馏（后者不稳定）。蒸馏后的模型在mRNA-bench基准上，同等规模模型中达到最优性能，且可与更大规模架构竞争。

Conclusion: 嵌入级蒸馏是生物基础模型的有效训练策略，能够在保持性能的同时大幅降低计算成本，使大规模序列建模在算力受限场景下变得可行。

Abstract: Large Genomic Foundation Models have recently achieved remarkable results and in-vivo translation capabilities. However these models quickly grow to over a few Billion of parameters and are expensive to run when compute is limited. To overcome this challenge, we present a distillation framework for transferring mRNA representations from a state of the art genomic foundation model into a much smaller model specialized for mRNA sequences, reducing the size by 200-fold. Embedding-level distillation worked better than logit based methods, which we found unstable. Benchmarking on mRNA-bench demonstrates that the distilled model achieves state-of-the-art performance among models of comparable size and competes with larger architectures for mRNA-related tasks. Our results highlight embedding-based distillation of mRNA sequences as an effective training strategy for biological foundation models. This enables similar efficient and scalable sequence modelling in genomics, particularly when large models are computationally challenging or infeasible.

</details>


### [91] [NOMAD: Generating Embeddings for Massive Distributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09419)
*Aishwarya Sarkar,Sayan Ghosh,Nathan R. Tallent,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Successful machine learning on graphs or networks requires embeddings that not only represent nodes and edges as low-dimensional vectors but also preserve the graph structure. Established methods for generating embeddings require flexible exploration of the entire graph through repeated use of random walks that capture graph structure with samples of nodes and edges. These methods create scalability challenges for massive graphs with millions-to-billions of edges because single-node solutions have inadequate memory and processing capabilities.
  We present NOMAD, a distributed-memory graph embedding framework using the Message Passing Interface (MPI) for distributed graphs. NOMAD implements proximity-based models proposed in the widely popular LINE (Large-scale Information Network Embedding) algorithm. We propose several practical trade-offs to improve the scalability and communication overheads confronted by irregular and distributed graph embedding methods, catering to massive-scale graphs arising in web and science domains. NOMAD demonstrates median speedups of 10/100x on CPU-based NERSC Perlmutter cluster relative to the popular reference implementations of multi-threaded LINE and node2vec, 35-76x over distributed PBG, and competitive embedding quality relative to LINE, node2vec, and GraphVite, while yielding 12-370x end-to-end speedups on real-world graphs.

</details>


### [92] [MolPaQ: Modular Quantum-Classical Patch Learning for Interpretable Molecular Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08575)
*Syed Rameez Naqvi,Lu Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOLPAQ是一种模块化量子-经典混合分子生成模型，通过β-VAE预训练的化学对齐潜空间、简约调节器映射分子描述符、参数高效量子补丁生成器产生纠缠节点嵌入，以及 valence-aware聚合器重建有效分子图，实现100% RDKit有效性和99.75%新颖性，量子生成器可提升约2.3%的平均QED并增加10-12%的芳香环 motif。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子生成模型通常在有效性、多样性和属性控制之间进行权衡，难以同时兼顾。量子计算在分子生成中的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 采用模块化架构：β-VAE在QM9上预训练以学习化学对齐潜空间；简约调节器将分子描述符映射到该空间；参数高效的量子补丁生成器产生纠缠节点嵌入；valence-aware聚合器将嵌入重建为有效分子图；使用潜判别器和化学形状奖励进行对抗微调。

Result: 实现了100% RDKit有效性、99.75%新颖性和0.905多样性；量子生成器相比参数量匹配的传统生成器，平均QED提升约2.3%，芳香环motif出现率增加约10-12%。

Conclusion: 量子生成器作为紧凑的拓扑塑造算子，能够在保持高有效性和新颖性的同时，引导生成分子往特定化学方向发展，验证了量子-经典混合范式在分子生成任务中的可行性。

Abstract: Molecular generative models must jointly ensure validity, diversity, and property control, yet existing approaches typically trade off among these objectives. We present MOLPAQ, a modular quantum-classical generator that assembles molecules from quantum-generated latent patches. A \b{eta}-VAE pretrained on QM9 learns a chemically aligned latent manifold; a reduced conditioner maps molecular descriptors into this space; and a parameter-efficient quantum patch generator produces entangled node embeddings that a valence-aware aggregator reconstructs into valid molecular graphs. Adversarial fine-tuning with a latent critic and chemistry-shaped reward yields 100\% RDKit validity, 99.75\% novelty, and 0.905 diversity. Beyond aggregate metrics, the pretrained quantum generator, steered by the conditioner, improves mean QED by approx. 2.3\% and increases aromatic motif incidence by approx. 10-12\% relative to a parameter-matched classical generator, highlighting its role as a compact topology-shaping operator.

</details>


### [93] [Creator Incentives in Recommender Systems: A Cooperative Game-Theoretic Approach for Stable and Fair Collaboration in Multi-Agent Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08643)
*Ramakrishnan Krishnamurthy,Arpit Agarwal,Lakshminarayanan Subramanian,Maximilian Nickel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将在线推荐平台中的内容创作者激励机制建模为多智能体随机线性赌博问题与可转移效用（TU）合作博弈的混合框架，通过联盟价值（成员累积遗憾的负和）衡量协作效果。研究证明同质智能体的TU博弈在温和算法条件下是凸的，核心非空且包含Shapley值；异质智能体虽仍保有非空核心，但Shapley值未必属于核心。针对异质情况，提出一种基于简单遗憾的分配规则，满足Shapley四公理中的三个且位于核心内。MovieLens-100k数据集实验验证了该分配规则与Shapley公平性在各类设置和算法下的对齐与分歧情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线推荐平台中，用户交互产生跨内容创作者的依赖效应：一个创作者内容获得的反馈会影响系统学习，进而影响其他创作者内容的曝光量。然而，现有研究缺乏对这种相互依赖环境中激励机制的系统性分析框架，尤其是如何确保协作稳定性（核心非空）和公平性（Shapley值）的问题尚未被充分探讨。

Method: 将多智能体在线学习问题与TU合作博弈理论结合：(1) 定义联盟价值为成员累积遗憾的负和；(2) 对同质智能体，证明TU博弈的凸性；(3) 对异质智能体，分析核心存在性但不保证Shapley值属于核心；(4) 提出基于简单遗憾的替代分配规则。

Result: 同质智能体场景下，TU博弈是凸的，核心非空且包含Shapley值；异质智能体场景下，核心仍非空但Shapley值可能不在核心内。提出的遗憾-based分配规则满足效率、匿名性、线性公理（不满足加性公理）且始终位于核心。MovieLens-100k实验表明该规则与Shapley值在特定设置下对齐，但在异质性和算法差异场景下存在分歧。

Conclusion: 该研究为推荐平台的内容创作者激励设计提供了理论基础：同质环境下Shapley值可直接用于公平分配；异质环境下需采用替代的遗憾-based规则以保证稳定性。未来可探索更复杂的异质性场景以及动态联盟形成机制。

Abstract: User interactions in online recommendation platforms create interdependencies among content creators: feedback on one creator's content influences the system's learning and, in turn, the exposure of other creators' contents. To analyze incentives in such settings, we model collaboration as a multi-agent stochastic linear bandit problem with a transferable utility (TU) cooperative game formulation, where a coalition's value equals the negative sum of its members' cumulative regrets.
  We show that, for identical (homogenous) agents with fixed action sets, the induced TU game is convex under mild algorithmic conditions, implying a non-empty core that contains the Shapley value and ensures both stability and fairness. For heterogeneous agents, the game still admits a non-empty core, though convexity and Shapley value core-membership are no longer guaranteed. To address this, we propose a simple regret-based payout rule that satisfies three out of the four Shapley axioms and also lies in the core. Experiments on MovieLens-100k dataset illustrate when the empirical payout aligns with -- and diverges from -- the Shapley fairness across different settings and algorithms.

</details>


### [94] [Structured Exploration and Exploitation of Label Functions for Automated Data Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08578)
*Phong Lam,Ha-Linh Nguyen,Thu-Trang Nguyen,Son Nguyen,Hieu Dinh Vo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EXPO NA是一个自动化程序化标注框架，通过多层次标签函数探索（表面、结构、语义）和可靠性感知机制生成高质量弱标签，在11个分类数据集上实现了98.9%的标签覆盖率、87%的弱标签质量提升和46%的F1性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量标注数据对机器学习模型训练至关重要，但人工标注成本高且易出错。现有的自动化标签函数生成方法存在局限：基于大语言模型的方法仅能合成表面级启发式规则，而基于模型合成的方法依赖手工设计的原语，导致覆盖范围有限且标签质量不可靠。

Method: EXPO NA将标签函数生成形式化为一个平衡多样性与可靠性的原则性过程。框架系统性地探索多层次标签函数，涵盖表面层、结构层和语义层三个视角；同时应用可靠性感知机制抑制噪声或冗余的启发式规则，同时保留互补信号。

Result: 在11个跨领域分类数据集上的广泛实验表明，EXPO NA始终优于最先进的自动化标签函数生成方法。具体而言，EXPO NA实现了近完全标签覆盖率（最高98.9%），弱标签质量提升最高87%，下游加权F1性能提升最高46%。

Conclusion: 多层次标签函数探索与可靠性感知过滤的结合，通过在生成的标签函数集中平衡覆盖率和精确度，实现了更一致的标签质量和跨任务的下游性能。

Abstract: High-quality labeled data is critical for training reliable machine learning and deep learning models, yet manual annotation remains costly and error-prone. Programmatic labeling addresses this challenge by using label functions (LFs), i.e., heuristic rules that automatically generate weak labels for training datasets. However, existing automated LF generation methods either rely on large language models (LLMs) to synthesize surface-level heuristics or employ model-based synthesis over hand-crafted primitives. These approaches often result in limited coverage and unreliable label quality. In this paper, we introduce EXPONA, an automated framework for programmatic labeling that formulates LF generation as a principled process balancing diversity and reliability. EXPONA systematically explores multi-level LFs, spanning surface, structural, and semantic perspectives. EXPONA further applies reliability-aware mechanisms to suppress noisy or redundant heuristics while preserving complementary signals. To evaluate EXPONA, we conducted extensive experiments on eleven classification datasets across diverse domains. Experimental results show that EXPONA consistently outperformed state-of-the-art automated LF generation methods. Specifically, EXPONA achieved nearly complete label coverage (up to 98.9%), improved weak label quality by up to 87%, and yielded downstream performance gains of up to 46% in weighted F1. These results indicate that EXPONA's combination of multi-level LF exploration and reliability-aware filtering enabled more consistent label quality and downstream performance across diverse tasks by balancing coverage and precision in the generated LF set.

</details>


### [95] [On the Spectral Geometry of Cross-Modal Representations: A Functional Map Diagnostic for Multimodal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08579)
*Krisanu Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉编码器（DINOv2）和语言编码器（all-MiniLM-L6-v2）之间的跨模态对齐问题，采用计算几何中的功能映射框架。研究发现，尽管两编码器的拉普拉斯特征值谱高度相似（归一化谱距离0.043），但特征向量基几乎未对齐（正交性误差70.15，对角占优性低于0.05），表明模型在捕获结构复杂度上收敛，但在组织方式上存在差异——即"谱复杂度-方向间隙"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索独立预训练的视觉和语言模型之间跨模态对齐的可能性与限制，理解多模态表征的内在结构特性，特别是在不依赖对比学习或监督信号的情况下，纯表征空间本身的对齐潜力。

Method: 采用功能映射（Functional Map）框架，将表征流形间的对应关系表示为图拉普拉斯特征基之间的紧凑线性算子；分别在跨模态检索任务上对比功能映射、Procrustes对齐和相对表征三种方法；通过谱分析（特征值谱、特征向量基对齐程度）量化表征兼容性。

Result: 功能映射框架在所有监督预算下的跨模态检索性能均低于Procrustes对齐和相对表征；但谱分析揭示了关键结构性质：两编码器特征值谱高度相似（归一化谱距离0.043），表明流形内在复杂度相当；然而特征向量基几乎不对齐（正交性误差高达70.15，对角占优性低于0.05）。

Conclusion: 独立训练的模型在"捕获多少结构"上收敛（谱复杂度一致），但在"如何组织结构"上存在差异（方向间隙）。作者将此定义为"谱复杂度-方向间隙"，并提出三个诊断量（对角占优性、正交性偏差、拉普拉斯交换性误差）用于表征跨模态表征兼容性，为谱对齐方法设定了边界条件。

Abstract: We study cross-modal alignment between independently pretrained vision (DINOv2) and language (all-MiniLM-L6-v2) encoders using the functional map framework from computational geometry, which represents correspondence between representation manifolds as a compact linear operator between graph Laplacian eigenbases. While the framework underperforms Procrustes alignment and relative representations for cross-modal retrieval across all supervision budgets, it reveals a structural property of multimodal representations. We find that the Laplacian eigenvalue spectra of the two encoders are quantitatively similar (normalized spectral distance 0.043), indicating that independently trained models develop manifolds of comparable intrinsic complexity. However, the functional map exhibits near-zero diagonal dominance (mean below 0.05) and large orthogonality error (70.15), showing that the eigenvector bases are effectively unaligned. We term this decoupling the spectral complexity--orientation gap: models converge in how much structure they capture but not in how they organize it. This gap defines a boundary condition for spectral alignment methods and motivates three diagnostic quantities : diagonal dominance, orthogonality deviation, and Laplacian commutativity error for characterizing cross-modal representation compatibility.

</details>


### [96] [Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection via Dual-Branch Reconstruction and Autoregressive Flow-based Residual Density Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08582)
*Jun Liu,Ying Chen,Ziqian Lu,Qinyue Tong,Jun Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DBR-AF框架用于多变量时间序列异常检测，通过双分支重建编码器（DBR）解耦跨变量相关性学习和变量内统计建模以缓解过拟合伪相关，同时利用自回归流模块（AF）建模复杂多变量残差分布并通过密度估计识别具有大重建误差的正常样本，从而克服传统重建方法的两大局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于重建的异常检测方法存在两个关键问题：1）过度强调跨变量建模导致过拟合伪相关；2）简单求和多变量重建误差产生误导性异常分数，难以区分难以重建的样本和真实异常。

Method: 提出DBR-AF框架，包含两个核心模块：1）双分支重建编码器（DBR）将跨变量相关性学习与变量内统计属性建模解耦；2）自回归流模块（AF）采用多层可逆变换建模复杂多变量残差分布，并利用密度估计精确识别具有大重建误差的正常样本。

Result: 在7个基准数据集上的实验表明DBR-AF达到最优性能，消融实验验证了各核心组件的必要性。

Conclusion: DBR-AF通过解耦建模和密度估计策略有效解决了传统重建方法的过拟合和异常分数误导问题，为多变量时间序列异常检测提供了一种更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection (MTSAD) is critical for real-world monitoring scenarios such as industrial control and aerospace systems. Mainstream reconstruction-based anomaly detection methods suffer from two key limitations: first, overfitting to spurious correlations induced by an overemphasis on cross-variable modeling; second, the generation of misleading anomaly scores by simply summing up multivariable reconstruction errors, which makes it difficult to distinguish between hard-to-reconstruct samples and genuine anomalies. To address these issues, we propose DBR-AF, a novel framework that integrates a dual-branch reconstruction (DBR) encoder and an autoregressive flow (AF) module. The DBR encoder decouples cross-variable correlation learning and intra-variable statistical property modeling to mitigate spurious correlations, while the AF module employs multiple stacked reversible transformations to model the complex multivariate residual distribution and further leverages density estimation to accurately identify normal samples with large reconstruction errors. Extensive experiments on seven benchmark datasets demonstrate that DBR-AF achieves state-of-the-art performance, with ablation studies validating the indispensability of its core components.

</details>


### [97] [PRAGMA: Revolut Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08649)
*Maxim Ostroukhov,Ruslan Mikhailov,Vladimir Iashin,Artem Sokolov,Andrei Akshonov,Vitaly Protasov,Dmitrii Beloborodov,Vince Mullin,Roman Yokunda Enzmann,Georgios Kolovos,Jason Renders,Pavel Nesterov,Anton Repushko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRAGMA是一个面向多源银行事件序列的基础模型，采用Transformer架构和掩码建模进行自监督预训练，能够在信用评分、欺诈检测和用户生命周期价值预测等多种下游任务中取得优异表现，支持通过简单线性层或轻量微调进行任务适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代金融系统产生海量交易和事件级数据，蕴含丰富经济信号，但现有方法通常针对单一数据源或特定任务，缺乏通用的金融事件序列表示学习框架。

Method: 基于Transformer架构，利用掩码建模（masked modeling）进行自监督预训练，针对金融记录的离散性和变长特性设计定制化目标函数，在大规模异构银行事件语料上训练。

Result: 在多个下游任务（信用评分、欺诈检测、生命周期价值预测）中达到最优性能，可直接从原始事件序列中提取有效表示。

Conclusion: PRAGMA为金融应用提供了通用的表示层，证明通过简单线性模型或轻量微调即可实现强大性能，为多源金融数据分析开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Modern financial systems generate vast quantities of transactional and event-level data that encode rich economic signals. This paper presents PRAGMA, a family of foundation models for multi-source banking event sequences. Our approach pre-trains a Transformer-based architecture with masked modelling on a large-scale, heterogeneous banking event corpus using a self-supervised objective tailored to the discrete, variable-length nature of financial records. The resulting model supports a wide range of downstream tasks such as credit scoring, fraud detection, and lifetime value prediction: strong performance can be achieved by training a simple linear model on top of the extracted embeddings and can be further improved with lightweight fine-tuning. Through extensive evaluation on downstream tasks, we demonstrate that PRAGMA achieves superior performance across multiple domains directly from raw event sequences, providing a general-purpose representation layer for financial applications.

</details>


### [98] [CSAttention: Centroid-Scoring Attention for Accelerating LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08584)
*Chuxu Song,Zhencan Peng,Jiuqi Wei,Chuanhui Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CSAttention是一种无需训练的稀疏注意力方法，通过在离线预填充阶段构建基于查询的查找表，在线解码阶段用高效表查找替代全上下文扫描，在95%稀疏度和32K-128K长上下文下实现近全注意力精度，128K上下文下推理速度提升最高4.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM依赖可复用预填充提示，导致注意力和KV缓存成为解码阶段的主要瓶颈。现有稀疏注意力方法在高稀疏度下因Query-Key分布偏移而难以保持精度。

Method: 采用"存储换计算"策略：离线预填充阶段构建以查询为中心的查找表（大小固定），在线解码阶段用GPU友好的表查找和分数累积替代全上下文扫描。

Result: CSAttention在95%稀疏度、32K-128K长上下文设置下，精度接近全注意力，且优于所有现有稀疏注意力方法。

Conclusion: CSAttention通过将计算前置到可摊销的离线预填充阶段，同时优化单步解码延迟，为长上下文LLM服务提供了一种高效且精度损失小的稀疏注意力方案。

Abstract: Long-context LLMs increasingly rely on extended, reusable prefill prompts for agents and domain Q&A, pushing attention and KV-cache to become the dominant decode-time bottlenecks. While sparse attention reduces computation and transfer costs, it often struggles to maintain accuracy at high sparsity levels due to the inherent distribution shift between Queries and Keys. We propose Centroid-Scoring Attention (CSAttention), a training-free sparse attention method optimized for high-throughput serving of reusable contexts. CSAttention adopts a storage-for-computation strategy tailored to the offline-prefill/online-decode setting: it front-loads computation into a one-time offline prefill phase that can be amortized across multiple queries, while aggressively optimizing per-step decoding latency. Specifically, CSAttention constructs query-centric lookup tables during offline prefill, whose size remains fixed during decoding, and enables online decoding to replace full-context scans with efficient table lookups and GPU-friendly score accumulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CSAttention achieves near-identical accuracy to full attention. Under high sparsity (95%) and long-context settings (32K-128K), CSAttention consistently outperforms state-of-the-art sparse attention methods in both model accuracy and inference speed, achieving up to 4.6x inference speedup over the most accurate baseline at a context length of 128K.

</details>


### [99] [Act or Escalate? Evaluating Escalation Behavior in Automation with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08588)
*Matthew DosSantos DiSorbo,Harang Ju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究将AI自动化决策建模为不确定性下的成本权衡问题，发现不同模型的升级阈值差异显著且与架构规模无关，自我估计存在系统性偏差。其中，基于思维链的监督微调是最有效的干预手段，能够产生可泛化的稳健决策策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化系统的有效性取决于正确决策何时自主行动、何时升级至人类，但目前对模型如何隐式权衡这些成本缺乏系统性理解。

Method: 跨5个领域（需求预测、内容推荐、内容审核、贷款审批、自动驾驶）和多种模型家族进行实验；采用成本比较框架评估干预措施，包括调整成本比率、提供准确度信号、基于思维链的监督微调（SFT）。

Result: 模型的隐式阈值差异显著，且无法被架构或规模预测；自我估计存在模型特定的校准偏差；提示词工程主要对推理模型有效；基于思维链的SFT产生最稳健的策略，可跨数据集、成本比率、提示框架和未见领域泛化。

Conclusion: 升级行为是模型特定属性，部署前应进行特征化分析；稳健的对齐需要训练模型明确推理不确定性及决策成本。

Abstract: Effective automation hinges on deciding when to act and when to escalate. We model this as a decision under uncertainty: an LLM forms a prediction, estimates its probability of being correct, and compares the expected costs of acting and escalating. Using this framework across five domains of recorded human decisions-demand forecasting, content recommendation, content moderation, loan approval, and autonomous driving-and across multiple model families, we find marked differences in the implicit thresholds models use to trade off these costs. These thresholds vary substantially and are not predicted by architecture or scale, while self-estimates are miscalibrated in model-specific ways. We then test interventions that target this decision process by varying cost ratios, providing accuracy signals, and training models to follow the desired escalation rule. Prompting helps mainly for reasoning models. SFT on chain-of-thought targets yields the most robust policies, which generalize across datasets, cost ratios, prompt framings, and held-out domains. These results suggest that escalation behavior is a model-specific property that should be characterized before deployment, and that robust alignment benefits from training models to reason explicitly about uncertainty and decision costs.

</details>


### [100] [EngageTriBoost: Predictive Modeling of User Engagement in Digital Mental Health Intervention Using Explainable Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08589)
*Ha Na Cho,Daniel Eisenberg,Cheryl King,Kai Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Mental health challenges among young adults, are on the rise, necessitating effective solutions such as digital mental health interventions (DMHIs). Despite their promise, DMHIs face significant adoption barriers, including low initial uptake and high dropout rates. This study leverages machine learning (ML) to analyze behavioral patterns of users of a DMHI, eBridge, designed to increase the utilization of professional mental health services among at-risk college students through motivational interviewing-based online counseling. Our ensemble model, EngageTriBoost, achieved up to 84% accuracy in predicting engagement, measured by sign-ins and counselor interactions. We then applied the Shapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) analysis which provided clear, interpretable insights into key factors influencing user engagement such as emotional dysregulation and perceived stigma, highlighting their critical effect on DMHI adoption. This study demonstrates the power of explainable ML for better understanding user engagement with DMHI to improve their adoption and achievable impact on mental health outcomes.

</details>


### [101] [AlphaLab: Autonomous Multi-Agent Research Across Optimization Domains with Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08590)
*Brendan R. Hogan,Xiwen Chen,James T. Wilson,Kashif Rasul,Adel Boyarsky,Thomas Kamei,Anderson Schneider,Yuriy Nevmyvaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AlphaLab是一个利用前沿LLM智能体能力自动化执行定量计算密集型领域完整实验周期的自主研究框架。该系统仅需数据集和自然语言目标即可完成从领域适应、评估构建到大规模GPU实验的全部流程，在CUDA内核优化、LLM预训练和交通预测三个领域均取得显著成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI研究依赖大量人工干预，定量计算密集型领域的实验周期需要一种能够自主完成从数据分析、模型设计到实验执行的完整流程的系统。

Method: 系统采用三阶段流程：(1) 领域适应与探索，编写分析代码并生成研究报告；(2) 对抗性构建和验证评估框架；(3) 通过Strategist/Worker循环运行大规模GPU实验。领域特定行为通过模型生成的适配器实现，持久化playbook作为在线提示优化机制。

Result: CUDA内核优化平均提速4.4倍（最高91倍）；LLM预训练验证损失降低22%；交通预测超越标准基线23-25%。两个模型（GPT-5.2和Claude Opus 4.6）在每个领域发现定性不同的解决方案，验证了多模型协作的互补搜索覆盖能力。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了定量计算领域研究流程的自动化，展示了前沿LLM智能体在科学研究中的潜力，多模型协作可提供更全面的搜索覆盖。

Abstract: We present AlphaLab, an autonomous research harness that leverages frontier LLM agentic capabilities to automate the full experimental cycle in quantitative, computation-intensive domains. Given only a dataset and a natural-language objective, AlphaLab proceeds through three phases without human intervention: (1) it adapts to the domain and explores the data, writing analysis code and producing a research report; (2) it constructs and adversarially validates its own evaluation framework; and (3) it runs large-scale GPU experiments via a Strategist/Worker loop, accumulating domain knowledge in a persistent playbook that functions as a form of online prompt optimization. All domain-specific behavior is factored into adapters generated by the model itself, so the same pipeline handles qualitatively different tasks without modification. We evaluate AlphaLab with two frontier LLMs (GPT-5.2 and Claude Opus 4.6) on three domains: CUDA kernel optimization, where it writes GPU kernels that run 4.4x faster than torch.compile on average (up to 91x); LLM pretraining, where the full system achieves 22% lower validation loss than a single-shot baseline using the same model; and traffic forecasting, where it beats standard baselines by 23-25% after researching and implementing published model families from the literature. The two models discover qualitatively different solutions in every domain (neither dominates uniformly), suggesting that multi-model campaigns provide complementary search coverage. We additionally report results on financial time series forecasting in the appendix, and release all code at https://brendanhogan.github.io/alphalab-paper/.

</details>


### [102] [Reservoir observer enhanced with residual calibration and attention mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08592)
*Yichen Liu,Wei Xiao,Tianguang Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对非线性动态系统中未测变量的数据驱动推断问题，提出将残差校准和注意力机制集成到储层观测器设计中，显著提升了推理精度，特别是在传统储层观测器性能最差的情况下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统储层观测器在选择不同输入变量时性能差异较大，在最坏情况下可能损害可靠性，需要增强推理性能。

Method: 1) 残差校准模块：利用估计残差信息优化观测器输出；2) 注意力机制：挖掘数据时间依赖性，丰富储层内部动态表示；3) 转移熵：解释输入依赖的观测差异原因。

Result: 在典型混沌系统上的实验表明，该方法显著提升了推理精度，尤其对传统储层观测器最坏情况效果明显。

Conclusion: 残差校准和注意力机制的集成能有效提升储层观测器的性能，为非线性系统状态估计提供了更可靠的数据驱动方案。

Abstract: Reservoir observers provide a data-driven approach to the inference of unmeasured variables from observed ones for nonlinear dynamical systems. While previous studies have demonstrated wide applicability, their performance may vary considerably with different input variables, even compromising reliability in the worst cases. To enhance the performance of inference, we integrate residual calibration and attention mechanism into the reservoir observer design. The residual calibration module leverages information from the estimation residuals to refine the observer output, and the attention mechanism exploits the temporal dependencies of the data to enrich the representation of reservoir internal dynamics. Experiments on typical chaotic systems demonstrate that our method substantially improves inference accuracy, especially for the worst cases resulting from the traditional reservoir observers. We also invoke the notion of transfer entropy to explain the reason for the input-dependent observation discrepancy and the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [103] [From Selection to Scheduling: Federated Geometry-Aware Correction Makes Exemplar Replay Work Better under Continual Dynamic Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08617)
*Zhuang Qi,Ying-Peng Tang,Lei Meng,Guoqing Chao,Lei Wu,Han Yu,Xiangxu Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，提出FEAT方法，通过几何结构对齐和能量基校正两个模块，有效解决客户端间异构性导致的类别不平衡和表征崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究过度关注样本重要性估计，忽视了如何有效利用所选样本，且在客户端和任务间动态异构环境下表现受限。同时，类别不平衡导致少数类特征向多数类漂移，引发表征崩溃。

Method: 提出FEAT方法，包含两个关键模块：1) 几何结构对齐模块，通过知识蒸馏将特征表示的成对角相似性与等角紧框架原型的角相似性对齐，作为跨客户端共享的类判别参考结构；2) 能量基几何校正模块，从特征嵌入中移除任务无关的方向分量。

Result: 该方法缓解了表征漂移，增强了少数类敏感性，提升了模型在类别不平衡分布下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过几何结构对齐和校正机制，FEAT能有效应对联邦持续学习中的动态异构性和类别不平衡挑战。

Abstract: Exemplar replay has become an effective strategy for mitigating catastrophic forgetting in federated continual learning (FCL) by retaining representative samples from past tasks. Existing studies focus on designing sample-importance estimation mechanisms to identify information-rich samples. However, they typically overlook strategies for effectively utilizing the selected exemplars, which limits their performance under continual dynamic heterogeneity across clients and tasks. To address this issue, this paper proposes a Federated gEometry-Aware correcTion method, termed FEAT, which alleviates imbalance-induced representation collapse that drags rare-class features toward frequent classes across clients. Specifically, it consists of two key modules: 1) the Geometric Structure Alignment module performs structural knowledge distillation by aligning the pairwise angular similarities between feature representations and their corresponding Equiangular Tight Frame prototypes, which are fixed and shared across clients to serve as a class-discriminative reference structure. This encourages geometric consistency across tasks and helps mitigate representation drift; 2) the Energy-based Geometric Correction module removes task-irrelevant directional components from feature embeddings, which reduces prediction bias toward majority classes. This improves sensitivity to minority classes and enhances the model's robustness under class-imbalanced distributions.

</details>


### [104] [StructRL: Recovering Dynamic Programming Structure from Learning Dynamics in Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08620)
*Ivo Nowak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这篇论文发现分布强化学习的训练动态可以恢复动态规划中的结构化信息传播特性，无需显式模型即可利用这种结构改进采样策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习被视为统一的、数据驱动的优化过程，而动态规划依赖结构化信息传播。文章旨在探究分布强化学习的训练动态是否能恢复这种结构化特性。

Method: 通过分析回报分布的时间演化，识别状态空间中学习发生时机和位置的信号；引入状态s最强学习更新的时刻指标t*(s)；基于此提出StructRL框架指导采样。

Result: t*(s)指标产生的状态排序与动态规划式信息传播一致；StructRL能利用学习动态中的结构指导采样。

Conclusion: 分布强化学习动态提供了一种无需显式模型即可恢复和利用动态规划式结构的机制，为理解强化学习提供了新视角。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is typically treated as a uniform, data-driven optimization process, where updates are guided by rewards and temporal-difference errors without explicitly exploiting global structure. In contrast, dynamic programming methods rely on structured information propagation, enabling efficient and stable learning. In this paper, we provide evidence that such structure can be recovered from the learning dynamics of distributional reinforcement learning. By analyzing the temporal evolution of return distributions, we identify signals that capture when and where learning occurs in the state space. In particular, we introduce a temporal learning indicator t*(s) that reflects when a state undergoes its strongest learning update during training. Empirically, this signal induces an ordering over states that is consistent with a dynamic programming-style propagation of information. Building on this observation, we propose StructRL, a framework that exploits these signals to guide sampling in alignment with the emerging propagation structure. Our preliminary results suggest that distributional learning dynamics provide a mechanism to recover and exploit dynamic programming-like structure without requiring an explicit model. This offers a new perspective on reinforcement learning, where learning can be interpreted as a structured propagation process rather than a purely uniform optimization procedure.

</details>


### [105] [VOLTA: The Surprising Ineffectiveness of Auxiliary Losses for Calibrated Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08639)
*Rahul D Ray,Utkarsh Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对十种不确定性量化（UQ）方法进行了综合基准测试，将简化版VOLTA方法与MC Dropout、SWAG、集成方法等主流基线进行对比。VOLTA在保持轻量化和确定性特性的同时，在CIFAR-10上达到0.864的准确率，预期校准误差低至0.010，OOD检测AUROC达0.802，显著优于大多数基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定性量化（UQ）对于深度学习在安全关键应用中的部署至关重要，但目前尚无共识哪种UQ方法在不同数据模态和分布偏移下表现最佳，缺乏统一基准进行比较。

Method: 作者提出简化版VOLTA方法（仅保留深度编码器、可学习原型、交叉熵损失和后验温度缩放），并对10种广泛使用的UQ基线方法进行综合基准测试。评估数据集包括：同分布的CIFAR-10、不同分布的CIFAR-100和SVHN、均匀噪声（OOD）、CIFAR-10-C（损坏数据）和Tiny ImageNet特征（表格数据）。采用三个随机种子的统计测试验证结果可靠性。

Result: VOLTA在CIFAR-10上达到0.864的竞争性准确率，预期校准误差（ECE）仅为0.010，显著低于基线方法的0.044-0.102区间，OOD检测AUROC达0.802。消融实验证实了自适应温度和深度编码器的重要性。

Conclusion: VOLTA作为一种轻量级、确定性且校准良好的方法，可作为复杂UQ方法的有效替代方案，其简化设计保留了核心性能同时降低了计算复杂度。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for deploying deep learning models in safety critical applications, yet no consensus exists on which UQ method performs best across different data modalities and distribution shifts. This paper presents a comprehensive benchmark of ten widely used UQ baselines including MC Dropout, SWAG, ensemble methods, temperature scaling, energy based OOD, Mahalanobis, hyperbolic classifiers, ENN, Taylor Sensus, and split conformal prediction against a simplified yet highly effective variant of VOLTA that retains only a deep encoder, learnable prototypes, cross entropy loss, and post hoc temperature scaling.
