Abstract: The generation of planning domains from natural language descriptions remains an open problem even with the advent of large language models and reasoning models. Recent work suggests that while LLMs have the ability to assist with domain generation, they are still far from producing high quality domains that can be deployed in practice. To this end, we investigate the ability of an agentic language model feedback framework to generate planning domains from natural language descriptions that have been augmented with a minimal amount of symbolic information. In particular, we evaluate the quality of the generated domains under various forms of symbolic feedback, including landmarks, and output from the VAL plan validator. Using these feedback mechanisms, we experiment using heuristic search over model space to optimize domain quality.

</details>


### [164] [Hidden in Plain Sight: Visual-to-Symbolic Analytical Solution Inference from Field Visualizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08863)
*Pengze Li,Jiaquan Zhang,Yunbo Long,Xinping Liu,Zhou wenjie,Encheng Su,Zihang Zeng,Jiaqi Liu,Jiyao Liu,Junchi Yu,Lihao Liu,Philip Torr,Shixiang Tang,Aoran Wang,Xi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ViSA任务，从二维线性稳态场的可视化图像中推断解析解符号表达式，并发布ViSA-R2模型和ViSA-Bench基准测试。ViSA-R2采用类物理学家思维链流程，在8B Qwen3-VL基础上超越开源基线和前沿闭源VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视觉观测中恢复物理场解析解是AI辅助科学推理的基础能力，但该方向尚未被充分探索。现有VLM在复杂符号推理和多步验证方面存在不足。

Method: 提出ViSA-R2模型，配备自验证的solution-centric chain-of-thought pipeline，流程为：结构模式识别→解族假设→参数推导→一致性验证。同时发布ViSA-Bench基准，覆盖30个线性稳态场景。

Result: ViSA-R2在标准化协议下，数值精度、表达式结构相似性和字符级准确率均优于强开源基线和评估的闭源前沿VLM。

Conclusion: 通过类物理学家思维链与自验证机制，VLM可从可视化推断符号化解析解，为AI科学推理提供新范式。

Abstract: Recovering analytical solutions of physical fields from visual observations is a fundamental yet underexplored capability for AI-assisted scientific reasoning. We study visual-to-symbolic analytical solution inference (ViSA) for two-dimensional linear steady-state fields: given field visualizations (and first-order derivatives) plus minimal auxiliary metadata, the model must output a single executable SymPy expression with fully instantiated numeric constants. We introduce ViSA-R2 and align it with a self-verifying, solution-centric chain-of-thought pipeline that follows a physicist-like pathway: structural pattern recognition solution-family (ansatz) hypothesis parameter derivation consistency verification. We also release ViSA-Bench, a VLM-ready synthetic benchmark covering 30 linear steady-state scenarios with verifiable analytical/symbolic annotations, and evaluate predictions by numerical accuracy, expression-structure similarity, and character-level accuracy. Using an 8B open-weight Qwen3-VL backbone, ViSA-R2 outperforms strong open-source baselines and the evaluated closed-source frontier VLMs under a standardized protocol.

</details>


### [165] [SPPO: Sequence-Level PPO for Long-Horizon Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08865)
*Tianyi Wang,Yixia Li,Long Li,Yibiao Chen,Shaohan Huang,Yun Chen,Peng Li,Yang Liu,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPPO通过将推理过程重构为序列级上下文盗贼问题，采用解耦的标量价值函数，在无需多采样的情况下实现低方差优势信号，兼顾PPO的采样效率和结果导向更新的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准token级PPO在LLM推理对齐中存在两个核心问题：1）长CoT推理链上的时序信用分配不稳定；2）价值模型带来高昂的显存成本。虽然无 Critic 的替代方案（如GRPO）可缓解这些问题，但需要多次采样估计基线，导致计算开销大、训练吞吐量严重受限。

Method: SPPO将推理过程重新建模为序列级上下文盗贼问题（Sequence-Level Contextual Bandit），采用解耦的标量价值函数（decoupled scalar value function）来推导低方差优势信号，无需多采样即可实现稳定的策略更新。

Result: 在数学基准测试上的实验表明，SPPO显著优于标准PPO，并与计算密集的组基线方法性能相当，同时大幅降低了资源消耗。

Conclusion: SPPO为推理LLM的对齐提供了一个资源高效的可扩展框架，成功融合了PPO的样本效率与结果导向更新的稳定性。

Abstract: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) is central to aligning Large Language Models (LLMs) in reasoning tasks with verifiable rewards. However, standard token-level PPO struggles in this setting due to the instability of temporal credit assignment over long Chain-of-Thought (CoT) horizons and the prohibitive memory cost of the value model. While critic-free alternatives like GRPO mitigate these issues, they incur significant computational overhead by requiring multiple samples for baseline estimation, severely limiting training throughput. In this paper, we introduce Sequence-Level PPO (SPPO), a scalable algorithm that harmonizes the sample efficiency of PPO with the stability of outcome-based updates. SPPO reformulates the reasoning process as a Sequence-Level Contextual Bandit problem, employing a decoupled scalar value function to derive low-variance advantage signals without multi-sampling. Extensive experiments on mathematical benchmarks demonstrate that SPPO significantly surpasses standard PPO and matches the performance of computation-heavy group-based methods, offering a resource-efficient framework for aligning reasoning LLMs.

</details>


### [166] [Strategic Algorithmic Monoculture:Experimental Evidence from Coordination Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09502)
*Gonzalo Ballestero,Hadi Hosseini,Samarth Khanna,Ran I. Shorrer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: AI agents increasingly operate in multi-agent environments where outcomes depend on coordination. We distinguish primary algorithmic monoculture -- baseline action similarity -- from strategic algorithmic monoculture, whereby agents adjust similarity in response to incentives. We implement a simple experimental design that cleanly separates these forces, and deploy it on human and large language model (LLM) subjects. LLMs exhibit high levels of baseline similarity (primary monoculture) and, like humans, they regulate it in response to coordination incentives (strategic monoculture). While LLMs coordinate extremely well on similar actions, they lag behind humans in sustaining heterogeneity when divergence is rewarded.

</details>


### [167] [StaRPO: Stability-Augmented Reinforcement Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08905)
*Jinghan Zhang,Fengran Mo,Tharindu Cyril Weerasooriya,Ruimin Dai,Xiaoyan Han,Yanjie Fu,Dakuo Wang,Kunpeng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StaRPO是一种稳定性增强的强化学习框架，通过引入ACF和PE两个轻量级指标评估推理过程的局部一致性和全局目标导向性，从而弥补现有RL方法仅关注最终答案正确性的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL策略优化框架主要依赖最终答案正确性作为反馈信号，忽略了推理过程的内部逻辑结构。这导致模型生成的响应虽然流畅且语义相关，但可能存在逻辑不一致、结构混乱或冗余等问题。

Method: 将推理稳定性分解为两个可计算的轻量级指标：(1) 自相关函数(ACF)评估局部步进间一致性；(2) 路径效率(PE)评估推理轨迹的全局目标导向性。将稳定性奖励与任务奖励结合，提供互补的、过程感知的反馈信号。

Result: 实验表明ACF和PE奖励与逻辑错误具有相关性；在四个推理基准测试中，StaRPO始终优于基线方法，能够同时提升最终答案准确率和逻辑稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过显式纳入推理稳定性指标，StaRPO为LLM在复杂推理任务中提供了更全面的优化目标，验证了过程感知反馈在强化学习中的价值。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is effective in enhancing the accuracy of large language models in complex reasoning tasks. Existing RL policy optimization frameworks rely on final-answer correctness as feedback signals and rarely capture the internal logical structure of the reasoning process. Consequently, the models would generate fluent and semantically relevant responses but logically inconsistent, structurally erratic, or redundant. To this end, we propose StaRPO, a stability-augmented reinforcement learning framework that explicitly incorporates reasoning stability into the optimization objective. Our StaRPO decomposes stability into two computable lightweight metrics: the Autocorrelation Function (ACF) to evaluate local step-to-step coherence, and Path Efficiency (PE) to evaluate global goal-directedness of the reasoning trajectory. These stability rewards are combined with task rewards to provide complementary and process-aware feedback. We validate the effectiveness of using ACF and PE rewards by showing their correlation with logic errors on two backbone models. Experiments on four reasoning benchmarks show that StaRPO consistently outperforms compared baselines and can enhance both final-answer accuracy and logical stability.

</details>


### [168] [SEA-Eval: A Benchmark for Evaluating Self-Evolving Agents Beyond Episodic Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08988)
*Sihang Jiang,Lipeng Ma,Zhonghua Hong,Keyi Wang,Zhiyu Lu,Shisong Chen,Jinghao Zhang,Tianjun Pan,Weijia Zhou,Jiaqing Liang,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了自进化智能体(SEA)的正式定义，并设计了首个SEA评估基准SEA-Eval，通过顺序任务流分析发现现有顶尖框架存在显著的进化瓶颈——相同成功率下token消耗差异高达31.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的智能体受限于静态工具集和情景遗忘问题，无法跨任务积累经验或优化策略，从单纯的任务执行者向真正的自进化智能体演进缺乏系统性评估方法。

Method: 提出SEA的形式化定义（基于数字具身和跨任务持续进化），设计SEA-Eval基准从任务内执行可靠性和长期进化性能两个维度评估，通过顺序任务流分析追踪成功率与Token消耗的时间变化。

Result: 实验揭示当前SOTA框架存在显著进化瓶颈：表面相同的成功率掩盖了高达31.2倍的token消耗差异，顺序分析下呈现分歧的进化轨迹。

Conclusion: SEA-Eval为评估智能体从任务执行者向自进化数字实体的演进提供了严格科学基础，现有框架在持续进化能力上存在根本性缺陷。

Abstract: Current LLM-based agents demonstrate strong performance in episodic task execution but remain constrained by static toolsets and episodic amnesia, failing to accumulate experience or optimize strategies across task boundaries. While the Self-Evolving Agent (SEA) paradigm has been previously proposed, this paper contributes a new formal definition of SEA grounded in digital embodiment and continuous cross-task evolution, and introduces SEA-Eval, the first benchmark designed to evaluate SEA characteristics across two dimensions, intra-task execution reliability and long-term evolutionary performance. By organizing tasks into sequential streams and analyzing Success Rate and Token Consumption over time, SEA-Eval quantifies evolutionary gain and structural stability in ways that existing episodic benchmarks cannot. Empirical evaluations reveal a significant evolutionary bottleneck in current state-of-the-art frameworks, where identical success rates mask up to 31.2 times differences in token consumption and divergent evolutionary trajectories under sequential analysis. SEA-Eval provides a rigorous scientific foundation for advancing agents from mere task executors toward genuinely self-evolving digital entities.

</details>


### [169] [Advantage-Guided Diffusion for Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09035)
*Daniele Foffano,Arvid Eriksson,David Broman,Karl H. Johansson,Alexandre Proutiere*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AGD-MBRL通过优势估计引导反向扩散过程，解决扩散世界模型在短视野时的短视问题，在MuJoCo控制任务上相比PolyGRAD等方法提升样本效率最高2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于自回归世界模型的MBRL存在误差累积问题；现有扩散引导方法要么仅基于策略（丢弃价值信息），要么基于奖励（在短扩散视野时产生短视行为）。需要一种能在生成窗口之外关注长期回报的引导机制。

Method: 提出优势引导扩散(AGD)：通过智能体的优势估计引导反向扩散过程，采样聚焦于预期获得更高长期回报的轨迹。开发两种引导器：(1)Sigmoid优势引导(SAG)和(2)指数优势引导(EAG)。方法可无缝集成到PolyGRAD架构中，仅引导状态组件，动作生成保持策略条件化，无需修改扩散训练目标。

Result: 理论证明：SAG或EAG引导的扩散模型可实现按优势加权重新采样的轨迹分布。实验验证：生成的轨迹遵循改进策略（更高价值）。在MuJoCo任务(HalfCheetah, Hopper, Walker2D, Reacher)上，AGD-MBRL相比PolyGRAD、在线Diffuser风格奖励引导及模型无关基线(PPO/TRPO)在样本效率和最终回报上均有提升，部分情况提升达2倍。

Conclusion: 优势感知引导是解决扩散模型MBRL短视野短视问题的简单有效方法。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) with autoregressive world models suffers from compounding errors, whereas diffusion world models mitigate this by generating trajectory segments jointly. However, existing diffusion guides are either policy-only, discarding value information, or reward-based, which becomes myopic when the diffusion horizon is short. We introduce Advantage-Guided Diffusion for MBRL (AGD-MBRL), which steers the reverse diffusion process using the agent's advantage estimates so that sampling concentrates on trajectories expected to yield higher long-term return beyond the generated window. We develop two guides: (i) Sigmoid Advantage Guidance (SAG) and (ii) Exponential Advantage Guidance (EAG). We prove that a diffusion model guided through SAG or EAG allows us to perform reweighted sampling of trajectories with weights increasing in state-action advantage-implying policy improvement under standard assumptions. Additionally, we show that the trajectories generated from AGD-MBRL follow an improved policy (that is, with higher value) compared to an unguided diffusion model. AGD integrates seamlessly with PolyGRAD-style architectures by guiding the state components while leaving action generation policy-conditioned, and requires no change to the diffusion training objective. On MuJoCo control tasks (HalfCheetah, Hopper, Walker2D and Reacher), AGD-MBRL improves sample efficiency and final return over PolyGRAD, an online Diffuser-style reward guide, and model-free baselines (PPO/TRPO), in some cases by a margin of 2x. These results show that advantage-aware guidance is a simple, effective remedy for short-horizon myopia in diffusion-model MBRL.

</details>


### [170] [Overhang Tower: Resource-Rational Adaptation in Sequential Physical Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09072)
*Ruihong Shen,Shiqian Li,Yixin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 人类在有限认知资源下进行物理规划时，会同时调整预测机制和规划策略：简单场景依赖直觉物理引擎的模拟推理，复杂场景转向CNN式的视觉启发式策略；时间压力下则从深度前瞻转为浅层规划。这种"双重转换"揭示了一种层级化的资源理性架构，统一了物理直觉研究中"模拟vs启发式"和决策研究中"审慎vs短视"的两大长期争论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直觉物理研究分为两派：直觉物理引擎(IPE)假说认为人进行模拟推理，而快慢系统研究则强调基于线索的启发式策略；决策研究也有关于审慎前瞻与短视策略的争论。但这两个辩论独立进行， sequential physical planning 的认知架构尚不明确，尤其在认知资源限制下预测机制与规划策略如何协同适应仍是开放问题。

Method: 使用Overhang Tower任务（建造任务，要求参与者在保持稳定性的前提下最大化水平悬挑长度），操纵复杂度（任务难度）和时间压力，测量人类行为表现。实验发现可以用IPE-based模拟和CNN-based视觉启发式两种机制很好地拟合行为数据。

Result: 人类表现出"双重转换"：1) 物理预测机制：简单场景以IPE模拟为主，复杂度增加时转向CNN视觉启发式；2) 规划策略：时间压力下审慎的前瞻深度被截断，移向更浅层的规划视野。这两种转换同时发生，单一机制的理论无法预测此结果。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一种层级化的资源理性架构：认知系统根据认知预算灵活地在计算成本和预测保真度之间权衡。这一发现统一了两个长期争论——直觉物理的"模拟vs启发式"以及决策的"审慎vs短视规划"——将其理解为由认知预算动态重配置的资源适应性策略库。

Abstract: Humans effortlessly navigate the physical world by predicting how objects behave under gravity and contact forces, yet how such judgments support sequential physical planning under resource constraints remains poorly understood. Research on intuitive physics debates whether prediction relies on the Intuitive Physics Engine (IPE) or fast, cue-based heuristics; separately, decision-making research debates deliberative lookahead versus myopic strategies. These debates have proceeded in isolation, leaving the cognitive architecture of sequential physical planning underspecified. How physical prediction mechanisms and planning strategies jointly adapt under limited cognitive resources remains an open question. Here we show that humans exhibit a dual transition under resource pressure, simultaneously shifting both physical prediction mechanism and planning strategy to match cognitive budget. Using Overhang Tower, a construction task requiring participants to maximize horizontal overhang while maintaining stability, we find that IPE-based simulation dominates early stages while CNN-based visual heuristics prevail as complexity grows; concurrently, time pressure truncates deliberative lookahead, shifting planning toward shallower horizons: a dual transition unpredicted by prior single-mechanism accounts. These findings reveal a hierarchical, resource-rational architecture that flexibly trades computational cost against predictive fidelity. Our results unify two long-standing debates (simulation vs. heuristics and myopic vs. deliberative planning) as a dynamic repertoire reconfigured by cognitive budget.

</details>


### [171] [Camera Artist: A Multi-Agent Framework for Cinematic Language Storytelling Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09195)
*Haobo Hu,Qi Mao,Yuanhang Li,Libiao Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Camera Artist是一个多智能体框架，通过引入专门的 cinematography Shot Agent（摄影镜头智能体）来生成具有明确电影语言的电影叙事视频，解决了现有系统缺乏镜头间叙事连贯性和电影化表达的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体电影制作系统缺乏两个关键能力：(1) 跨镜头叙事进展的结构化机制；(2) 电影语言的刻意运用。这导致生成的视频存在叙事碎片化和电影质量受限的问题。

Method: 基于现有智能体管道构建，引入专门的Cinematography Shot Agent，集成了两个核心模块：(1) 递归故事板生成（增强镜头间叙事连贯性）；(2) 电影语言注入（产生更具表现力的电影化镜头设计）。

Result: 定量和定性实验表明，该方法在叙事一致性、动态表现力和感知电影质量方面均优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: Camera Artist通过专门的电影摄影智能体有效解决了多智能体电影制作中的叙事碎片化问题，为自动化电影制作提供了更具电影感的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose Camera Artist, a multi-agent framework that models a real-world filmmaking workflow to generate narrative videos with explicit cinematic language. While recent multi-agent systems have made substantial progress in automating filmmaking workflows from scripts to videos, they often lack explicit mechanisms to structure narrative progression across adjacent shots and deliberate use of cinematic language, resulting in fragmented storytelling and limited filmic quality. To address this, Camera Artist builds upon established agentic pipelines and introduces a dedicated Cinematography Shot Agent, which integrates recursive storyboard generation to strengthen shot-to-shot narrative continuity and cinematic language injection to produce more expressive, film-oriented shot designs. Extensive quantitative and qualitative results demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms existing baselines in narrative consistency, dynamic expressiveness, and perceived film quality.

</details>


### [172] [DRBENCHER: Can Your Agent Identify the Entity, Retrieve Its Properties and Do the Math?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09251)
*Young-Suk Lee,Ramon Fernandez Astudillo,Radu Florian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DRBENCHER是一个合成基准测试生成器，用于评估需要同时具备网页浏览和多步计算能力的深度研究智能体。该基准通过答案优先的流水线方法，涵盖生物化学、金融、地球物理、安全和历史五个领域，要求模型完成多跳实体识别、属性检索和领域特定计算等复杂任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试将浏览能力和计算能力分开评估，无法真实反映研究智能体在实际场景中同时运用这两种能力的水平，存在评估盲区。这导致对模型真实性能缺乏全面的衡量标准。

Method: 该研究提出统一的三段式答案优先流水线，遵循四项核心标准：(1) 可验证性——通过在知识图谱值上执行参数化代码生成参考答案；(2) 复杂性——要求多跳实体识别、属性检索和领域特定计算；(3) 难度——两阶段验证瀑布流过滤掉可由生成模型解决的题目；(4) 多样性——贪婪最大最小嵌入过滤器最大化覆盖范围。涵盖五大领域：生物化学、金融、地球物理、安全和历史。

Result: 人类评估显示76%有效性（排除过时数据后为84%），其中35%的错误源于知识图谱条目过时。最强大的前沿模型仅达到20%答案准确率。与人工构建的基准测试（BrowseComp+、MATH-500、GPQA）相比，DRBENCHER实现了最高的语义多样性。

Conclusion: 当前前沿模型在需要浏览与计算结合的任务上表现严重不足，仅20%的准确率揭示了重大能力缺陷。研究揭示了基于动态数据推理系统的固有局限性——知识图谱过时导致约三分之一的问题无法正确回答，表明该领域需要重大改进。

Abstract: Deep research agents increasingly interleave web browsing with multi-step computation, yet existing benchmarks evaluate these capabilities in isolation, creating a blind spot in assessing real-world performance. We introduce DRBENCHER, a synthetic benchmark generator for questions that require both browsing and computation. It enforces four criteria: verifiability (gold answers are computed by executing parameterized code over knowledge-graph values), complexity (multi-hop entity identification, property retrieval, and domain-specific computation), difficulty (a two-stage verification cascade filters out questions solvable by the generating model), and diversity (a greedy max-min embedding filter maximizes coverage). These criteria are realized via a unified answer-first pipeline spanning five domains: biochemistry, financial, geophysical, security, and history. Human evaluation shows 76% validity (84% excluding stale data), with 35% of errors due to outdated knowledge-graph entries, highlighting an inherent limitation of systems that reason over evolving data. Automatic evaluation shows that the strongest frontier model achieves only 20% answer accuracy. Compared to manually constructed benchmarks (BrowseComp+, MATH-500, GPQA), DRBENCHER achieves the highest semantic diversity.

</details>


### [173] [SAGE: A Service Agent Graph-guided Evaluation Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09285)
*Ling Shi,Yuqin Dai,Ziyin Wang,Ning Gao,Wei Zhang,Chaozheng Wang,Yujie Wang,Wei He,Jinpeng Wang,Deiyi Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAGE是一个用于评估LLM在客户服务场景中表现的多智能体基准测试框架，通过动态对话图形式化SOPs，并引入对抗性意图分类体系和模块化扩展机制。实验发现当前模型存在"执行差距"（意图分类正确但动作执行错误）和"共情韧性"（高压对抗下仍保持礼貌表面）现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基准测试主要依赖静态范式和单维度指标，无法反映真实部署中用户行为的多样性，也无法严格验证模型对标准操作流程(SOPs)的遵循程度，导致评估与实际应用场景脱节。

Method: 提出SAGE框架：1) 将非结构化SOPs形式化为动态对话图，实现精确的逻辑合规验证和路径覆盖分析；2) 设计对抗性意图分类体系；3) 构建模块化扩展机制以低成本跨领域部署；4) 使用Judge Agent和规则引擎分析User Agent与Service Agent的交互，生成确定性真值。

Result: 在6个工业场景下对27个LLM进行评估，发现两个关键现象：1)"执行差距"——模型能准确分类意图但无法推导出正确的后续动作；2)"共情韧性"——模型在高压对抗强度下仍维持礼貌对话表面，尽管底层逻辑已失败。

Conclusion: SAGE基准测试揭示了当前LLM在真实服务场景中的显著能力差距，为评估和改进客户服务LLM提供了系统化工具。

Abstract: The development of Large Language Models (LLMs) has catalyzed automation in customer service, yet benchmarking their performance remains challenging. Existing benchmarks predominantly rely on static paradigms and single-dimensional metrics, failing to account for diverse user behaviors or the strict adherence to structured Standard Operating Procedures (SOPs) required in real-world deployments. To bridge this gap, we propose SAGE (Service Agent Graph-guided Evaluation), a universal multi-agent benchmark for automated, dual-axis assessment. SAGE formalizes unstructured SOPs into Dynamic Dialogue Graphs, enabling precise verification of logical compliance and comprehensive path coverage. We introduce an Adversarial Intent Taxonomy and a modular Extension Mechanism, enabling low-cost deployment across domains and facilitating automated dialogue data synthesis. Evaluation is conducted via a framework where Judge Agents and a Rule Engine analyze interactions between User and Service Agents to generate deterministic ground truth. Extensive experiments on 27 LLMs across 6 industrial scenarios reveal a significant ``Execution Gap'' where models accurately classify intents but fail to derive correct subsequent actions. We also observe ``Empathy Resilience'', a phenomenon where models maintain polite conversational facades despite underlying logical failures under high adversarial intensity. Code and resources are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SAGE-Bench-4CD3/.

</details>


### [174] [Constraint-Aware Corrective Memory for Language-Based Drug Discovery Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09308)
*Maochen Sun,Youzhi Zhang,Gaofeng Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CACM是一种基于大语言模型的自主药物发现框架，通过协议审计和诊断机制解决"逐步规划 vs 整体集合评估"之间的控制问题。它将记忆分为静态、动态和纠正三个通道并压缩，保持紧凑的规划上下文。实验表明该方法比当前最佳基线提升36.4%的目标成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语言的药物发现系统面临核心控制问题：智能体逐步执行动作，但任务有效性在整体候选集合层面判定。现有方法依赖冗长的原始历史和不精确的自我反思，导致故障定位模糊、智能体状态噪声增加，限制了系统的可靠性。

Method: 提出CACM框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 协议审计(Protocol Auditing)——验证候选集是否满足协议级要求(集合大小、多样性、结合质量、成药性)；2) 扎根诊断师(Grounded Diagnostician)——跨模态分析任务需求、口袋上下文和候选集证据，定位违规并生成可操作的纠正提示；3) 三通道记忆组织——静态、动态、纠正通道分离，并在回写前压缩以保持紧凑的规划上下文。

Result: CACM在目标级成功率上比当前最佳基线提升36.4%，表明可靠的基于语言的药物发现不仅需要更强大的分子工具，更需要更精确的诊断和更经济的智能体状态。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了集合级诊断和紧凑记忆管理对自主药物发现智能体的关键价值，为构建更可靠的LLM驱动药物发现系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models are making autonomous drug discovery agents increasingly feasible, but reliable success in this setting is not determined by any single action or molecule. It is determined by whether the final returned set jointly satisfies protocol-level requirements such as set size, diversity, binding quality, and developability. This creates a fundamental control problem: the agent plans step by step, while task validity is decided at the level of the whole candidate set. Existing language-based drug discovery systems therefore tend to rely on long raw history and under-specified self-reflection, making failure localization imprecise and planner-facing agent states increasingly noisy. We present CACM (Constraint-Aware Corrective Memory), a language-based drug discovery framework built around precise set-level diagnosis and a concise memory write-back mechanism. CACM introduces protocol auditing and a grounded diagnostician, which jointly analyze multimodal evidence spanning task requirements, pocket context, and candidate-set evidence to localize protocol violations, generate actionable remediation hints, and bias the next action toward the most relevant correction. To keep planning context compact, CACM organizes memory into static, dynamic, and corrective channels and compresses them before write-back, thereby preserving persistent task information while exposing only the most decision-relevant failures. Our experimental results show that CACM improves the target-level success rate by 36.4% over the state-of-the-art baseline. The results show that reliable language-based drug discovery benefits not only from more powerful molecular tools, but also from more precise diagnosis and more economical agent states.

</details>


### [175] [Mind the Gap Between Spatial Reasoning and Acting! Step-by-Step Evaluation of Agents With Spatial-Gym](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09338)
*Lars Benedikt Kaesberg,Tianyu Yang,Niklas Bauer,Terry Ruas,Jan Philip Wahle,Bela Gipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Spatial-Gym是一个用于评估空间约束推理能力的Gymnasium环境，通过2D网格谜题中的路径规划任务，以顺序决策方式测试模型性能。最佳模型GPT-OSS 120B仅达到16.0%的解决率，远低于人类98.0%的基线水平。研究发现分步推理格式对弱模型有益但对强模型有害，视觉输入显著降低性能，而扩展思维链推理保持3-5倍准确率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理对导航和机器人技术至关重要，但现有基准测试采用一次性评估范式，与人类在交互环境中逐步推理的工作方式不符，缺乏对模型空间推理能力的有效诊断。

Method: 开发Spatial-Gym环境，将2D网格谜题路径规划建模为顺序决策任务，支持可选的回溯机制。在500个测试场景中，从一次性、分步、分步+回溯三种设置下评估8个模型，对比人类、随机和A*基线。

Result: 最佳模型GPT-OSS 120B解决率为16.0%，低于人类基线82个百分点。分步格式可帮助弱模型提升5.4%，但使强模型下降5.6%。回溯提高任务完成率但仅对弱模型提升解决率有效。

Conclusion: 三个关键发现：(1)模型未能根据难度调整推理努力；(2)视觉输入使解决率降低73%；(3)扩展思维链推理在分步设置下仍保持3-5倍准确率优势。Spatial-Gym为诊断模型局限性及通过强化学习改进空间推理提供了框架。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is central to navigation and robotics, yet measuring model capabilities on these tasks remains difficult. Existing benchmarks evaluate models in a one-shot setting, requiring full solution generation in a single response, unlike humans, who work in interactive environments step-by-step. We introduce Spatial-Gym, a Gymnasium environment that isolates spatial constraint reasoning by testing pathfinding in 2D-grid puzzles as a sequential decision task with optional backtracking. We evaluate eight models in three settings (one-shot, step-by-step, step-by-step with backtracking) against human, random, and A* baselines on 500 episodes. The best model, GPT-OSS 120B, achieves a solve rate of 16.0%, 82 points below the human baseline (98.0%). Step-by-step format helps weaker models (up to +5.4%) by removing formatting errors, but hurts stronger models (up to 5.6%) by constraining global planning. Backtracking improves episode completion, but increases solve rate only for weaker models; stronger models rarely backtrack and do not benefit from it. Our experiments have three key findings: (1) models fail to scale reasoning effort with difficulty, (2) vision models receiving images of the spatial environment reduce solve rate by 73%, and (3) extended chain-of-thought reasoning retains a 3-5x accuracy advantage over standard inference even in the step-by-step setting. Spatial-Gym enables diagnosis of model limitations and provides a framework for improving spatial reasoning through reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [176] [HiL-Bench (Human-in-Loop Benchmark): Do Agents Know When to Ask for Help?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09408)
*Mohamed Elfeki,Tu Trinh,Kelvin Luu,Guangze Luo,Nathan Hunt,Ernesto Montoya,Nandan Marwaha,Yannis He,Charles Wang,Fernando Crabedo,Alessa Castilo,Bing Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HiL-Bench是一个用于评估AI编程智能体在信息不完整时是否主动寻求帮助能力的基准测试。当前评估标准忽视了这一关键能力，导致模型可能因"幸运猜对"而获得高分。核心指标Ask-F1通过谐波平均同时衡量问题精准率和障碍召回率，防止通过"问题轰炸"作弊。评估揭示了前沿模型存在严重的"判断鸿沟"——信息不完整时性能大幅下降。强化学习训练可改善这一能力，且学到的并非领域特定启发式规则，而是通用的不确定性检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码智能体评估基准存在根本性缺陷：它们提供完整、无歧义的操作指令，仅奖励最终执行正确性。这导致评估无法区分"真正理解需求后正确执行"和"对缺失信息做出幸运猜测"两种截然不同的能力。现实场景中，用户需求往往包含缺失信息、歧义请求或矛盾约束，因此智能体必须在不确定时主动寻求澄清——这种"选择性升级（selective escalation）"能力是当前benchmark的盲区。

Method: 设计HiL-Bench基准，每个任务包含需通过渐进式探索才能发现的"blocker"（障碍），涵盖缺失信息、歧义请求、矛盾信息三类。每个障碍由人类验证确认。提出Ask-F1作为核心指标——问题精准率与障碍召回率的谐波均值，其数学结构内在防止通过大量提问来刷分。评估覆盖SWE（软件工程）和text-to-SQL两个领域。

Result: 发现所有前沿模型均存在巨大的"判断鸿沟"：相比完整信息条件，性能在需要自主判断时大幅下降且无法恢复。失败分析揭示三种一致的错误模式：1）过度自信——持有错误信念但无法检测差距；2）检测到高不确定性但持续出错；3）泛化过度——提问宽泛且不精确，缺乏自我修正。这表明问题源于模型层面的通用缺陷而非特定任务。RL训练中，32B模型在Ask-F1奖励 shaping下同时提升了问题质量和任务通过率，且改善效果可跨领域迁移。

Conclusion: 帮助寻求能力的缺陷是模型级别的共性问题而非任务特例。通过强化学习训练，模型可习得该能力——关键在于学会检测不可解决的不确定性并据此行动，而非习得特定领域的提问规则。Ask-F1Reward shaping是训练选择性升级能力的有效范式。

Abstract: Frontier coding agents solve complex tasks when given complete context but collapse when specifications are incomplete or ambiguous. The bottleneck is not raw capability, but judgment: knowing when to act autonomously and when to ask for help. Current benchmarks are blind to this failure mode. They supply unambiguous detailed instructions and solely reward execution correctness, so an agent that makes a lucky guess for a missing requirement will score identically to one that would have asked to be certain.
  We present HiL-Bench (Human-in-the-Loop Benchmark) to measure this selective escalation skill. Each task contains human-validated blockers (missing information, ambiguous requests, contradictory information) that surface only through progressive exploration, not upfront inspection. Our core metric, Ask-F1, the harmonic mean of question precision and blocker recall, captures the tension between over-asking and silent guessing; its structure architecturally prevents gaming through question spam.
  Evaluation across SWE and text-to-SQL domains reveals a large universal judgment gap: no frontier model recovers more than a fraction of its full-information performance when deciding whether to ask. Failure analysis identifies three key help-seeking patterns: overconfident wrong beliefs with no gap detection; high uncertainty detection yet persistent errors; broad, imprecise escalation without self-correction. These consistent patterns confirm poor help-seeking is a model-level flaw, not task-specific. RL training on shaped Ask-F1 reward shows judgment is trainable: a 32B model improves both help-seeking quality and task pass rate, with gains that transfer across domains. The model does not learn domain-specific heuristics for when to ask; it learns to detect unresolvable uncertainty and act on it.

</details>


### [177] [Do We Really Need to Approach the Entire Pareto Front in Many-Objective Bayesian Optimisation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09417)
*Chao Jiang,Jingyu Huang,Miqing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对有限评估预算下的多目标贝叶斯优化问题，提出SPMO框架及ESPI采集函数，转变传统思路从近似完整帕累托前沿到直接搜索最优单解，兼顾理论收敛性与实践有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多目标贝叶斯优化方法试图逼近整个帕累托前沿，但在评估预算极其有限（通常仅数百次评估）且决策者最终仅需一个解决方案的情况下，这种做法效率低下。因此，研究思路转向直接寻找单一高质量解而非近似整个前沿。

Method: 提出SPMO（单点式多目标搜索框架），其中包含ESPI（期望单点改进）采集函数。该函数可在样本平均近似（SAA）框架下使用梯度方法优化，且具备理论收敛保证。

Result: 实验表明SPMO在计算上可行，并在多种基准测试和真实世界问题上超越了当前最优方法。

Conclusion: SPMO框架在有限评估预算下相比现有方法表现更优，兼具计算可扩展性和理论收敛保证，可应用于基准测试和真实世界问题。

Abstract: Many-objective optimisation, a subset of multi-objective optimisation, involves optimisation problems with more than three objectives. As the number of objectives increases, the number of solutions needed to adequately represent the entire Pareto front typically grows substantially. This makes it challenging, if not infeasible, to design a search algorithm capable of effectively exploring the entire Pareto front. This difficulty is particularly acute in the Bayesian optimisation paradigm, where sample efficiency is critical and only a limited number of solutions (often a few hundred) are evaluated. Moreover, after the optimisation process, the decision-maker eventually selects just one solution for deployment, regardless of how many high-quality, diverse solutions are available. In light of this, we argue an idea that under a very limited evaluation budget, it may be more useful to focus on finding a single solution of the highest possible quality for the decision-maker, rather than aiming to approximate the entire Pareto front as existing many-/multi-objective Bayesian optimisation methods typically do. Bearing this idea in mind, this paper proposes a \underline{s}ingle \underline{p}oint-based \underline{m}ulti-\underline{o}bjective search framework (SPMO) that aims to improve the quality of solutions along a direction that leads to a good tradeoff between objectives. Within SPMO, we present a simple acquisition function, called expected single-point improvement (ESPI), working under both noiseless and noisy scenarios. We show that ESPI can be optimised effectively with gradient-based methods via the sample average approximation (SAA) approach and theoretically prove its convergence guarantees under the SAA. We also empirically demonstrate that the proposed SPMO is computationally tractable and outperforms state-of-the-arts on a wide range of benchmark and real-world problems.

</details>


### [178] [E3-TIR: Enhanced Experience Exploitation for Tool-Integrated Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09455)
*Weiyang Guo,Zesheng Shi,Liye Zhao,Jiayuan Ma,Zeen Zhu,Junxian He,Min Zhang,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出E3-TIR（增强经验利用）框架，通过动态整合专家前缀、专家引导和自我探索三种经验类型，解决大语言模型工具集成推理训练中的效率低下和数据成本高的问题。相比传统训练范式，在只使用不到10%合成数据的情况下获得6%的性能提升，ROI提升1.46倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM工具集成推理(TIR)训练范式存在显著局限：Zero-RL因缺乏先验指导导致探索效率低和模式退化；SFT-then-RL因低熵崩塌面临高数据成本和能力瓶颈。本文旨在提出一种能在训练早期阶段高效利用经验、平衡探索多样性与训练效率的暖身训练范式。

Method: 提出E3-TIR框架，将训练形式化为三类经验的动态整合：(1)专家前缀(Expert Prefixes)；(2)专家引导(Expert Guided)；(3)自我探索(Self-Exploration)。通过在专家"锚点"周围执行多样化分支探索，并采用混合策略优化机制，有效缓解分布偏移和解决共享前缀带来的优化冲突，实现模型知识边界的动态适应。

Result: 在工具使用任务上，E3-TIR相比传统训练范式实现6%的性能提升，同时仅需使用不到10%的合成数据。在综合考量性能、数据成本和训练效率的ROI指标上，获得1.46倍于基线的提升。

Conclusion: E3-TIR通过动态整合多类型经验有效解决了TIR训练中的探索效率和数据成本问题，为LLM智能体训练提供了一种高效且经济的暖身训练范式。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated significant potential in Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR), existing training paradigms face significant limitations: Zero-RL suffers from inefficient exploration and mode degradation due to a lack of prior guidance, while SFT-then-RL is limited by high data costs and capability plateaus caused by low-entropy collapse. To address these challenges, we propose E3-TIR (Enhanced Experience Exploitation), a warm-up paradigm for the early stages of agent training. Specifically, we formulate training as the dynamic integration of three experience types: Expert Prefixes, Expert Guided, and Self-Exploration. By executing diverse branching exploration around expert "anchors" and employing a mix policy optimization mechanism, we effectively mitigate distribution shifts and resolve optimization conflicts arising from shared prefixes. Our method dynamically adapts the model's knowledge boundaries, effectively balancing exploration diversity with training efficiency.Experimental results demonstrate that E3-TIR achieves a 6 performance improvement over traditional paradigms on tool-use tasks, while requiring less than 10 of the synthetic data. Furthermore, in terms of ROI, a comprehensive metric integrating performance, data cost, and training efficiency we achieve a 1.46x gain compared to baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/yuki-younai/E3-TIR.

</details>


### [179] [Process Reward Agents for Steering Knowledge-Intensive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09482)
*Jiwoong Sohn,Tomasz Sternal,Kenneth Styppa,Torsten Hoefler,Michael Moor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Process Reward Agents (PRA)，一种测试时方法，通过领域接地、在线、逐步奖励机制增强冷冻策略模型的推理能力。PRA支持基于搜索的解码，可在每个生成步骤对候选轨迹进行排序和剪枝。在医学推理基准测试中，PRA使用Qwen3-4B达到80.8%的准确率，为4B参数规模的最优水平，并能泛化至0.5B到8B参数的未见模型，准确率提升高达25.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识密集型领域的推理面临挑战：中间步骤难以局部验证（不同于数学或代码），评估步骤正确性需跨大型外部知识源综合线索，导致细微错误可能传播且不被检测。现有过程奖励模型(PRM)及检索增强变体均为事后评分，无法集成到动态推理流程中。

Method: 提出Process Reward Agents (PRA)方法：(1) 冻结策略模型 + 领域接地奖励模块的解耦范式；(2) 支持基于搜索的解码，在每个生成步骤对候选轨迹进行排序和剪枝；(3) 提供领域接地、在线、逐步奖励信号。

Result: MedQA达到80.8%准确率（Qwen3-4B），4B参数规模SOTA；泛化至0.5B-8B参数的未见模型，准确率提升高达25.7%。

Conclusion: PRA实现了冷冻推理器与领域特定奖励模块的解耦，允许在不重新训练的情况下将新骨干模型部署到复杂领域，无需更新策略模型即可提升推理能力。

Abstract: Reasoning in knowledge-intensive domains remains challenging as intermediate steps are often not locally verifiable: unlike math or code, evaluating step correctness may require synthesizing clues across large external knowledge sources. As a result, subtle errors can propagate through reasoning traces, potentially never to be detected. Prior work has proposed process reward models (PRMs), including retrieval-augmented variants, but these methods operate post hoc, scoring completed trajectories, which prevents their integration into dynamic inference procedures. Here, we introduce Process Reward Agents (PRA), a test-time method for providing domain-grounded, online, step-wise rewards to a frozen policy. In contrast to prior retrieval-augmented PRMs, PRA enables search-based decoding to rank and prune candidate trajectories at every generation step. Experiments on multiple medical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that PRA consistently outperforms strong baselines, achieving 80.8% accuracy on MedQA with Qwen3-4B, a new state of the art at the 4B scale. Importantly, PRA generalizes to unseen frozen policy models ranging from 0.5B to 8B parameters, improving their accuracy by up to 25.7% without any policy model updates. More broadly, PRA suggests a paradigm in which frozen reasoners are decoupled from domain-specific reward modules, allowing the deployment of new backbones in complex domains without retraining.

</details>


### [180] [SUPERNOVA: Eliciting General Reasoning in LLMs with Reinforcement Learning on Natural Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08477)
*Ashima Suvarna,Kendrick Phan,Mehrab Beikzadeh,Hritik Bansal,Saadia Gabriel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SUPERNOVA框架通过系统性分析指令微调数据集中的专家标注推理模式，为可验证奖励的强化学习(RLVR)构建高质量训练数据，聚焦源任务选择、任务混合策略和合成数据增强三个关键因素，在多个推理基准上实现显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR已在数学和代码等正式领域取得突破，但在需要因果推理、时间理解等通用推理任务上仍面临挑战。核心瓶颈在于缺乏覆盖多样化推理技能的高质量可验证训练数据。

Method: 设计SUPERNOVA数据筛选框架，对100+组RL实验进行对照分析，系统研究三个关键因素：①源任务选择策略；②任务混合方法；③数据质量提升的合成干预手段。将指令微调数据集中的人类标注推理模式转化为RLVR格式。

Result: SUPERNOVA训练的模型在BBEH、Zebralogic、MMLU-Pro等困难推理基准上超越Qwen3.5等强基线，尤其在BBEH上实现最高52.8%的相对提升。

Conclusion: 基于人类标注资源的原则性数据筛选对将RLVR扩展至通用推理具有重要价值。源任务选择非平凡且对下游推理性能影响显著；基于单个目标任务表现选择源任务策略优于基于总体平均表现的策略。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has significantly improved large language model (LLM) reasoning in formal domains such as mathematics and code. Despite these advancements, LLMs still struggle with general reasoning tasks requiring capabilities such as causal inference and temporal understanding. Extending RLVR to general reasoning is fundamentally constrained by the lack of high-quality, verifiable training data that spans diverse reasoning skills. To address this challenge, we propose SUPERNOVA, a data curation framework for RLVR aimed at enhancing general reasoning. Our key insight is that instruction-tuning datasets containing expert-annotated ground-truth encode rich reasoning patterns that can be systematically adapted for RLVR. To study this, we conduct 100+ controlled RL experiments to analyze how data design choices impact downstream reasoning performance. In particular, we investigate three key factors: (i) source task selection, (ii) task mixing strategies, and (iii) synthetic interventions for improving data quality. Our analysis reveals that source task selection is non-trivial and has a significant impact on downstream reasoning performance. Moreover, selecting tasks based on their performance for individual target tasks outperforms strategies based on overall average performance. Finally, models trained on SUPERNOVA outperform strong baselines (e.g., Qwen3.5) on challenging reasoning benchmarks including BBEH, Zebralogic, and MMLU-Pro. In particular, training on SUPERNOVA yields relative improvements of up to 52.8\% on BBEH across model sizes, demonstrating the effectiveness of principled data curation for RLVR. Our findings provide practical insights for curating human-annotated resources to extend RLVR to general reasoning. The code and data is available at https://github.com/asuvarna31/supernova.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Automated Standardization of Legacy Biomedical Metadata Using an Ontology-Constrained LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08552)
*Josef Hardi,Martin J. O'Connor,Marcos Martinez-Romero,Jean G. Rosario,Stephen A. Fisher,Mark A. Musen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于大语言模型(LLM)的生物医学元数据标准化系统，通过实时查询权威术语服务获取规范词汇，显著提升元数据填写准确率，为生物医学数据的FAIR原则提供实用方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学元数据常不完整且不符合社区标准，限制了数据集的可发现性、互操作性和重用性。现有指南缺乏机器可执行表示，传统LLM方法将约束作为静态文本提示，仅依赖模型训练知识，难以获取最新规范术语。

Method: 开发基于LLM的元数据标准化系统，实时查询权威生物医学术语服务(如UMLS、SNOMED CT等)获取规范词汇；使用839条来自Human BioMolecular Atlas Program (HuBMAP)的遗留元数据记录进行评估，以专家标注的金标准进行精确匹配评估。

Result: 实时工具调用增强的LLM在本体约束字段和非本体约束字段上均持续提升预测准确率，优于仅使用LLM的方法。

Conclusion: 该方法为生物医学元数据自动化标准化提供了一种实用、可扩展的解决方案，有效结合LLM推理能力与实时术语服务验证。

Abstract: Scientific metadata are often incomplete and noncompliant with community standards, limiting dataset findability, interoperability, and reuse. When reporting guidelines exist, they typically lack machine-actionable representations. Producing FAIR datasets requires encoding metadata standards as machine-actionable templates with rich field specifications and precise value constraints. Recent work has shown that LLMs guided by field names and ontology constraints can improve metadata standardization, but these approaches treat constraints as static text prompts, relying on the model's training knowledge alone. We present an LLM-based metadata standardization system that queries authoritative biomedical terminology services in real time to retrieve canonically correct vocabulary terms on demand. We evaluate this approach on 839 legacy metadata records from the Human BioMolecular Atlas Program (HuBMAP) using an expert-curated gold standard for exact-match assessment. Our evaluation shows that augmenting the LLM with real-time tool access consistently improves prediction accuracy over the LLM alone across both ontology-constrained and non-ontology-constrained fields, demonstrating a practical, scalable approach to automated standardization of biomedical metadata.

</details>


### [182] [QCFuse: Query-Centric Cache Fusion for Efficient RAG Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08585)
*Jianxin Yan,Zeheng Qian,Wangze Ni,Zhitao Shen,Zhiping Wang,Haoyang Li,Jia Zhu,Lei Chen,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: QCFuse是一个以用户查询为中心的KV缓存融合系统，通过语义摘要锚点增强查询表示，并选择性重计算相关token，在保持精度的同时将LLM响应效率提升40%，同时具备注意力降噪效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖局部视角进行token选择，缺乏用户查询的全局感知能力。获取上下文感知的查询表示成本高昂，且高效注意力分析需要严格的流水线约束，导致全局感知难以实现。

Method: QCFuse利用语义摘要锚点增强查询表示，选择性重计算与查询相关的token，并基于最关键Transformer层的注意力分布更新token，以保持流水线结构的高效性。

Result: 在真实数据集上的评估表明，QCFuse在保持同等精度的前提下，显著提升LLM响应效率达40%。此外，在某些场景下，QCFuse还实现了注意力降噪效果，产生了更高的响应准确率。

Conclusion: QCFuse展示了在LLM推理优化方面的巨大潜力，通过平衡效率与精度，为RAG系统中配备KV缓存的LLM提供了有效的优化方案。

Abstract: Cache fusion accelerates generation process of LLMs equipped with RAG through KV caching and selective token recomputation, thereby reducing computational costs and improving efficiency. However, existing methods primarily rely on local perspectives for token selection and lack global awareness from the user query. Utilizing this global awareness is challenging due to the high cost of obtaining context-aware query representations and the strict pipeline constraints required for efficient attention analysis. Thus, this demonstration introduces QCFuse, an innovative KV cache fusion system centered on the user query. QCFuse leverages semantic summary anchors to enhance query representations and selectively recomputes query-related tokens to improve accuracy, updating tokens based on the attention distribution of the most critical Transformer layer to preserve the high efficiency of the pipeline structure. Evaluations on real-world datasets demonstrate that QCFuse significantly improves the response efficiency of LLMs by 40\% while maintaining equivalent accuracy compared to current methods. Additionally, in certain scenarios, QCFuse achieves an attention denoising effect that yields higher response accuracy, demonstrating substantial potential in the optimization of LLM inference.

</details>


### [183] [Evaluating Data Quality Tools: Measurement Capabilities and LLM Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09163)
*Tobias Rehberger,Thomas Hütter,Lisa Ehrlinger,Wolfram Wöß*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文评估了六款数据质量工具（Great Expectations、Deequ、Evidently、Informatica、Experian、Ataccama），从规则定义、重复检测、指标聚合和不确定性处理四个维度进行比较，并考察了各工具对大语言模型（LLM）的集成程度。研究发现：商业工具功能更全面且已引入LLM辅助功能，开源工具灵活性高但实施成本大；所有工具的LLM集成均局限于规则创建工作流，尚未支持直接的数据验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据质量对分析可靠性和运营效率至关重要，但市场上数据质量工具众多且功能差异显著，亟需系统性的评估框架来指导工具选型。

Method: 基于合作伙伴的真实用例，提炼出四项评估标准（规则定义、重复检测、指标聚合、不确定性处理），对六款主流数据质量工具进行对比分析。

Result: 商业工具提供更全面的测量功能和新兴的LLM辅助能力，开源工具则以更高实施成本换取灵活性；所有工具的LLM集成均限于规则创建。

Conclusion: 当前数据质量工具尚未实现通过LLM进行直接数据验证的能力，LLM在数据质量领域的应用仍处于早期阶段。

Abstract: High data quality is critical for reliable analytics and operational efficiency. A growing ecosystem of tools has emerged to support data quality management, ranging from lightweight open-source libraries to comprehensive enterprise platforms. This paper evaluates six data quality tools: Great Expectations, Deequ, Evidently, Informatica, Experian, and Ataccama. The evaluation criteria cover rule definition, duplicate detection, metric aggregation, and uncertainty handling, and were derived from real-world use cases of company partners. We further examine to what extent these tools integrate Large Language Models (LLMs). Our findings show that proprietary tools offer more comprehensive measurement features and emerging LLM-based assistance, while open-source tools provide flexibility at the cost of higher implementation effort. Across all tools, LLM integration remains limited to rule creation workflows. Direct data validation through LLMs is not yet supported by any of the evaluated tools.

</details>


### [184] [Decoupling Vector Data and Index Storage for Space Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09173)
*Yuanming Ren,Juncheng Zhang,Yanjing Ren,Rui Yang,Di Wu,Patrick P. C. Lee*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: DecoupleVS通过解耦向量数据与索引元数据，优化存储和查询性能，在十亿级数据集上实现存储空间降低58.7%，同时保持高效搜索和更新性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的ANNS系统中，向量数据与索引元数据的共置导致存储开销大、查询时读取过多、更新时写入放大严重。

Method: 提出DecoupleVS框架，对向量数据和索引元数据进行解耦管理，采用专门的压缩技术、数据布局设计、查询优化和更新策略。

Result: 在真实十亿级公开和私有数据集上测试，存储空间降低达58.7%，搜索和更新性能达到或超越现有最先进的单片式磁盘ANNS系统。

Conclusion: 解耦策略可有效平衡存储效率与查询性能，为大规模向量数据库提供可行的优化方向。

Abstract: Managing large-scale vector datasets with disk-based approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) systems faces critical efficiency challenges stemming from the co-location of vector data and auxiliary index metadata. Our analysis of state-of-the-art ANNS systems reveals that such co-location incurs substantial storage overhead, generates excessive reads during search queries, and causes severe write amplification during updates. We present DecoupleVS, a decoupled vector storage management framework that enables specialized optimizations for vector data and auxiliary index metadata. DecoupleVS incorporates various design techniques for effective compression, data layouts, search queries, and updates, so as to significantly reduce storage space, while maintaining high search and update performance and high search accuracy. Evaluation on real-world public and proprietary billion-scale datasets shows that DecoupleVS reduces storage space by up to 58.7\%, while delivering competitive or improved search query and update performance, compared to state-of-the-art monolithic disk-based ANNS systems.

</details>


### [185] [A Catalog of Data Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09277)
*Divya Bhadauria,Hazar Harmouch,Felix Naumann,Divesh Srivastava,Lisa Ehrlinger*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个包含35种错误类型的综合目录，用于表格数据，涵盖数据错误（如缺失值、重复元组）和错误指标（如异常值、偏差），分为三类：缺失、错误和冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界数据库中存在大量数据错误，严重影响机器学习管道和业务分析报告等下游应用。现有错误定义和分类方案价值有限，主要是非正式的，且未覆盖所有错误类型。随着AI发展，统计错误（如偏差、异常值）需被纳入考虑，但现有分类体系很少涉及。

Method: 系统梳理并整合现有相关工作，提出包含35种不同错误类型的综合目录，对表格数据进行分类。建立了三个互不重叠的类别：缺失类（missing）、错误类（incorrect）和冗余类（redundant）。为每种错误类型提供形式化定义和实践案例，并解决了相关工作中的术语不一致问题。

Result: 建立了包含35种错误类型的全面目录，涵盖传统数据错误（缺失值、重复元组、约束违规）和统计错误指标（异常值、偏差），对表格数据进行了系统分类。

Conclusion: 该目录使研究者和实践者能够针对不同错误类型，采用系统化的错误特定检测和清洗策略，实现数据质量工具的改进。

Abstract: Data errors are widespread in real-world databases and severely impact downstream applications, such as machine learning pipelines or business analytics reports. Causes of such errors are manifold and can arise during both the design phase and the operational phase of a database. Some error types, such as missing values, duplicate tuples, or constraint violations, are widely recognized; others, such as disguised missing values or word transpositions, remain underexplored. Existing attempts to define and classify errors in data offer valuable but limited taxonomies, mostly informal and not covering the full range of error types. With the rise of AI, practitioners must increasingly detect and correct statistical errors such as bias and outliers, which are rarely considered within existing error taxonomies. This catalog presents a comprehensive list of 35 distinct error types, including both data errors (e.g., missing values, duplicate tuples) and error indicators (e.g., outliers, bias) for tabular data, classified into three non-overlapping categories: missing, incorrect, and redundant. For each error type, we provide a formal definition and practical example, and resolve terminological inconsistencies across related work. Our catalog enables researchers and practitioners to address various error types and systematically implement error-specific detection and cleaning strategies in data quality tools.

</details>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Beyond the Individual: Virtualizing Multi-Disciplinary Reasoning for Clinical Intake via Collaborative Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08927)
*Huangwei Chen,Wu Li,Junhao Jia,Yining Chen,Xiaotao Pang,Ya-Long Chen,Li Gonghui,Haishuai Wang,Jiajun Bu,Lei Wu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Aegle，一个基于图结构多智能体架构的同步虚拟MDT（多学科团队）框架，旨在将MDT级别的诊疗推理能力引入门诊首诊场景。该框架通过SOAP结构化表示形式化诊疗状态，将证据收集与诊断推理解耦，从而提升可追溯性和偏差控制。Orchestrator动态激活专科智能体执行并行推理，再由Aggregator整合为连贯的临床记录。在ClinicalBench和真实数据集RAPID-IPN上的实验表明，Aegle在24个科室、53项指标上均优于现有最先进的闭源和开源模型，同时提升了最终诊断准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 门诊首诊通常由单一医生在时间压力下完成，容易产生认知偏差和证据捕获不完整的问题。虽然多学科团队（MDT）可以降低这些风险，但成本高昂且难以扩展到实时门诊场景。因此，需要一种可扩展的解决方案，将MDT级别的诊疗推理能力引入日常门诊咨询。

Method: Aegle采用图结构的多智能体架构，包含三个核心组件：(1) SOAP结构化表示——将诊疗状态形式化，分离证据收集与诊断推理；(2) Orchestrator（编排器）——动态激活专科智能体；(3) Aggregator（聚合器）——将并行推理结果整合为连贯临床记录。各专科智能体执行解耦的并行推理，提高效率和可扩展性。

Result: 在ClinicalBench和真实世界的RAPID-IPN数据集上，覆盖24个科室、53项指标的实验表明：Aegle在文档质量和咨询能力方面始终优于现有的最先进的专有模型和开源模型，同时提升了最终诊断准确率。

Conclusion: Aegle通过创新的图结构多智能体架构和SOAP结构化表示，成功将MDT级别的诊疗推理能力引入门诊场景，兼顾了诊疗质量与可扩展性，为AI辅助临床决策提供了新的范式。

Abstract: The initial outpatient consultation is critical for clinical decision-making, yet it is often conducted by a single physician under time pressure, making it prone to cognitive biases and incomplete evidence capture. Although the Multi-Disciplinary Team (MDT) reduces these risks, they are costly and difficult to scale to real-time intake. We propose Aegle, a synchronous virtual MDT framework that brings MDT-level reasoning to outpatient consultations via a graph-based multi-agent architecture. Aegle formalizes the consultation state using a structured SOAP representation, separating evidence collection from diagnostic reasoning to improve traceability and bias control. An orchestrator dynamically activates specialist agents, which perform decoupled parallel reasoning and are subsequently integrated by an aggregator into a coherent clinical note. Experiments on ClinicalBench and a real-world RAPID-IPN dataset across 24 departments and 53 metrics show that Aegle consistently outperforms state-of-the-art proprietary and open-source models in documentation quality and consultation capability, while also improving final diagnosis accuracy. Our code is available at https://github.com/HovChen/Aegle.

</details>


### [187] [Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning for Low-Carbon P2P Energy Trading among Self-Interested Microgrids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08973)
*Junhao Ren,Honglin Gao,Lan Zhao,Qiyu Kang,Gaoxi Xiao,Yajuan Sun*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出基于多智能体强化学习的微电网P2P电力交易框架，通过价格和电量双维度竞价策略优化储能套利，实现可再生能源消纳提升和碳排放降低的双赢目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生能源发电和需求的不确定性给日前调度带来挑战，需提高可再生能源渗透率并维持日内平衡。

Method: 开发多智能体强化学习框架，微电网独立竞价（价格和电量），在时变主网电价下优化储能套利；提出市场出清机制协调交易并促进激励相容。

Result: 学习的竞价策略提高可再生能源利用率，减少对高碳电力的依赖，提升社区层面经济福利。

Conclusion: 实现减排与本地繁荣的双赢局面。

Abstract: Uncertainties in renewable generation and demand dynamics challenge day-ahead scheduling. To enhance renewable penetration and maintain intra-day balance, we develop a multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for self-interested microgrids participating in peer-to-peer (P2P) electricity trading. Each microgrid independently bids both price and quantity while optimizing its own profit via storage arbitrage under time-varying main-grid prices. A market-clearing mechanism coordinating trades and promoting incentive compatibility is proposed. Simulation results show that the learned bidding policy improves renewable utilization and reduces reliance on high-carbon electricity, while increasing community-level economic welfare, delivering a win-win situation in emission reduction and local prosperity.

</details>


### [188] [Risk-seeking conservative policy iteration with agent-state based policies for Dec-POMDPs with guaranteed convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09495)
*Amit Sinha,Matthieu Geist,Aditya Mahajan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Optimally solving decentralized decision-making problems modeled as Dec-POMDPs is known to be NEXP-complete. These optimal solutions are policies based on the entire history of observations and actions of an agent. However, some applications may require more compact policies because of limited compute capabilities, which can be modeled by considering a limited number of memory states (or agent states). While such an agent-state based policy class may not contain the optimal solution, it is still of practical interest to find the best agent-state policy within the class. We focus on an iterated best response style algorithm which guarantees monotonic improvements and convergence to a local optimum in polynomial runtime in the Dec-POMDP model size. In order to obtain a better local optimum, we use a modified objective which incentivizes risk-seeking alongside a conservative policy iteration update. Our empirical results show that our approach performs as well as state-of-the-art approaches on several benchmark Dec-POMDPs, achieving near-optimal performance while having polynomial runtime despite the limited memory. We also show that using more agent states (a larger memory) leads to greater performance. Our approach provides a novel way of incorporating memory constraints on the agents in the Dec-POMDP problem.

</details>


### [189] [Aligned Agents, Biased Swarm: Measuring Bias Amplification in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08963)
*Keyu Li,Jin Gao,Dequan Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文研究多智能体系统（MAS）中偏见的形成机制，发现结构化工作流会像"回音壁"一样放大微小偏差，即使单个智能体保持中立，系统性偏见仍会累积甚至加剧。引入了Discrim-Eval-Open基准测试来评估偏见级联，并发现了"触发脆弱性"现象——注入客观上下文反而加速极化。研究结论是架构复杂性不能保证伦理稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统在复杂工作流中的应用日益广泛，但其涌现特性（尤其是偏见的累积）尚不清楚。现实世界的MAS过于复杂难以完全分析，需要先隔离其基础机制进行研究。传统观点认为多智能体协作会自然稀释偏见，但研究者对此提出质疑。

Method: 研究团队引入Discrim-Eval-Open基准测试，通过强制跨人口统计学群体的比较判断来绕过个体模型中立性，评估偏见级联在各种MAS结构中的传播情况。研究剥离了高级群体复杂性，聚焦于基础动态分析。

Result: 研究发现：1）建筑复杂性往往加剧而非缓解偏见；2）即使孤立运行的智能体保持中立，系统性放大效应仍会出现；3）发现了"触发脆弱性"现象——注入纯粹客观上下文反而大幅加速极化。

Conclusion: 结构复杂性不能保证伦理稳健性。论文建立了一个关键基线：研究多智能体系统的基础动态而非高级复杂性，为理解MAS中的偏见机制提供了重要参考。

Abstract: While Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) are increasingly deployed for complex workflows, their emergent properties-particularly the accumulation of bias-remain poorly understood. Because real-world MAS are too complex to analyze entirely, evaluating their ethical robustness requires first isolating their foundational mechanics. In this work, we conduct a baseline empirical study investigating how basic MAS topologies and feedback loops influence prejudice. Contrary to the assumption that multi-agent collaboration naturally dilutes bias, we hypothesize that structured workflows act as echo chambers, amplifying minor stochastic biases into systemic polarization. To evaluate this, we introduce Discrim-Eval-Open, an open-ended benchmark that bypasses individual model neutrality through forced comparative judgments across demographic groups. Analyzing bias cascades across various structures reveals that architectural sophistication frequently exacerbates bias rather than mitigating it. We observe systemic amplification even when isolated agents operate neutrally, and identify a 'Trigger Vulnerability' where injecting purely objective context drastically accelerates polarization. By stripping away advanced swarm complexity to study foundational dynamics, we establish a crucial baseline: structural complexity does not guarantee ethical robustness. Our code is available at https://github.com/weizhihao1/MAS-Bias.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Some variations of the secretary problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08593)
*Sarthak Agrawal,Sanjeev Saxena*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 两篇论文分别研究了"返回秘书问题"的变体（允许候选人以固定概率重新出现）和"宽松成功标准秘书问题"（成功定义为选中前三名之一而非仅限最佳），通过阈值策略分析最优决策规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典秘书问题假设每位候选人一出现便被决策且永不返回。本文动机：1）现实决策中候选人会重复出现，需要利用额外信息优化选择；2）实际场景中"成功"标准可能是选中前几名之一，而非必须选中最佳，因此需要研究放宽成功标准的影响。

Method: 1）针对返回问题：建立随机模型表征候选人以概率p重新出现的序列决策过程，推导最优阈值规则的解析特性。2）针对宽松成功问题：采用经典阈值策略（拒绝前r个候选人），通过概率分析估计选中前三的成功率。

Result: 1）返回问题：确定了最优阈值规则的结构，证明了重新出现概率p如何改善选择性能——更多重复信息可提高选中优质候选人的概率。2）宽松成功问题：选中前三的成功概率显著高于仅选最佳的情况，且最优停止阈值相比经典问题明显前移。

Conclusion: 1）允许候选人重新出现是有价值的决策改进策略，概率p越大，优化效果越明显。2）将成功标准从"选最佳"放宽至"选前三"大幅提升成功概率，表明在候选人间相似度较高的场景下可采用更早停止策略，平衡风险与收益。

Abstract: We consider two variations of the classical secretary problem.
  * A variation of the returning secretary problem where each interviewee may appear a second time with a fixed probability p. The decision-maker observes interviewees sequentially and must choose whether to accept or reject each appearance. We characterize the optimal threshold rule and examine its dependence on the reappearance probability p, highlighting how additional information from repeated appearances improves selection performance.
  * A variation of the secretary problem in which success is defined as selecting any one of the top three interviewees rather than the single best. Interviewees are observed sequentially in random order, and decisions are irreversible. We estimated the success probability under this relaxed success criterion using the threshold strategy of the classical secretary problem. The results show that allowing selection among the top three significantly increases the success probability and shifts the optimal stopping threshold earlier than in the classical problem. This model provides insight into realistic decision-making scenarios where top interviewees are more or less similar.

</details>


### [191] [Speed Thrills: Visceral Demonstrations That Get Students Excited About Efficient Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08938)
*Alistair Moffat,David Hawking*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出"算法惊险"(thrills of algorithms)教学方法,通过两个简单问题的逐步优化演示,让运行时间从"天/年"级降至"秒"级,以直观震撼的方式激发学生对算法效率的重视。论文提供详细的伪代码和复杂度分析,便于教师在课堂上实时演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据结构和算法课程中,学生往往难以理解高效算法的重要性。教师需要一种直观、有冲击力且难以忘怀的方式来展示算法优化的价值。

Method: 设计两个简单问题,每个问题展示一系列从低效到高效的算法改进序列,并在课堂上实时演示这些改进带来的运行时间戏剧性下降。

Result: 运行时间从估计的"天"甚至"年"级别显著降低到仅需"几秒",这种视觉冲击力能有效传达高效算法的重要性。

Conclusion: 该方法特别有效,因为可以在讲师的笔记本电脑上现场演示,且研究者提供了详细的伪代码和复杂度分析以供教学使用。

Abstract: We address the problem of motivating students in Data Structures and Algorithms courses by presenting two simple problems that each have a series of improvements to a basic algorithm, leading to spectacular decreases in runtimes. Coining a new term, we refer to such sequences as being "thrills of algorithms". Seeing runtimes drop from an estimate of days (or even years) to just a few seconds has a visceral impact which conveys the importance of efficient algorithms in a way unlikely to be forgotten. The demonstrations are particularly compelling because they can be performed live in class on the lecturer's laptop. To assist staff teaching such courses we provide detailed pseudocode descriptions and complexity analyses for the various methods, and can supply implementations on request.

</details>


### [192] [Packing Compact Subgraphs with Applications to Districting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09522)
*Ho-Lin Chen,Po-Yu Chou,Prathamesh Dharangutte,Jie Gao,Shang-En Huang,Fang-Yi Yu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究专注于图论中的不交连通过子图打包问题，应用于政治选区划分。核心贡献是将平衡星形选区的近似比从O(log n)提升至O(1)，并将结果从平面图扩展到minor-free图和有界扩张图类。对于半径-k选区也获得O(1)近似比，并通过参数松弛实现(1+ε)近似。此外，证明最小权重阈值可替代平衡性约束，并给出相关硬度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治选区划分应用中需要满足紧凑性（常数半径）和组合约束（如人口阈值或政党平衡）的不交连通子图。现有算法近似比O(log n)较差，适用范围限于平面图，需要改进算法性能和扩展图类覆盖范围。

Method: 使用精化分析（refined analysis）改进原算法，实现常数近似比；通过图论结构性理论（如minor-free图、有界扩张图的结构性质）扩展结果适用范围；引入平衡性参数松弛技术实现(1+ε)近似。

Result: 1. 平衡星形选区近似比O(log n)→O(1)（平面图/minor-free图）
2. 扩展至minor-free图和有界扩张图
3. 半径-k选区O(1)近似（平面图/apex-minor-free图）
4. 松弛参数后(1+ε)近似
5. 最小权重阈值变体的硬度结果

Conclusion: 通过精化分析实现了显著的理论突破，将对数近似比降至常数。成果不仅提升算法性能，更大幅扩展了可处理的图类范围，对分布式计算和选区划分等实际应用具有重要意义。最小权重阈值变体的硬度结果为后续研究指明方向。

Abstract: Packing disjoint subgraphs in a given graph is a fundamental problem with many applications. Motivated by political districting, we focus on connected subgraphs that are compact (e.g., having constant radius from a single center vertex) and that satisfy additional composition requirements, such as a minimum population/weight threshold or balanced weight types (e.g., political affiliations). We aim to maximize coverage by disjoint districts that meet these requirements.
  In this work, we present new results that substantially improve the previously known bounds on balanced star districts for planar and minor-free graphs (Dharangutte et al. 2025). In particular, we improve the approximation factor from $O(\log n)$ to $O(1)$ for packing balanced star districts using the exact same algorithm, but with a refined analysis. We also extend the results beyond planar graphs to minor-free graphs and an even broader family of graphs of bounded expansion. Additionally, we obtain an $O(1)$ approximation for packing radius-$k$ districts (with a constant $k$) in planar and apex-minor-free graphs. In order to get a $(1+\varepsilon)$ approximation on the max coverage, we show that this can be achieved if we allow a slight relaxation of the balancedness parameters (by a factor that can be made arbitrarily close to $1$), for bounded radius-$k$ districts on planar and apex-minor-free graphs.
  We show that all of these results can also be obtained if we enforce a minimum weight threshold for each district as the composition requirement, rather than balancedness. We present various results on hardness and hardness of approximation for this variant, by graph and district types.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [EdgeFlow: Fast Cold Starts for LLMs on Mobile Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09083)
*Yongsheng Yan,Jiacheng Shen,Xuchuan Luo,Yangfan Zhou*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: EdgeFlow通过自适应精度调整解决移动端大模型冷启动高延迟问题，将冷启动延迟降低最多4.07倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动端部署LLM面临高冷启动延迟问题。现有框架（llama.cpp、MNN、llm.npu）在模型未加载到设备内存时启动推理会产生不可避免的高延迟。核心瓶颈是闪存带宽被不重要模型参数浪费。

Method: 1) NPU感知的自适应量化算法：根据参数重要性和NPU约束为权重分配不同精度；2) SIMD友好的打包格式：加速将变精度权重转换为固定大小的NPU原生数据类型；3) 协同粒度流水线：细粒度动态协调CPU和NPU计算。

Result: 在模型精度可比条件下，EdgeFlow相比llama.cpp、MNN和llm.npu三个SOTA移动LLM推理框架，冷启动延迟降低最多4.07倍。

Conclusion: 通过自适应精度调整策略，EdgeFlow能有效利用移动端NPU资源，显著优化移动LLM部署的冷启动性能。

Abstract: Deploying large language models (LLMs) on mobile devices is an emerging trend to enable data privacy and offline accessibility of LLM applications. Modern mobile neural processing units (NPUs) make such deployment increasingly feasible. However, existing mobile LLM inference frameworks suffer from high start-up latency due to their inevitable cold starts, i.e., launching LLM inferences when the model is not hosted in device memory. In this paper, we identify the key bottleneck of mobile LLM cold starts as the waste of flash bandwidth on unimportant model parameters. We design EdgeFlow, a mobile LLM inference framework that mitigates the cold start issue by adaptively adjusting the precisions of LLM parameters. Specifically, EdgeFlow leverages 1) an NPU-aware adaptive quantization algorithm that assigns different precisions to weights in a finer granularity according to their importance and NPU constraints, 2) an SIMD-friendly packing format that accelerates the transformation of various-precision weights into fixed-sized NPU-native data types, and 3) a synergistic granular pipeline that coordinates CPU and NPU computation in a fine-grained and dynamic manner. Experimental results show that EdgeFlow reduces cold-start latency by up to 4.07x compared with three state-of-the-art mobile LLM inference frameworks, i.e., llama.cpp, MNN, and llm.npu, under comparable model accuracy.

</details>
